# PORTAL-ENDPOINT-MAP — staff portal demand × backend supply

_Generated: 2026-07-24 · updated: 2026-07-25 (portal canonicity resolved; §15 re-anchored) · wire contract: **v1.0.0** (`src/contracts/wire.ts:35`) · backend: **`07025b3`**, re-anchored 2026-07-25 to **`578d13d`** (notifications Phase 1 fully committed; working tree carries a separate uncommitted notifications Phase-2 deep-link WIP) · portal demand source: `fetchd_staff_portal_desktop` @ **`6be4c66`** (branch `scaffold/phase-1`, dirty working tree) · mobile: `6d96614` (`check:contracts` green)_

**What this is.** The verified, per-operation map from every staff-portal `ApiClient` operation (demand) to the backend's real endpoint surface (supply), plus every backend route the portal does *not* call. Built by a 45-agent read-only audit (15 domains × audit → adversarial verify → write); every claim was re-checked against actual route/schema/wire code by a separate verifier before landing here. This document proposes; it does not change anything — no contract, schema, or code was edited.

**How to read an entry.** Each portal operation gets one block: portal signature + governing mock logic (demand), bucket, backend anchor + wire shape + DATA-CONTRACT ref + backing tables (supply), target request/response in snake_case wire terms (never the mock's shapes), auth/side-effects/error codes, and the action. Buckets:

- **A — reuse**: an existing endpoint serves the op as-is.
- **B — modify additively**: an existing endpoint nearly serves it; the exact additive change + mobile-safety note is stated.
- **C — build new**: nothing covers it; a full proposed staff-scoped signature + a one-line why-can't-we-reuse justification is given. Some C entries deliberately resolve to *"no server endpoint"* (client-side per a locked ruling).
- **OPEN**: blocked on a product/architecture decision recorded in Open Questions — do not build until adjudicated.
- **D** (flagged inside entries): a schema change proposal, listed in roll-up 3.

Confidence is **VERIFIED** (re-confirmed in code by the adversarial pass this session) or **INFERRED** (stated why). Mock quirks — anchor date-shifting, client-minted ids, flat-string encodings, display-cased enums — are treated as demand signals, never as the target contract.

---

## Portal canonicity — RESOLVED 2026-07-25: one portal

There were two portal checkouts when this audit ran; there is now one. **`fetchd_staff_portal_desktop` (`scaffold/phase-1` @ `6be4c66`) is the sole, canonical portal.** The wire-synced `~/Desktop/fetchd-staff-portal` (`main` @ `c707c57`, contracts synced at `2a3d9c0` — the repo STATUS.md's alignment table described) was **deleted 2026-07-24** and its history survives on no `fetchd-nwa` remote; every `2a3d9c0` reference is now a dangling anchor. Two consequences:

- The canonical portal has **no `sync:contracts` and no generated `contracts.ts`** — contract-wiring it is a **to-build item, not an ambiguity** (see [roll-up 5](#rollup-5), item 1).
- ⚠️ The desktop portal repo's remote `main` (`39b51a7`) is an unrelated empty "Initial commit" branch — **`scaffold/phase-1` is the branch of record**; do not mistake `origin/main` for the deleted wire-synced portal.

Staff-identity vocabulary (backend `staff_role` vs portal `permissions`, DRIFT-43/56) remains a live decision — no longer pinned on which portal.

## Auth / Session / Platform (me, agreements, media, uploads, settings, health, workers) <a id="auth"></a>
_Demand: no auth namespace on ApiClient @ client.ts:327-346, placeholder ME_STAFF_ID='staff-shanthi' @ features/staff/lib/me.ts:9, MessagesApi.send @ client.ts:273 (SendMessageInput.attachments @ client.ts:147-154), StaffMember @ types.ts:212-220, ApiError @ types.ts:53-66, config.ts:11-15, src/app/App.tsx:7-14 · Supply: me.ts, authWebhook.ts, agreements.ts, media.ts, uploadsSign.ts, health.ts, workersTick.ts · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: auth-accounts.md, api-conventions.md, reports-media.md (media half)_

This is a supply-heavy domain: the portal's ApiClient (client.ts:327-346) has NO auth, NO me, and NO media namespace, so almost all demand here is shell-level (config/App/placeholder ME_STAFF_ID) or forward-looking — hence portal_op is null on every entry except one. The single real ApiClient method touching this domain is `messages.send`, whose attachment support is a bucket-C gap on the staff side (the staff thread body is `{text}` strict server-side); its proposed fix mirrors the existing owner attachment pattern (threads.ts:46-58). Media supply is complete and portal-aware — POST /media/upload is the purpose-built [staff] browser proxy, and GET/DELETE /media/:id are staff cross-owner. The two genuine supply gaps are (1) staff message attachments (unbuilt server-side) and (2) the uncontracted error envelope, a promotion candidate whose backend code vocabulary is fully disjoint from the portal's ApiError.

### (no portal op) — establish a signed-in staff session + attach an auth credential
- **Portal:** `no auth namespace on ApiClient @ client.ts:327-346; placeholder ME_STAFF_ID='staff-shanthi' @ features/staff/lib/me.ts:9`
- **Behavior:** Establish a signed-in staff session and attach an auth credential to every ApiClient call. Today the portal has NO auth of any kind: config.ts:11-15 carries only {env, apiMode, apiBaseUrl} (no token/session field), src/app/App.tsx:7-14 renders straight into the router with no auth guard/login screen, staff identity is a hardcoded placeholder, and getApiClient() throws in http mode (client.ts:348-354).
- **Bucket:** OPEN
- **Backend:** contract: auth-accounts.md R2/R6/R11/R12/R26; auth-accounts.md:117-122 (portal auth/session rows all MISSING); DISCREPANCIES.md:1048 (invites open question) · tables: owners @ schema.sql:190 (supabase_uid UNIQUE :192); staff @ schema.sql:233 (supabase_uid UNIQUE :235)
- **Auth:** staff · **Errors:** 401 unauthenticated (bad/absent JWT), 403 not_provisioned (verified token, no mirror row) — plugin.ts:116-121
- **Action:** No NEW backend login route is required: Supabase Auth owns credentials and the backend already accepts staff sessions — `authenticate` JWKS-verifies the Bearer JWT and resolves it to a live staff mirror row (src/auth/plugin.ts:103-131), and `requireStaff` (plugin.ts:139-148) gates every staff* route. The portal must add a Supabase JS client + staff login UI and send `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` on every call (dev-only alt: `X-Dev-Principal: staff:<uuid>:<role>`, plugin.ts:53-79, double-gated off in prod — env.ts resolveBypassHeaderEnabled hard-forces false when NODE_ENV=production, per R11). The genuinely-missing server-side piece is staff-account PROVISIONING tooling: mirror rows are created ONLY by the signed POST /auth/webhook (R6, provisioning.ts:82-94 branches on app_role) and no repo contains code that creates Supabase staff users carrying the required `app_role` invite metadata (auth-accounts.md:160 'Who sends the invites?' — unverifiable from code).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - No repo tooling provisions Supabase staff users with app_role metadata — the invite/first-login path at go-live is unverifiable from code (auth-accounts.md:160; DISCREPANCIES.md:1048).
  - Staff-identity vocabulary is a live decision on the sole portal: backend `staff_role` enum vs the desktop portal's `permissions` 'owner'|'manager'|'trainer' model (DRIFT-43; DISCREPANCIES.md:1049) — no longer contingent on portal canonicity (resolved 2026-07-25).

### (no portal op) — render "who am I" for the signed-in staffer
- **Portal:** `ME_STAFF_ID: StaffId @ features/staff/lib/me.ts:9; StaffMember @ types.ts:212-220` · mock logic: mock CURRENT_STAFF='staff-shanthi' @ src/api/mock/index.ts:60
- **Behavior:** Render 'who am I' for the signed-in staffer (name/role/campus/avatar) and pin every my-scoped view (MyScheduleCard, report authorship byStaffId — mock pins reports to CURRENT_STAFF at mock/index.ts:200,788,802). Portal encodes this as a hardcoded placeholder ME_STAFF_ID='staff-shanthi' (features/staff/lib/me.ts:9) whose own comment says it stands in 'until a client.me() (or auth session) exists'.
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** GET /me @ src/routes/me.ts:162 (staff arm 175-179) · contract: DATA-CONTRACT.md:2047 (GET /me); DISCREPANCIES.md NOTE-35 @ :847-852; DRIFT-43 @ :499-504 · tables: staff @ schema.sql:233-244
- **Response:** `{ id, name, role: 'owner-shanthi'|'trainer'|'office', location: string|null, image_path: string, active: boolean }`
- **Auth:** staff · **Errors:** 401 unauthenticated, 403 not_provisioned (staff mirror row gone — me.ts:176-178)
- **Action:** Reuse GET /me — its staff arm already returns the staff mirror row via staffProfile (me.ts:69-78, 175-179). Two caveats: (1) the staff /me wire is EXPLICITLY UNRATIFIED — 'a Day-2 design choice, not a frozen contract... Ratify when the portal lands' (me.ts:64-68; DISCREPANCIES NOTE-35 @ :847) — freeze it before the portal consumes it. (2) The portal's StaffMember shape {id,name,role,campus,isTrainer,availability,permissions} (types.ts:212-220) diverges: it uses `campus` (backend emits `location` display label) and a `permissions:'owner'|'manager'|'trainer'` authz matrix the backend has never implemented (R12: no per-StaffRole gating anywhere) vs the DB/wire StaffRole 'owner-shanthi'|'trainer'|'office' (wire.ts:74). This is adjudicated as DRIFT-43 (three conflicting staff-role vocabularies, DISCREPANCIES.md:499-504): ALIGN the portal to the staff_role vocabulary; do NOT mint the parallel permissions model. (isTrainer/availability are not on /me; availability belongs to the shifts surface, permissions is adjudicated drift.)
- **Promote:** staff /me profile shape is NOT in wire.ts (both owner and staff /me are uncontracted); promote a frozen StaffMeWire into wire.ts when the portal lands, aligned to StaffRole (wire.ts:74)
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Ratify + byte-freeze the staff /me wire before the portal reads it (NOTE-35).
  - Reconcile portal `campus` vs backend `location` display label, and the portal `permissions` matrix vs staff_role — adjudicated as DRIFT-43; which model migrates remains a live DRIFT-43/56 decision (portal canonicity resolved 2026-07-25 — the desktop portal is the only portal; DISCREPANCIES.md:1049).

### (no portal op) — attach report photos/videos from the browser
- **Portal:** `no media/upload method on ApiClient (client.ts:327-346); Report.photoCount @ types.ts:420`
- **Behavior:** Staff attach photos/videos to a report card from the browser during authoring/editing. The portal today shows only gradient placeholders — Report.photoCount (types.ts:420, 'gradient placeholders until real photos') — and has no upload method anywhere on the ApiClient (ReportsApi.saveDraft/send carry no media, client.ts:246-254).
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** POST /media/upload @ src/routes/media.ts:242 [staff] · wire: MediaWire @ wire.ts:335-346 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §C.2 amendment :1619-1632, :2170; reports-media.md R15/R17/R20/R25 · tables: media_assets @ schema.sql:576-600; media_derivative_jobs @ schema.sql:614-623
- **Request:** `raw file bytes in body (Content-Type = file MIME); query { purpose: 'report-photo'|'report-video', report_id: uuid } (strict, media.ts:101-106); header Idempotency-Key required (media.ts:248)` **Response:** `MediaWire { id, purpose, kind, url, expires_at, blurhash, width, height, duration_ms, derivatives: { label -> url } }`
- **Auth:** staff (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** R2 PUT pre-tx (orphan-on-retry: request hash covers stable metadata, not the per-call random key — media.ts:259-273, R25); INSERT media_assets + enqueue media_derivative_jobs in one withMutation tx (createMediaRow, media.ts:488-495); derivatives worker generates WebP thumb/feed/lightbox + blurhash on a later /workers/tick · **Errors:** 400 bad_request (content-type/purpose mismatch, empty body — media.ts:252-257), 403 forbidden (owner principal), 404 not_found (unknown report or its dog no longer live — media.ts:469-478), 401 unauthenticated
- **Action:** Reuse POST /media/upload — the Day-19c server-proxy path purpose-built for exactly the CORS-bound portal (DATA-CONTRACT §C.2 amendment :1619-1632; reports-media R15). The browser POSTs raw bytes; the server streams to R2 (no browser->R2 CORS, no presign exposure), resolves report_id -> report.dog_id -> dogs.owner_id and stamps THAT owner so the dog's owner reads it back via the owner GET (media.ts:465-480). 25 MB cap (MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES bodyLimit, media.ts:238,244); content-type must match purpose (assertContentTypeMatchesPurpose, media.ts:252, R20); staff-only (requireStaff, media.ts:247).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Report photos link by media_assets.report_id FK ONLY (R26) — there is NO owner-side 'media-by-report' read endpoint yet, so the owner app cannot display portal-uploaded report photos until that lands (owner app renders bundled demo photos meanwhile — DRIFT-36 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:457).

### (no portal op) — render an uploaded media asset via a signed URL
- **Portal:** `no media namespace on ApiClient (client.ts:327-346)`
- **Behavior:** Render an uploaded report photo (or message attachment) in the portal via a short-lived signed URL. No ApiClient media-read method exists today.
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** GET /media/:id @ src/routes/media.ts:146 · wire: MediaWire @ wire.ts:335-346 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §C.2 :2171; reports-media.md R4/R22, auth table @ reports-media.md:140 · tables: media_assets @ schema.sql:576-600
- **Response:** `MediaWire { id, purpose, kind, url, expires_at, blurhash, width, height, duration_ms, derivatives }`
- **Auth:** staff · **Errors:** 404 not_found (missing; owner cross-tenant also 404 — staff is cross-owner trusted), 400 bad_request (non-uuid id, media.ts:148-151), 401 unauthenticated
- **Action:** Reuse GET /media/:id — a [staff] principal reads ANY live media row cross-owner (owner-scope check applies only to principal.kind==='owner', media.ts:158-164; DATA-CONTRACT §C.2 :1614-1618 / :2171). Returns MediaWire with base + all derivative URLs presigned at 5-min TTL (GET_URL_TTL_SECONDS, media.ts:70, 537-560); the browser refreshes on expiry (R22).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED

### (no portal op) — remove a report photo/video

Idempotency-Key required (media.ts:175).
- **Confidence:** **INFERRED** — Supply is VERIFIED in code; the portal demand is inferred — no photo-removal verb exists on the ApiClient yet, so this maps a future editor need, not a current call.

### `messages.send` — attach media to an outbound staff message
- **Portal:** `MessagesApi.send(input: SendMessageInput): Promise<MessageThread> @ client.ts:273; SendMessageInput.attachments @ client.ts:147-154; MessageAttachment @ types.ts:551-554` · mock logic: mock messages.send appends attachments verbatim @ src/api/mock/index.ts:858-897 (attachments passthrough @ :891)
- **Behavior:** Staff attach media to an outbound message (SendMessageInput.attachments: MessageAttachment[] @ client.ts:147-154; the mock composer offers kinds photo|video|pdf). The portal's MessageAttachment is a flat {kind, name} placeholder with no URL/id (types.ts:551-554).
- **Bucket:** C · schema change flagged (see Schema change below)
- **Backend:** wire: MessageAttachmentWire @ wire.ts:275-283 · contract: reports-media.md:151 (message-attachment rides the media pipeline); DISCREPANCIES NOTE-29 @ :811-816 (staff replies text-only; mock pdf unrepresentable) · tables: media_assets @ schema.sql:576-600; messages @ schema.sql:1372 / message_attachments @ schema.sql:1400 (messaging domain)
- **Request:** `PROPOSED { text?: string, attachment_media_ids?: string[] } (text OR >=1 attachment, mirroring threads.ts:46-58)` **Response:** `MessageWire { ..., attachments?: MessageAttachmentWire[] } (wire.ts:285-295)`
- **Auth:** staff · **Side effects:** media pre-uploaded then linked; batch-validate ids via mediaAssetsRepository.findManyByIds (mediaAssetsRepository.ts:181; cited as the messaging-overlap seam at reports-media.md:151) · **Errors:** 400 bad_request (empty message, unknown media id), 403 forbidden (attachment role gate), 401 unauthenticated
- **Action:** Full new signature (cross-refs the messaging domain — POST /staff/threads/:id/messages is owned by the staffThreads auditor). PROPOSED: extend `POST /staff/threads/:id/messages` body from strict {text} (staffThreads.ts:33-34) to { text?, attachment_media_ids?: string[] } where each id was pre-created via POST /media/upload; the route re-signs them into MessageAttachmentWire on read — this exactly mirrors the OWNER send pattern that already exists (threads.ts:46-58 accepts { text?, attachment_media_ids? } with text-OR-attachment refinement). Why can't we reuse what exists (proven by broad grep of all src/routes/ files): (1) the staff reply body is `{ text }` .strict() today — staff messages are TEXT-ONLY server-side (DISCREPANCIES NOTE-29 @ :811-816), and no other route file carries attachment handling; (2) the `message-attachment` media purpose is OWNER-ONLY (staff -> 403, R17, media.ts:134-139), so a staff attachment path additionally requires extending that role gate to staff (or a new staff-message-attachment purpose); (3) the portal's 'pdf' attachment kind is UNREPRESENTABLE — MediaKind is image|video only (wire.ts:88), so pdf must be dropped or the media_kind enum widened (schema change, relitigates a locked enum — routed to open_questions).
- **Schema change:** ONLY if 'pdf' attachments are required: widen media_kind enum (image|video) — flagged as breaking; prefer dropping pdf or a doc-purpose. Routed to open_questions, do not relitigate the locked enum here.
- **Promote:** ALIGN portal MessageAttachment {kind:'photo'|'video'|'pdf', name} (types.ts:551-554) to MessageAttachmentWire { media_id, kind: image|video, url, width, height, blurhash, duration_ms }; do not mint the flat {kind,name} shape
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Does staff messaging need attachments in v1, or stay text-only? If yes, the owner-only message-attachment role gate (R17) must extend to staff.
  - 'pdf' attachment kind has no media representation (MediaKind image|video, wire.ts:88) — drop it or widen the enum?

### (no portal op) — cross-cutting error envelope contract
- **Portal:** `class ApiError { code: 'not-found'|'invalid-transition'|'capacity-full'|'validation'|'network'|'not-implemented' } @ src/api/types.ts:53-66`
- **Behavior:** Cross-cutting error contract. Every backend error is emitted as { error: { code, message, details? } } by the single error handler (auth/plugin.ts:191-207; codes + statuses in lib/errors.ts:43-96, plus 'internal' fallback @ plugin.ts:206). This envelope is UNCONTRACTED — wire.ts contains no error shape at all. The portal's own ApiError uses a DIFFERENT, wholly disjoint code vocabulary.
- **Bucket:** OPEN
- **Backend:** contract: api-conventions.md (error envelope); DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-43 @ :499-504 (parallel vocabularies pattern)
- **Response:** `{ error: { code: string, message: string, details?: unknown } }`
- **Errors:** backend status map @ errors.ts:67-96: unauthenticated(401), not_provisioned(403), forbidden(403), bad_request(400), not_found(404), invalid_payload(422), conflict(409), agreement_unsigned + gate family(422), internal(500)
- **Action:** Promotion candidate. Freeze the error envelope { error: { code, message, details? } } + the backend ApiErrorCode enum (23 snake_case codes @ errors.ts:43-65: unauthenticated, not_provisioned, forbidden, not_found, bad_request, invalid_payload, conflict, idempotency_*, ambiguous_principal, the 422 gate family incl. agreement_unsigned, plus handler-level 'internal') into wire.ts, and ALIGN the portal ApiError code union (kebab 'not-found'|'invalid-transition'|'capacity-full'|'validation'|'network'|'not-implemented', types.ts:53-66) to it. The portal HTTP client is unbuilt (getApiClient throws in http mode, client.ts:348-354), so alignment is free now. Why now: the two vocabularies share zero members; a portal that starts consuming the API will mis-map every error until this is frozen.
- **Promote:** freeze the error envelope + ApiErrorCode enum into wire.ts; align portal ApiError.code (types.ts:53-66) to the backend snake_case codes
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Which error-code vocabulary is canonical, and does the details schema need per-code typing (e.g. agreement_unsigned.details.missing: [{document_key,label}], invalid_payload.details.kind — concrete shapes live in lib/bookingErrors.ts, passed through opaquely per errors.ts:98-107)?

## Dogs & Owners (vets, vaccines, welcome, alumni, dog-side evaluation gate) <a id="dogs"></a>
_Demand: DogsApi.list/get @ client.ts:184-185, OwnersApi.list/get @ client.ts:189-190 (client.ts:183-191) · Supply: dogs.ts, staffDogs.ts, staffAlumni.ts, vets.ts, welcomeDogs.ts, requiredVaccines.ts · Logic: evalGate.ts (dog-side gate portions) · Business logic: dogs-evaluations.md_

The Dogs & Owners domain surfaces exactly 4 portal demand ops — DogsApi.list/get and OwnersApi.list/get (client.ts:183-191), all read-only, the only dog/owner namespaces in client.ts. Only `dogs.list` is bucket B (extend the existing GET /staff/dogs, runtime mobile-safe with a type-level sync ripple); `dogs.get`, `owners.list`, and `owners.get` are all bucket C — GET /dogs/:id 404s staff by design, no cross-owner owner directory route exists (proven by full route-table enumeration of all 40 route files), and GET /me is self-only. The remaining 21 routes across the six supply files are all SUPPLY-ONLY: mobile owner dog/vaccine/med/feeding CRUD, other portal namespaces (session-count→Reports d07 with adjudicated BUG-1 undercount, alumni verbs→future staff editor), public welcome-dogs, and the never-seeded required-vaccines catalog. Corrections this pass were citation-precision fixes plus two substantive refinements (the session-count BUG-1 caveat and the StaffDogWire mobile sync-ripple nuance); no bucket flipped and every entry re-earned VERIFIED.

### `dogs.list` — cross-owner dog directory
- **Portal:** `list(): Promise<Dog[]> @ client.ts:184` · mock logic: mock dogs namespace index.ts:229-238; directory eval-exemption evalGate.ts:4-15; Dog shape types.ts:79-105; pills dogDisplay.ts:43-62
- **Behavior:** Cross-owner directory of ALL dogs (mock returns db.dogs unfiltered, index.ts:230-233). No owner scoping, no eval-gate — the Dogs directory is the sole exemption from the operational eval filter (evalGate.ts:4-15). Feeds the portal's rich Dog rows (pills: vax/active/alumni via dogDisplay.ts:43-62).
- **Bucket:** B (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** GET /staff/dogs @ routes/staffDogs.ts:45 · wire: StaffDogWire @ wire.ts:352 · contract: GET /staff/dogs directory @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1580; §B Dog wire @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1786 (§J.3 is_alumni Δ @ 1808) · tables: dogs @ schema.sql:251; dog_completed_programs @ schema.sql:435 (is_alumni derive)
- **Response:** `EXTENDED StaffDogWire[]: { id, name, breed, owner_id, owner_name, profile_image_path?, evaluation_status, is_alumni, boarding_enabled, spayed_neutered?, age_months, active, vaccines: [{ id, name, expires_at, requirement_key? }], vet? }`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read) · **Errors:** 403 forbidden for owner principal (requireStaff, staffDogs.ts:47; principalNarrows.ts:60); 401 unauth
- **Action:** REUSE + EXTEND GET /staff/dogs (staffDogs.ts:45, StaffDogWire wire.ts:352-359). It already serves the cross-owner staff dog LIST demand (requireStaff staffDogs.ts:47; live owner-owned dogs ordered by name, staff-owned school cats excluded via inner join on live owners — dogsRepository.ts:463-477) but emits only a 6-field projection (toStaffDogWire staffDogs.ts:31-40) — partial coverage, hence B not A. ADDITIVE-ONLY change. MOBILE-SAFE at runtime: StaffDogWire is synced into mobile's generated contracts.ts:342 as a TYPE, but no mobile code fetches /staff/dogs (grep: zero non-generated references), so extending it cannot break mobile behavior — it does require a mobile `npm run sync:contracts` re-run to keep check:contracts green (wire.ts minor bump). Add schema-backed fields already emitted on the owner DogWire (dogWire.ts:54-81): evaluation_status, is_alumni, boarding_enabled, spayed_neutered?, age_months, active (derive from live()), vaccines[] (portal derives its Current/Due-soon/Expired pill), vet?. Fields with NO backing column (gender, weight_lbs, vip, home_campus, primary 'category', single 'meals' string, member_since-as-label, packageProgress) -> open_questions/schema_change; VIP + flagIds are FlagsApi (domain 12) cross-ref, not new dog columns.
- **Schema change:** Only if the portal truly needs them: nullable dogs.gender / dogs.weight_grams (D, additive). VIP is a FlagsApi custom-flag (domain 12), NOT a new column. home_campus/member_since derivable from owner.location / dogs.created_at (schema.sql:306) — no DDL.
- **Promote:** Align portal Dog (types.ts:79-105) to an EXTENDED StaffDogWire; retire the mock camelCase parallel: evalStatus->evaluation_status, vaccination.status pill DERIVED client-side from vaccines[] expiry, fixed->spayed_neutered, alumni->is_alumni. Never mint a portal-only dog shape.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Portal Dog list-row vs detail field-set is INFERRED from the Dog type declaration (types.ts:79-105) — exact fields the portal list renders unverified.
  - gender / weightLbs / vip / homeCampus / primary category / single 'meals' string / packageProgress have no backend column (dogs DDL schema.sql:251-314 confirmed column-free of all of these) — real column vs derived vs dropped is unresolved (mirrors dogs-evaluations.md Q9, D7).

Auth requireStaff. Errors: 404 dog not found/expired, 403 owner principal (mirror GET /staff/dogs). Idempotent GET, no gates fired. WHY NOT REUSE: GET /dogs/:id (dogs.ts:279) is OWNER-scoped and 404s every staff principal by design (dogs.ts:282-286, R7 non-enumeration); GET /staff/dogs (staffDogs.ts:45) is list-only; the staff alumni/session-count verbs are narrow single-purpose reads. ### Open questions
- DogsApi.list -> GET /staff/dogs is bucket B, but the portal Dog (types.ts:79-105) carries fields with NO backend column (re-confirmed against dogs DDL schema.sql:251-314): gender, weightLbs, vip, homeCampus, primary 'category' enrollment, single 'meals'/'fixed' display strings, packageProgress. Resolve each as real column (schema D) vs derived (owner.location->campus, dogs.created_at->member_since, spayed_neutered->fixed, vaccines[]->vaccination.status pill) vs dropped/cross-ref (vip+flagIds are FlagsApi domain 12). Mirrors dogs-evaluations.md Q9 + D7 (portal is an independent mock universe; src/api/http/ confirmed empty).
- OwnersApi (list+get) are both bucket C — NO owner directory route exists in the backend (re-verified via full route-table enumeration of all 40 route files); GET /me is self-only (me.ts:162-180). The new GET /staff/owners[/:id] needs a decision on Owner.balanceCents (no owner column — cross-domain derived from invoices/credit ledger) and contactPref/channels (no single-enum source — owners stores push/email booleans + *_notification_categories JSONB, schema.sql:210-213).
- There is NO OwnerWire in wire.ts (grep confirmed; owner /me is hand-rolled inline, me.ts:35-62 — adjudicated in dogs-evaluations.md:198 wire-coverage). The proposed staff OwnerWire and the extended StaffDogWire both need promotion into wire.ts to kill the mobile<->portal hand-mirror duplication.
- DogsApi/OwnersApi are READ-ONLY (list/get, client.ts:183-191) — the portal models no dog/owner WRITE op, yet the eval-verdict-writer gap (owner-writable evaluation_status, D2/BUG-11; missing staff 'passed/failed' verb, Q1) sits adjacent. Confirm the portal never needs a staff dog-edit/eval-verdict verb here (cross-ref domain 07 report verdicts) before treating dogs.list/get as the whole staff dog surface.
- Adjacent adjudicated gap in this domain's route files' orbit: POST /dogs/:id/evaluation is documented in DATA-CONTRACT §C (DATA-CONTRACT.md:2051) and called by mobile, but NO backend route registers it (BUG-10 / dogs-evaluations.md D1 — re-confirmed absent from the full route table this session). Not a portal demand op; do not treat as a new finding.
- Exact portal Dog list-row vs detail-screen field split is INFERRED from the type declaration, not from reading the portal DogsScreen components — validate which rich fields the list actually renders before sizing the StaffDogWire extension.

## Bookings (day-program), Check-in board, Capacity, Dashboard snapshot <a id="bookings"></a>
_Demand: BookingsApi (client.ts:203-211), CheckInApi (client.ts:218-222), DashboardApi (client.ts:317-319), BookingListParams @ client.ts:75-81 · Supply: bookings.ts, staffBookings.ts, availability.ts · Logic: capacity.ts, checkinFold.ts · Business logic: bookings-scheduling.md, portal-dashboard-analytics.md_

Portal demand here is three interfaces — BookingsApi's staff cross-owner list plus capacity outlook, the CheckInApi board/checkIn/checkOut/undo/cancelSession family, and DashboardApi's operational snapshot — against backend supply in bookings.ts, staffBookings.ts, and availability.ts. Only three ops map cleanly onto existing routes with additive changes (bucket B: bookings.list -> GET /staff/bookings, capacityOutlook -> GET /availability, cancelSession -> POST /staff/bookings/:id/cancel); five are net-new (bucket C: board, checkIn, checkOut, undo, dashboard), and eight backend routes are supply-only owner/staff surfaces the portal never calls. The headline mismatch is the check-in reconciliation (LOAD-BEARING OQ #7): the portal's event-sourced fold (expected->in|eval->out, stepwise undo, cancel-session, stay-derived lodger rows) has no backend substrate — the backend has a single terminal booking_dogs.attendance write with no in/out state, no event log, no eval-start, no undo, and no departure stamp — already adjudicated as parallel-universe drift D7 @ bookings-scheduling.md:233 / DRIFT-4 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:265. Capacity numbers disagree at the data layer (portal 24/12/15/8=59 vs backend default 3/3 per weekday per location) and no read surface exposes booked usage today; the one materially-corrected prior claim is that location columns DO exist across owners/staff/cohorts/bookings/pending_requests/day_capacity — the genuine per-campus gaps are threads (no location) and shifts (no table).

### `bookings.list` — staff cross-owner Bookings-tab list
- **Portal:** `list(params?: BookingListParams): Promise<Booking[]> @ src/api/client.ts:204 (BookingListParams @ client.ts:75-81)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:319-335
- **Behavior:** Staff Bookings-tab cross-owner list, filtered by date range / service type / campus / dog-name search, sorted date-asc then dogId (mock/index.ts:319-335). Mock has NO status filter — returns every status incl. canceled/waitlisted.
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** GET /staff/bookings @ routes/staffBookings.ts:68 · wire: BookingWire @ wire.ts:143 · contract: §C Verb-4 bookings @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1534-1535 · tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629, booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688
- **Request:** `additive optional query on GET /staff/bookings: ?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD&category=<service_category>&location=<location_key>&search=<str> (endpoint takes NO query today — no query parsing exists in the handler, staffBookings.ts:68-78)` **Response:** `BookingWire[] { id, dog_id, additional_dog_ids?, category, status, date, trainer?, duration_minutes?, notes?, session_report_id?, location?, cancel_deadline_at?, cancelled_at?, cancel_forfeited?, cohort_id?, group_class_name?, lesson_setting? } @ wire.ts:143-171`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read) · **Errors:** bad_request (invalid query, for the proposed filters)
- **Action:** Reuse GET /staff/bookings — cross-owner live queue, staff-scoped (requireStaff @ staffBookings.ts:73), scheduled-asc. Additive change: optional server-side filters. NUANCE (corrected): the Day-20 hardening comment (staffBookings.ts:66-67) flags ONLY a ?from=&to= window; category/location/search are new proposals beyond the flagged item. Mobile-safety: [staff]-only route; the mobile repo's only /staff/bookings reference is a comment in generated contracts.ts (mobile/src/api/contracts.ts:166) — no mobile consumer; params optional, no-params = full queue unchanged -> purely additive. SEMANTICS GAP: the queue EXCLUDES cancelled+expired (bookingsRepository.findLiveActive @ bookingsRepository.ts:117-121, called staffBookings.ts:74) while mock list() returns all statuses — showing cancelled rows is a separate widening.
  - **Promote:** Align portal Booking (camelCase, display-cased type/campus, status vocab incl. 'confirmed'/'waitlisted' with no wire equivalent — types.ts:139-151) to BookingWire (snake_case, kebab category, lowercase location, status upcoming|past|cancelled) @ wire.ts:143. Parallel-universe drift adjudicated as D7 @ bookings-scheduling.md:233 / DRIFT-4 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:265.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open question:_ Does the staff Bookings tab need cancelled/expired rows? GET /staff/bookings excludes them; the mock list() includes them (and a 'waitlisted' status the backend does not model).

### `bookings.capacityOutlook` — N-day forward day-program fullness
- **Portal:** `capacityOutlook(days?: number): Promise<CapacityDay[]> @ src/api/client.ts:210` · mock logic: buildCapacityOutlook @ mock/logic/capacity.ts:12-32 (DAY_PROGRAM_CAPACITY 24+12+15+8 @ capacity.ts:10); called mock/index.ts:365-368
- **Behavior:** N-day (default 14) forward day-program fullness outlook: per date { booked, capacity }. Mock counts CONFIRMED Day-School+Day-Care bookings across BOTH campuses vs a fixed 24+12+15+8=59 combined seat pool; weekends closed (capacity 0).
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** GET /availability @ routes/availability.ts:57 · wire: DayCapacityWire @ routes/availability.ts:47 (route-local; NOT in wire.ts — NOTE-1 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:643) · contract: DayCapacity @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1912-1927 (grep-verified); configured-not-remaining is D9 @ bookings-scheduling.md:237, NOT a DATA-CONTRACT section · tables: day_capacity @ schema.sql:815 (sparse overrides); bookings/booking_dogs for the booked count
- **Request:** `existing GET /availability?from=&to=&mode=&location= (location REQUIRED, per-location); portal's all-campus outlook needs 2 calls (fayetteville+bentonville) summed client-side` **Response:** `TARGET DayCapacityWire[] additively: { location, date:'YYYY-MM-DD', school_openings, daycare_openings, school_booked, daycare_booked } (school_booked/daycare_booked = proposed additive)`
- **Auth:** [public/auth] (any authenticated principal; availability.ts:61 explicitly no role scope) · **Side effects:** none (read) · **Errors:** bad_request (invalid query fields — availability.ts:77-83); invalid_payload (reversed/oversized range, cap AVAILABILITY_MAX_DATES=92 @ lib/availability.ts:27 — availability.ts:91-104)
- **Action:** Reuse GET /availability for the capacity half. Additive change: expose booked usage per (location,date,mode) via new school_booked/daycare_booked fields — the count dayCapacityRepository.assertCapacityWithinLock already computes INSIDE the booking tx (dayCapacityRepository.ts:130-187, count @ :158); the read deliberately omits it today. Cross-client demand exists (mobile treats openings as remaining — D9 @ bookings-scheduling.md:237 / NOTE-2 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:649). Mobile-safety: DayCapacityWire is route-local + hand-mirrored as RawCapacity in mobile (availabilityRepository.ts:20-24 per NOTE-1); unknown additive keys ignored -> safe. Note the read is range-cached (avail:{location}:{from}:{to}, 5-min TTL — R22 @ bookings-scheduling.md:94); booked counts ride the existing avail:* invalidation on create/cancel. ZERO-BACKEND ALT (A): derive booked client-side from GET /staff/bookings + capacity from /availability. WHY NOT pure reuse: /availability emits CONFIGURED openings only, never booked usage (verified availability.ts:60-73).
  - **Promote:** Promote CapacityDay {date,booked,capacity} (types.ts:170-174) to a snake_case wire; hoist DayCapacityWire into wire.ts (route-local today, bypasses contract sync/version-bump — NOTE-1 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:643-647).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open question:_ Numeric config discrepancy: portal seat pool 24/12/15/8 (capacity.ts:10) vs backend default 3/3 per weekday per location (lib/availability.ts:16, DEFAULT_WEEKDAY_OPENINGS=3) — adjudicated as D7 @ bookings-scheduling.md:233 / DRIFT-4 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:265 (NOT DRIFT-1) — which numbers are real?
  - _Open question:_ Who writes day_capacity overrides in prod? No route writes them (verified: only availability.ts reads); locks.ts:197-200 documents a Day-19 portal writer that does not exist — bookings-scheduling.md Open questions #3.

### `checkin.board` — per-dog check-in board for a date/campus
- **Portal:** `board(date: ISODate, campus?: Campus): Promise<CheckInBoard> @ src/api/client.ts:218` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:372-429; foldCheckInEvents @ mock/logic/checkinFold.ts:26-72; daySchoolLodgers @ mock/logic/stayOccupancy.ts
- **Behavior:** Per-dog check-in board for a date/campus: day-program bookings (DS/DC/Eval, eval-gated) PLUS lodger rows DERIVED from stays (LG-1), each carrying a FOLDED status (expected/in/eval/out) + time, ranked expected-first (CI-6). Read-model over the day's check-in event log (mock/index.ts:372-429).
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** contract: §D.1 staff analytics deferred/data-ready @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2303-2320 · tables: booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688 (attendance/checked_in_at/checked_in_by_staff_id @ 696-698), bookings @ schema.sql:629
- **Request:** `PROPOSED GET /staff/checkin-board?date=YYYY-MM-DD&location=<location_key>` **Response:** `PROPOSED { date, location, rows: [{ dog_id, booking_id?, category, attendance, checked_in_at?, is_lead }] } — a thin join of the day's bookings + booking_dogs.attendance. Full fold status (expected/in/eval/out) has no backend substrate.`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read) · **Errors:** bad_request (invalid date/location)
- **Action:** BUILD staff-scoped. Why can't we reuse: no check-in board / dashboard / stats route exists — re-proven this session by sweeping all 41 files in fetchd_backend/src/routes/. §D.1 defers staff stats/analytics surfaces to the admin dashboard while confirming attendance DATA is captured (DATA-CONTRACT.md:2303-2320, esp. 2317-2318). Nearest data is GET /staff/bookings @ staffBookings.ts:68 + booking_dogs.attendance (schema.sql:696-698). Proposed: GET /staff/checkin-board?date=&location= [staff] -> the day's non-cancelled day-program bookings joined to per-dog attendance; no gates fired (read). The portal's event-sourced fold (expected->in|eval->out, stepwise undo, cancel-session, overnight no-show — checkinFold.ts:26-107) has NO backend equivalent: single terminal attendance write, no in/out state, no event log, no eval-start, no lodger/stay entity (stays are bookings with dropoff_at/pickup_at — bookings-scheduling.md:138-140,196). Already adjudicated as parallel-universe drift: D7 @ bookings-scheduling.md:233 / DRIFT-4 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:265. A faithful board is a subsystem (schema_change), not one read.
  - **Schema change:** For a faithful event-sourced board: new append-only checkin_events table (dog_id, booking_id/stay_id, date, kind, at, by_staff_id, location); else reinterpret booking_dogs.attendance and add a departure stamp.
  - **Promote:** Portal CheckInBoard / CheckInBoardRow / CheckInEvent (types.ts:250-293) -> net-new snake_case wire shapes if adopted; align Campus->LocationKey.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open question:_ OQ #7 @ bookings-scheduling.md:243ff (Open questions item 7): which model wins the check-in reconciliation (event-sourced fold + stay-occupancy vs backend single attendance write)? Explicitly recorded as having no STATUS.md decision.
  - _Open question:_ Lodger/stay rows have no backend home — stays are bookings with dropoff_at/pickup_at, no nightly BOARD_CAP (bookings-scheduling.md:196; BOARD_CAP is portal-mock-only, types.ts + stayOccupancy.ts).

### `checkin.checkIn` — append check-in / eval-start event
- **Portal:** `checkIn(dogId: DogId, date: ISODate): Promise<CheckInEvent> @ src/api/client.ts:219` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:431-437; checkInKind @ mock/logic/checkinFold.ts:75-83
- **Behavior:** Append a 'check-in' (or 'eval-start' for an Evaluation session) event; expected -> in|eval, stamps arrival time. Today-only (assertToday @ mock/index.ts:180-184, CI-3). mock/index.ts:431-437.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** POST /staff/bookings/:id/attendance @ routes/staffBookings.ts:202 (nearest verb) · wire: AttendanceWire @ wire.ts:177 · contract: §C Verb-4 attendance @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1541-1546; attendance surface bullet @ 2179 · tables: booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688
- **Request:** `reuse body { dog_id, status: 'attended' } (attendanceBodySchema @ staffBookings.ts:41-46) after a (dog,date,location)->booking resolve; OR proposed wrapper POST /staff/checkin { dog_id, date, location }` **Response:** `AttendanceWire { booking_id, dog_id, attendance, checked_in_at } @ wire.ts:177-182 (or a proposed CheckinEventWire for the fold model)`
- **Auth:** [staff] +idempotent · **Side effects:** writes booking_dogs.attendance/checked_in_at/checked_in_by_staff_id; attendance='attended' FRESHENS the 3-month re-eval divert clock (R8 @ bookings-scheduling.md:60,128) and feeds the alumni monthly-attendance scan (workers/scheduler.ts:243-248 + lib/alumniAttendanceScan.ts). · **Errors:** not_found (booking missing/expired @ staffBookings.ts:222-225; dog not on roster @ 239-241), conflict (cancelled @ 226-231), invalid_payload (body)
- **Action:** BUILD/align. Nearest verb POST /staff/bookings/:id/attendance sets attendance + checked_in_at + checked_in_by_staff_id (write @ staffBookings.ts:233-238). Mismatch: (a) addressed by booking id + dog_id, NOT (dog,date) — the portal's checkInContext (dog,date)->booking resolver (mock/index.ts:164-177) has no endpoint; (b) attendance is a TERMINAL fact ('attended'|'no-show'|'excused' — AttendanceStatus @ wire.ts:79, DB 'pending' excluded), not a reversible presence state; (c) no 'eval-start'/'in' concept. RECOMMENDED reconciliation: align portal to attendance semantics + add a (dog,date,location)->booking resolver, then checkIn = attendance='attended' (B-flavored, additive). Full-fidelity fold (undo/out/eval) needs checkin_events (C + schema_change). Idempotency-Key required (R20 @ bookings-scheduling.md:90).
  - **Schema change:** Only if the event-sourced model wins: checkin_events append-only table (see board).
  - **Promote:** Portal CheckInEvent (types.ts:264-~275) -> AttendanceWire (align) or net-new CheckinEventWire.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open question:_ OQ #7 (bookings-scheduling.md Open questions) reconciliation direction.
  - _Open question:_ 'eval-start'/'in' presence state has no backend representation — attendance is arrival-only.

### `checkin.checkOut` — append check-out event, stamp departure
- **Portal:** `checkOut(dogId: DogId, date: ISODate): Promise<CheckInEvent> @ src/api/client.ts:220` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:439-445; checkOutKind @ mock/logic/checkinFold.ts:86-91
- **Behavior:** Append 'check-out'; in|eval -> out, stamps departure time. Today-only. mock/index.ts:439-445, checkOutKind @ checkinFold.ts:86-91.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** contract: §C Verb-4 attendance @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1541-1546 (arrival only) · tables: booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688 (checked_in_at @ 697 only; no checked_out_at anywhere in schema.sql)
- **Request:** `PROPOSED { dog_id, date, location } (or a check-out verb on the resolved booking)` **Response:** `PROPOSED departure stamp on the attendance row, or a 'check-out' CheckinEventWire`
- **Auth:** [staff] +idempotent · **Side effects:** none in the attendance model today; proposed departure stamp only. · **Errors:** conflict (not currently in), not_found
- **Action:** BUILD. Why can't we reuse: the backend has NO concept of checking a dog back OUT — booking_dogs carries checked_in_at but no checked_out_at (verified: zero checked_out_at hits in schema.sql; table @ 688-701), and attendance is a single terminal mark (arrival only). Proposed: additive booking_dogs.checked_out_at + a staff check-out verb, OR (if the event-sourced model wins) a 'check-out' row in checkin_events. schema_change required; deferred pending the check-in reconciliation (OQ #7).
  - **Schema change:** booking_dogs.checked_out_at timestamptz (additive) OR checkin_events table.

### `checkin.undo` — stepwise undo of the latest boundary
- **Portal:** `undo(dogId: DogId, date: ISODate): Promise<CheckInEvent> @ src/api/client.ts:221` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:447-453; undoKind @ mock/logic/checkinFold.ts:94-98
- **Behavior:** Stepwise undo of the latest boundary (out -> in via undo-check-out; in|eval -> expected via undo-check-in), restoring the prior stamp by re-folding surviving events. mock/index.ts:447-453, undoKind @ checkinFold.ts:94-98.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** contract: R18 no-undo @ bookings-scheduling.md:86; §C Verb-4 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1541-1546 · tables: booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688
- **Request:** `PROPOSED { dog_id, date, location } (undo verb)` **Response:** `PROPOSED re-folded status; not expressible on current AttendanceWire`
- **Auth:** [staff] +idempotent · **Side effects:** none in the backend model today. · **Errors:** conflict (nothing to undo — dog still expected)
- **Action:** BUILD. Why can't we reuse: no undo verb exists — R18 @ bookings-scheduling.md:86 is explicit: 'no undo verb and no transition guard — a later write overwrites' (bookingsRepository.setAttendance). A staff can overwrite attendance to another terminal value, but there is NO event history to step back through, and wire AttendanceStatus can't express 'expected'/un-checked-in ('attended'|'no-show'|'excused' only — wire.ts:79; the DB 'pending' default is unsettable by the verb — attendanceBodySchema @ staffBookings.ts:41-46). Faithful stepwise undo needs the append-only checkin_events log the backend lacks (schema_change), OR accept lossy 're-mark' semantics that still can't restore 'pending'/'expected'. Deferred (OQ #7).
  - **Schema change:** checkin_events table for reversible history; or add an 'expected'/un-mark path to the attendance verb + wire.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open question:_ OQ #7 reconciliation.
  - _Open question:_ Wire AttendanceStatus can't represent 'expected'/un-checked-in, so undo to the initial state is inexpressible today.

## Requests / Approvals + divert <a id="requests"></a>
_Demand: portal ops bookings.requests / approveRequest / declineRequest / undoDecision / approveAll @ src/api/client.ts:205-209 · Supply: requests.ts, staffRequests.ts · Logic: approvals.ts · Business logic: requests-approval.md_

The portal's five approvals-queue operations (client.ts:205-209) all target the three staff request routes in staffRequests.ts: three are direct REUSE (bucket A — list, approve, deny) and two (undoDecision, approveAll) are bucket C with NO backend equivalent, architecturally incompatible with the real one-way state machine (converted/cancelled are terminal, no undo transition exists). The headline mismatch is that the entire portal BookingRequest shape (types.ts:155-167) is a parallel universe vs PendingRequestWire — flat whenLabel string, submission.sections intake, CampusAbbr, and a display-string 'Evaluation' category the real request lane never produces — so aligning the portal is a rewrite of the approvals feature, not a field remap. The mock models approve as a one-click status flip, but the real approve verb requires per-category scheduling (scheduled_at/location/pickup_at) and performs booking creation, credit debits/invoicing, notifications and reminders; the gap is demand-side, not supply. Divert conversion is folded into the approve verb (staffRequests.ts:190-260), and divert_reasons already rides the wire (wire.ts:217) as a pure portal-render follow-up.

### `bookings.requests` — staff approval-queue read
- **Portal:** `requests(): Promise<BookingRequest[]> @ src/api/client.ts:205` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:337-340 (returns db.requests, all statuses); shape src/api/types.ts:155-167
- **Behavior:** Staff approval-queue read: list all live pending requests cross-owner so a staffer can triage approve/decline. Mock returns the whole flat db.requests list (all statuses, no owner scope); portal splits pending vs decided client-side (ApprovalsTab.tsx:34).
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** GET /staff/requests @ routes/staffRequests.ts:123 · wire: PendingRequestWire @ src/contracts/wire.ts:198 · contract: §C.1 Model 3 / Amendment 2026-05-24 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:621-625; divert extras Amendment 2026-07-14 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:872-890 (wire additions 887-890) · tables: pending_requests @ schema.sql:745; pending_request_dogs @ schema.sql:791; pending_request_preferred_dates @ schema.sql:799
- **Request:** `query: ?status=submitted|approved|approved-awaiting-payment|converted|cancelled (optional; omitted = all live rows). No body.` **Response:** `PendingRequestWire[] (snake_case): {id, dog_id, additional_dog_ids?, category, submitted_at, preferred_dates: string[], notes?{per_dog?,joint?}, focus{staff_preference?,descriptor_keys?}, length_weeks?, status, approved_at?, converted_booking_id?, location?, payment?, divert_reasons?, lesson_setting?}`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read). Newest-submitted-first sort via sortRequestsBySubmittedAt (staffRequests.ts:131).
- **Action:** REUSE. GET /staff/requests already serves the exact demand: cross-owner (requireStaff @ staffRequests.ts:128), returns PendingRequestWire[], optional ?status filter (statusQuerySchema @ staffRequests.ts:76), newest-first. No backend change. Portal-side work: ALIGN BookingRequest → PendingRequestWire and derive whenLabel/campus/section rows from wire fields at render time.
- **Promote:** Portal BookingRequest (types.ts:155-167) → PendingRequestWire. Kill the parallel portal-only shape: mock encodes whenLabel (flat pre-formatted string), submission.sections (freeform intake), campus/CampusAbbr, and display-string type ('Evaluation'|'Private lesson'|'Boarding'|'Board & Train'); wire is category slugs + preferred_dates ISO[] + structured notes/focus/length_weeks/lesson_setting + location + divert_reasons. Surfacing divert_reasons is the STATUS.md open follow-up (STATUS.md:125-126) — the field already rides the wire (wire.ts:217), so it's a portal render change only.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Mock queue seeds an 'Evaluation' request (fixtures.ts:427-449, req-milo-eval) but the real request lane excludes 'evaluation' (REQUEST_CATEGORIES @ requests.ts:103 = private-lesson|board-and-train|boarding; evaluations book directly) — the real /staff/requests will never contain an evaluation row, so the portal's queue taxonomy is broader than supply. Note the real lane DOES also carry diverted day-school/day-care rows (POST /bookings 202 divert), which the mock lacks entirely.
  - Mock returns ALL statuses; GET /staff/requests with ?status omitted also returns converted/cancelled rows — findLive filters only on expired_at IS NULL (requestsRepository.ts:127-133; same semantics as R34 @ requests-approval.md:93) — portal must pass ?status=submitted (or filter) to get a true pending queue.

### `bookings.approveRequest` — approve one pending request
- **Portal:** `approveRequest(id: RequestId): Promise<BookingRequest> @ src/api/client.ts:206` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:342-345 → logic/approvals.ts:9-20 decideRequest(request,'approved') (status flip only, no gates, no booking)
- **Behavior:** Approve one pending request. Mock is a bare one-click status flip pending→approved (no scheduling collected). Real verb: per-category booking creation (PL/boarding), park-for-payment (B&T), or verbatim day-program divert conversion.
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** POST /staff/requests/:id/approve @ routes/staffRequests.ts:137 · wire: PendingRequestWire @ src/contracts/wire.ts:198 · contract: §C.1 Amendment 2026-05-24 item 4 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:659-679; day-program divert conversion @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:872-881 · tables: pending_requests @ schema.sql:745; writes bookings (schema.sql:629)/booking_dogs (schema.sql:688) via insertBookingWithGateMapping / createDayProgramBookings
- **Request:** `{scheduled_at?: ISO, pickup_at?: ISO, location?: 'fayetteville'|'bentonville', notes?: string<=2000} + header Idempotency-Key. Validated against the LOCKED row's category (validateApproveBody @ staffRequests.ts:420-466): private-lesson requires scheduled_at + location; boarding requires scheduled_at + pickup_at (strictly after, staffRequests.ts:450-452) + location; board-and-train body MAY be empty; day-school/day-care take NO body fields (booked verbatim from the stored request, staffRequests.ts:190-260).` **Response:** `PendingRequestWire (200, UPDATED — status→'converted' for PL/boarding/day-program, or 'approved-awaiting-payment' for B&T; approved_at + converted_booking_id populated as applicable). NOT the BookingWire — the created booking is a side effect, FE follows converted_booking_id (DATA-CONTRACT.md:675-677).`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** PL/boarding: checkBookingGates → INSERT booking + cancel_deadline_at from cancel_window_settings (staffRequests.ts:272-273) → markConverted → enqueue 'booking-confirmed' notification + reminders (staffRequests.ts:261-327); boarding also stamps dropoff_at/pickup_at via setBoardingStayWindow (staffRequests.ts:292-297,509-523). Day-program divert: createDayProgramBookings (credit debits OR PAYG invoice, capacity, overlap guard, reminders) → markConverted (first booking as FK anchor) → notification (staffRequests.ts:190-260); invalidates avail:{location}:* + per-dog credit patterns (staffRequests.ts:161-164). B&T: markApprovedAwaitingPayment (staffRequests.ts:186-189) — NO booking; owner completes via POST /requests/:id/confirm-payment (requestConfirmPayment.ts:95-96, domain 09) which creates the booking + Stripe charge/invoice — but per DRIFT-7 (DISCREPANCIES.md:283-287) NO client calls confirm-payment and approve-B&T enqueues no notification, so the B&T leg dead-ends in product today. Booking gates fired: payment, vaccine, agreement, evaluation. · **Errors:** 404 not_found (missing/soft-expired row); 409 conflict (status != 'submitted', staffRequests.ts:174-179); 422 invalid_payload (missing per-category scheduled_at/location/pickup_at); 422 gate bounces (payment_required/vaccine_missing/agreement_unsigned/evaluation_required); day-program bounce: 422 insufficient_credits / insufficient_capacity / already_booked (createDayProgramBookings.ts:195,217 + capacity assert) and leaves status='submitted' (R19 @ requests-approval.md:69). NOT already_requested — that code fires only at submission time (requests.ts:308-314, bookings.ts), never at approve.
- **Action:** REUSE. POST /staff/requests/:id/approve is the real approve verb and fully serves the operation. The gap is DEMAND-side, not supply: the portal's one-click approveRequest(id) (useBookingRequests.ts:19-22, ApprovalsTab.tsx:73-80) sends NO body, but the endpoint requires per-category scheduling for PL/boarding. Portal must add a per-category approve form (scheduled_at + location, +pickup_at for boarding; empty for B&T; none for day-program). No backend change needed — the mock's model is simply lighter than reality (already adjudicated as DRIFT-10, DISCREPANCIES.md:301-305).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Portal must branch the approve body by category — but the mock's BookingRequest carries no location/preferred_dates in wire form and includes an 'Evaluation' type the backend can't approve at all. The approve UX is a redesign, not a field-add (DRIFT-10, adjudicated).
  - Stale docstring: staffRequests.ts:82 says PL 'location OPTIONAL' but validateApproveBody rejects missing location for every non-B&T category (staffRequests.ts:434-436) — code+DATA-CONTRACT (DATA-CONTRACT.md:667) agree, comment is wrong (already adjudicated as NOTE-6, DISCREPANCIES.md:673-677).
  - The portal approve toast 'Owner notified.' (ApprovalsTab.tsx:43) happens to be TRUE for approve (booking-confirmed notification enqueued at staffRequests.ts:253-260/320-327) — but false for B&T approve (parks at awaiting-payment, no notification; DRIFT-7).

### `bookings.declineRequest` — decline/deny a pending request
- **Portal:** `declineRequest(id: RequestId): Promise<BookingRequest> @ src/api/client.ts:207` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:347-350 → logic/approvals.ts:9-20 decideRequest(request,'declined')
- **Behavior:** Decline/deny a pending request. Mock flips status pending→declined. Real verb sets status='cancelled' + stamps approved_by_staff_id (the actor discriminator vs owner self-cancel — the request_status enum has no 'denied' value).
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** POST /staff/requests/:id/deny @ routes/staffRequests.ts:350 · wire: PendingRequestWire @ src/contracts/wire.ts:198 · contract: §C.1 Amendment 2026-05-24 item 5 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:681-688 · tables: pending_requests @ schema.sql:745 (markCancelled with approvedByStaffId, requestsRepository.ts:497-517)
- **Request:** `{reason?: string<=500} + header Idempotency-Key. NOTE: reason is parsed (denyBodySchema, staffRequests.ts:102-108) but DISCARDED — markCancelled receives only approvedByStaffId (staffRequests.ts:380-382) and no column stores it (already adjudicated as NOTE-5, DISCREPANCIES.md:667-671).` **Response:** `PendingRequestWire (200, UPDATED — status→'cancelled', approved_at + approved_by_staff_id stamped by markCancelled when staff id passed, requestsRepository.ts:507-517)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** markCancelled + staff id. NO notification enqueued (staffRequests.ts:360-394 has no notificationsRepository call). Portal toast 'Owner notified.' (ApprovalsTab.tsx:44) is FALSE — the backend deny sends nothing (already adjudicated as DRIFT-9, DISCREPANCIES.md:295-299). · **Errors:** 404 not_found (missing/soft-expired, staffRequests.ts:370-372); 409 conflict (status != 'submitted' — cannot deny an already-converted/awaiting-payment/cancelled request, staffRequests.ts:373-378)
- **Action:** REUSE. POST /staff/requests/:id/deny serves decline. Portal-side: align 'declined' → status 'cancelled'; fix the 'Owner notified' toast copy (no notification is sent — DRIFT-9). Optionally collect a deny reason — but today it is silently dropped (NOTE-5). No backend change required for the core verb.
- **Promote:** Portal RequestDecision ('pending'|'approved'|'declined', types.ts:153) → wire RequestStatus ('submitted'|'approved'|'approved-awaiting-payment'|'converted'|'cancelled', wire.ts:50-55). 'declined' has no wire equivalent — maps to 'cancelled' + approved_by_staff_id.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Deny reason is accepted-then-discarded (NOTE-5, adjudicated): should the portal collect it and should the backend persist it (needs a pending_requests.deny_reason column + markCancelled param)?
  - The documented deny-vs-cancel discriminator (approved_by_staff_id IS NOT NULL, DATA-CONTRACT.md:687) is NOT on PendingRequestWire (wire.ts:198-220 has no such field; already adjudicated as DRIFT-8, DISCREPANCIES.md:289-293) — a portal that wants to render 'Denied by [staff]' vs 'Cancelled by owner' cannot, since approvedByStaffId stays in the non-wire full-row projection (requestsRepository.ts:52-90).

### `bookings.undoDecision` — revert a decided request to pending
- **Portal:** `undoDecision(id: RequestId): Promise<BookingRequest> @ src/api/client.ts:208` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:352-355 → logic/approvals.ts:22-27 undoDecision (throws only if already 'pending'); undo button ApprovalsTab.tsx:87-95
- **Behavior:** Revert a decided (approved/declined) request back to pending. Mock is trivially reversible because mock-approve is a status-only flip — logic/approvals.ts:22-27 flips any non-pending back to 'pending'.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** none · wire: PendingRequestWire @ src/contracts/wire.ts:198 (reused; no new shape) · contract: no coverage — state machine @ requests-approval.md:126 states 'There is no un-cancel / un-approve / undo transition anywhere in the backend' · tables: pending_requests @ schema.sql:745
- **Request:** `PROPOSED: {} (empty body) + header Idempotency-Key` **Response:** `PROPOSED: PendingRequestWire (200, status→'submitted', approved_at/approved_by_staff_id cleared)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** PROPOSED: none for undo-of-deny (deny had no side effects). Undo-of-approve is NOT offered (see action). · **Errors:** PROPOSED: 404 not_found; 409 conflict (status not staff-denied-cancelled)
- **Action:** BUILD NEW (scoped). Why can't we reuse: broad grep of all src/routes/*.ts for undo/reopen/revert/bulk verbs finds nothing that reverses a terminal request state — approve and deny are strictly one-way (R15 @ requests-approval.md:65 locks both to status='submitted' with 409 otherwise; requests-approval.md:126: converted/cancelled are terminal, no undo transition exists). Proposed signature: POST /staff/requests/:id/reopen [staff], empty body, Idempotency-Key required; valid ONLY from status='cancelled' WITH approved_by_staff_id set (i.e. a staff DENY, not an owner self-cancel) → flip to 'submitted', clear approved_at + approved_by_staff_id; 200 PendingRequestWire; errors 404 / 409 conflict (converted terminal; owner-cancelled is not the staffer's to reopen). Undo-of-APPROVE is deliberately NOT built: a converted request already INSERTed bookings, debited credits / opened invoices, and enqueued notifications + reminders — reversing it is a cancel-booking + refund saga, not a status flip. The mock conflates the two because mock-approve creates nothing (part of adjudicated DRIFT-10).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Undo-of-approve is effectively not implementable as a simple endpoint (must reverse booking creation, credit/charge, notifications, reminders). Should the portal drop the undo affordance on approved rows and keep undo only for staff-denials? Product decision for Allison/Shanthi.
  - Even undo-of-deny is a NEW backend transition (submitted←cancelled) that the state machine currently forbids (requests-approval.md:126) — needs an explicit ruling before build.

### `bookings.approveAll` — bulk-approve the queue
- **Portal:** `approveAll(): Promise<BookingRequest[]> @ src/api/client.ts:209` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:357-363 (maps all pending→approved, trivial); useApproveAll @ useBookingRequests.ts:35-41; ApprovalsTab.tsx:145-156 'Approve all' button (toast 'All pending requests approved. Owners notified.' @ 149)
- **Behavior:** Bulk-approve every pending request in the queue in one action. Mock maps all status==='pending' rows → 'approved' with no scheduling (mock/index.ts:357-363).
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** none · wire: PendingRequestWire @ src/contracts/wire.ts:198 (per-row; reused) · contract: no coverage — no bulk request surface in §C.1 · tables: pending_requests @ schema.sql:745; bookings/booking_dogs per converted row
- **Request:** `PROPOSED: {decisions: [{request_id: uuid, scheduled_at?: ISO, pickup_at?: ISO, location?: 'fayetteville'|'bentonville', notes?: string}]} + header Idempotency-Key. Per-request scheduling is unavoidable — see action.` **Response:** `PROPOSED: partial-result envelope {results: [{request_id, outcome: 'converted'|'approved-awaiting-payment'|'error', converted_booking_id?, error_code?}]} — some rows may bounce on gates/capacity/overlap while others succeed.`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** PROPOSED: per successfully-approved row, the same side effects as single approve (bookings, credits/invoices, notifications, reminders, cache invalidation). · **Errors:** PROPOSED: per-row 422 (gate/capacity/credits bounce) / 409 (non-submitted) / 404 in the results envelope; whole call 200 with mixed outcomes.
- **Action:** BUILD NEW (with a hard caveat) OR drop. Why can't we reuse: broad grep of all src/routes/*.ts confirms no bulk/approve-all endpoint exists anywhere, AND the single approve verb requires per-request scheduled_at/location(/pickup_at) for PL/boarding — so a no-arg approveAll (as the mock models it) is semantically impossible for those categories. Honest proposal: POST /staff/requests/approve-all [staff] taking a decisions[] array with per-request scheduling, looping the single-approve core per row (row-locked, one tx per row) and returning a partial-success envelope. A no-body variant could only serve B&T (→awaiting-payment) and day-program (verbatim) rows. Recommendation: likely YAGNI — the value of a bulk button collapses once each PL/boarding row needs its own date/location. Confirm intent before building.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Is bulk-approve actually wanted given PL/boarding each need per-request scheduling? The mock's zero-arg approveAll cannot map to the real per-category body (adjudicated as part of DRIFT-10) — recommend either dropping it or scoping to the no-body categories (B&T + day-program) only.
  - If built, per-row idempotency + partial-failure semantics need a ruling (one Idempotency-Key for the batch vs per-row keys).

### Supply-only routes (backend serves, portal does not call)
| Route | Where | Serves | Portal-relevant? |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET /requests | routes/requests.ts:180 | SUPPLY-ONLY. GET /requests?status= — owner-scoped read of the caller's OWN live requests, optional status filter; staff principals get an empty list [] (requests.ts:186). Serves the mobile owner app's 'my requests' list, not the portal. | No portal demand — the portal's cross-owner queue is served by GET /staff/requests, not this owner-scoped route. Portal will NOT need it. |
| GET /requests/:id | routes/requests.ts:195 | SUPPLY-ONLY. GET /requests/:id — owner single-request read of own row; staff principals 404 (requests.ts:201-203). Mobile owner request-detail surface. | No portal demand today — the portal ApprovalDetail (onOpenDetail, ApprovalsTab.tsx:65-69) reads request detail from the already-fetched list cache. There is NO staff single-request read (GET /staff/requests/:id does not exist — staffRequests.ts registers only the three routes at 123/137/350). Portal likely won't need it (detail from list); flag as a potential future gap only if the portal wants a fetch-by-id detail route. |
| POST /requests | routes/requests.ts:239 | SUPPLY-ONLY. POST /requests — owner submits a new pending request (private-lesson \| board-and-train \| boarding) with ranked preferred_dates; runs ownership + evaluation (B&T/boarding lead-dog) + duplicate (already_requested @ requests.ts:308-314) gates; INSERTs status='submitted', source='app'. Returns 201 PendingRequestWire. | No portal demand — request submission is an owner action (mobile). Portal (staff) will NOT need it. |
| PATCH /requests/:id | routes/requests.ts:365 | SUPPLY-ONLY. PATCH /requests/:id — owner edits own request while status='submitted' (preferred_dates / notes / focus / length_weeks / lesson_setting); identity fields locked; tri-state partial update (null=clear, undefined=leave). Returns 200 PendingRequestWire. | No portal demand — owner-only edit. Portal will NOT need it. |
| POST /requests/:id/cancel | routes/requests.ts:451 | SUPPLY-ONLY. POST /requests/:id/cancel — owner self-withdrawal; status→'cancelled' WITHOUT stamping approved_by_staff_id (markCancelled with no staff arg, requests.ts:480; the discriminator vs staff deny). Allowed from 'submitted' and 'approved-awaiting-payment'; 409 conflict from converted/cancelled (requests.ts:473-478). Returns 200 PendingRequestWire. | No portal demand — owner self-cancel is distinct from the staff deny verb (which the portal uses via declineRequest → POST /staff/requests/:id/deny). Portal will NOT need it. |

_Entry-level open question — GET /requests/:id:_
- If the portal later wants a dedicated staff request-detail fetch, a GET /staff/requests/:id (staff-scoped, cross-owner) would need to be added — no such route exists today.

### Open questions
- undoDecision + approveAll have NO backend equivalent (confirmed by broad grep across all src/routes/*.ts) and are architecturally incompatible with the real model: converted/cancelled are terminal one-way states (requests-approval.md:126; R15 @ :65 locks approve/deny to status='submitted') with no undo transition, and approve requires per-category scheduling (scheduled_at/location/pickup_at) that a no-arg bulk call cannot supply. These two mock affordances need a product decision (drop vs redesign) before any backend build.
- The entire portal BookingRequest shape (types.ts:155-167) is a parallel universe vs PendingRequestWire: flat whenLabel string, submission.sections intake, CampusAbbr, display-string 'type' including an 'Evaluation' category the real request lane never produces (REQUEST_CATEGORIES @ requests.ts:103; evaluations book directly). Aligning the portal to PendingRequestWire is a rewrite of the approvals feature, not a field remap.
- divert_reasons already rides PendingRequestWire (wire.ts:217) but the mock has zero divert concept — the STATUS.md follow-up (STATUS.md:125-126) is a pure portal-render addition once the portal consumes the real wire. Day-program divert CREATE is domain 05 (POST /bookings 202); the divert CONVERSION lives inside POST /staff/requests/:id/approve (staffRequests.ts:190-260), folded into the approveRequest entry.
- The sole/canonical desktop portal consumes NOTHING from wire.ts (no contracts.ts, no sync script — DRIFT-5/DRIFT-6, DISCREPANCIES.md:271-281); wiring it is a to-build task. The approve/deny UX redesign targets this portal (canonicity resolved 2026-07-25).
- Deny reason is accepted-then-dropped (NOTE-5, DISCREPANCIES.md:667-671) and the deny-vs-cancel discriminator (approved_by_staff_id) is not on the wire (DRIFT-8, DISCREPANCIES.md:289-293) — both block any portal 'Denied by [staff] for [reason]' rendering until backend changes land.
- Portal decline toast 'Owner notified.' (ApprovalsTab.tsx:44) is false — the real deny verb enqueues no notification (DRIFT-9, DISCREPANCIES.md:295-299); portal copy needs fixing regardless of wiring. The B&T approve leg is additionally dead-ended product-wide: no client calls POST /requests/:id/confirm-payment (DRIFT-7, DISCREPANCIES.md:283-287).

## Stays / Lodging (boarding, board & train, mid-stay day school) <a id="stays"></a>
_Demand: StaysApi @ client.ts:225-232 (StayListParams @ 83-89, StayTimingPatch @ 91-94; Stay/BT_NIGHTS/BOARD_CAP @ types.ts:186-207) · Supply: none owned — EXPLORE: grep src/routes/ + schema.sql for boarding/board_train/stay/lodging/night; boarding may live inside bookings categories (§C.1 six booking flows) · Logic: stayOccupancy.ts · Business logic: bookings-scheduling.md (boarding portions)_

The portal models Stay as a first-class entity (5-state StayStatus, exclusive endISO checkout, kennel, BOARD_CAP=15/night, B&T fixed 14 nights), but the backend has NO stays table — a stay is a single `bookings` row (category boarding/board-and-train) whose `dropoff_at`/`pickup_at` window is internal and unemitted on BookingWire, with no nightly boarding capacity anywhere. Read demand (list=B, get=B) needs `dropoff_at`/`pickup_at` promoted onto the wire; cancel=A reuses `POST /staff/bookings/:id/cancel` as-is; the two re-timing / mid-stay day-school verbs are net-new C-bucket staff routes. Stay creation itself has no StaysApi verb — stays are born through the request approve / confirm-payment flow, captured here as SUPPLY-ONLY. The portal is not wire-synced today, so promoting `dropoff_at`/`pickup_at` costs a wire.ts minor bump + mobile resync but carries zero portal migration risk.

### `stays.list` — list boarding + board-and-train stays
- **Portal:** `list(params?: StayListParams): Promise<Stay[]> @ client.ts:226 (StayListParams @ client.ts:83-89)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:466-481 (list + client-side campus/status/type/coversDate filters); stayOccupancy.ts:11-13 stayCovers, :21-27 deriveStayStatus, :30-34 nightlyOccupancy
- **Behavior:** Staff list of boarding + board-and-train stays, filterable by campus/status/type/coversDate; each row rendered as a Stay card (check-in→checkout window, derived StayStatus, kennel, care sheet).
- **Bucket:** B — schema change flagged (see Action)
- **Backend:** GET /staff/bookings @ routes/staffBookings.ts:68 · wire: BookingWire @ wire.ts:143-171 (confirmed: no dropoff_at/pickup_at today) · contract: §B Booking @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1812-1849 (Δ 2026-05-19 additive/unknown-keys @ 1821-1823; stay bucketing on pickup_at @ 1826-1828) · tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629 (+ booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688); category IN ('boarding','board-and-train'), stay window in dropoff_at/pickup_at @ schema.sql:652-653, CHECK gate @ schema.sql:675
- **Request:** `GET query: none today (route has no params). Optional additive: ?category=boarding|board-and-train, ?location=<slug>, ?from=&to=` **Response:** `BookingWire[] where each stay row adds dropoff_at?: string, pickup_at?: string (ISO); base keys id, dog_id, additional_dog_ids?, category, status, date, location?, notes?, cancel_deadline_at?, cancelled_at?, cancel_forfeited?`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read) · **Errors:** 401 (no principal), 403 forbidden (non-staff via requireStaff, principalNarrows.ts:55-62)
- **Action:** Additive: emit dropoff_at + pickup_at (ISO ts) on BookingWire for stay categories (boarding, board-and-train) so the portal can render the check-in→checkout window and derive StayStatus; keep category/campus/status filtering client-side (the route takes NO query params today — staffBookings.ts:62-67 comment: the portal is the 'dumbest viable' client and filters client-side; ?from=&to= is a Day-20 hardening). Optional ?category=/?location= filters are not required. Mobile-safety: both keys optional/omit-on-null; DATA-CONTRACT §B Δ 2026-05-19 (DATA-CONTRACT.md:1821-1823) states existing FE toBooking ignores unknown keys — additive-only, no mobile break.
  - _schema change:_ No table/column for kennel/suite assignment or per-stay belongings (only bookings.notes free-text @ schema.sql:638). Additive columns bookings.kennel text NULL / bookings.belongings text NULL ONLY if kennel assignment is a real requirement — deferred to open_questions, not forced (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-4 flags the Stay/kennel/BOARD_CAP model as portal-mock-only).
  - _promote:_ Align portal Stay onto BookingWire + DogWire — do NOT mint a parallel Stay wire. nights = derived client-side (pickup_at − dropoff_at); StayStatus (upcoming/in-residence/checking-out-today/checked-out/canceled) = derived from status + dropoff_at/pickup_at vs today. feeding/meds/vet come from the dog (dog_feeding @ schema.sql:404, dog_medications @ schema.sql:394, primary vet), NOT the stay; kennel + belongings have no backend column (see schema_change).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Open questions:
    - coversDate / overCapacityNights nightly-occupancy filter (BOARD_CAP=15, types.ts:207; overCapacityNights @ stayOccupancy.ts:37-48) has NO backend analogue — day_capacity (schema.sql:815) is day-program-only (school_openings/daycare_openings), no per-night boarding capacity exists (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-4; the related counting bug inside the mock itself is BUG-25)
    - GET /staff/bookings uses findLiveActive which EXCLUDES status='cancelled' (bookingsRepository.ts:117-122) — a canceled-stays view has no supply; portal StayStatus 'canceled' rows would never list
    - Portal derives 5 StayStatus values; backend booking_status has only upcoming/past/cancelled (schema.sql:88) — the in-residence/checking-out-today/checked-out split depends entirely on the proposed additive dropoff_at/pickup_at

### `stays.updateTiming` — re-time a stay (move check-in / checkout)
- **Portal:** `updateTiming(id: StayId, patch: StayTimingPatch): Promise<Stay> @ client.ts:229 (patch {startISO?, endISO?} @ client.ts:91-94)` · mock logic: stayOccupancy.ts:83-97 applyTimingPatch (LG-5 end>start @ 89-91, LG-4 fixed 14-night @ 93-95); mock/index.ts:501-510
