- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** POST /bookings @ routes/bookings.ts:353 (unrelated; NO reschedule verb exists — window is set once at approve, staffRequests.ts:288-297, or at B&T confirm-payment) · wire: BookingWire @ wire.ts:143-171 (proposed additive dropoff_at/pickup_at) · contract: §A Amendment Day-12 approve body @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:659-674 (boarding pickup_at strictly after scheduled_at @ 671-672); §F lifecycle; boarding-24h reminder anchored on (dropoffAt ?? scheduledAt) - 24h @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1204-1207 · tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629 (scheduled_at:635, dropoff_at:652, pickup_at:653, cancel_deadline_at:666); no CHECK pickup>dropoff exists (only the category gate @ 675)
- **Request:** `{ scheduled_at?: string, dropoff_at?: string, pickup_at?: string } (ISO ts)` **Response:** `BookingWire (+ dropoff_at?, pickup_at?)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Re-time may need to re-anchor the boarding-24h profile-check reminder (keyed off dropoff_at, DATA-CONTRACT.md:1204-1207) and re-stamp cancel_deadline_at. · **Errors:** not_found(404), conflict(409), invalid_payload/422 (pickup<=dropoff, non-stay category)
- **Action:** Build PATCH /staff/bookings/:id/timing (staff). Body: { scheduled_at?: ISO, dropoff_at?: ISO, pickup_at?: ISO }. Response: 200 BookingWire (+ dropoff_at/pickup_at). Auth: staff. Logic: lock booking (404 missing, 409 if cancelled); require category IN ('boarding','board-and-train') — the schema CHECK gates dropoff_at/pickup_at to stay categories only (schema.sql:675); validate pickup_at strictly after dropoff_at/scheduled_at API-side (NO DB CHECK enforces pickup>dropoff — re-verified; mirror the approve rule at DATA-CONTRACT.md:671-672 and the confirm-payment Zod refine at requestConfirmPayment.ts:83-85); if scheduled_at moves, re-stamp cancel_deadline_at from cancel_window_settings and re-anchor the pending boarding-24h reminder. Errors: not_found(404), conflict(409 cancelled), 422 (pickup<=dropoff / non-stay category). Idempotency-Key required. Why not reuse: NO endpoint updates a booking's scheduled_at/dropoff_at/pickup_at after creation — staffRequests approve (staffRequests.ts:288-297 via setBoardingStayWindow @ 509-523) and requestConfirmPayment set the window once and are single-shot; full route enumeration confirms no PATCH/reschedule verb on bookings anywhere (the only adjacent PATCH is owner-scoped PATCH /requests/:id @ requests.ts:365, submitted-status requests only, pre-conversion).
  - _promote:_ Timing patch keys map to bookings.scheduled_at/dropoff_at/pickup_at; the portal's startISO/endISO ISO-DAY encoding must promote to real ISO timestamps (backend: absolute timestamptz), not the mock's day-string exclusive-night model.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Open questions:
    - Portal enforces B&T = fixed 14-night block (BT_NIGHTS=14 @ types.ts:206, stayOccupancy.ts:93-95) and exclusive-endISO checkout; backend has NO 14-night constraint and no nights column — is the fixed-14 lock a real rule to port or a mock artifact? (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-4 flags B&T-fixed-14 as portal-mock-only)
    - Should re-timing a stay re-run booking gates or recompute capacity? Mock re-times freely with only end>start + fixed-block checks

### `stays.addToDaySchool` — add mid-stay day-school days for a lodger
- **Portal:** `addToDaySchool(dogId: DogId, dates: ISODate[], override?: boolean): Promise<Booking[]> @ client.ts:231` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:512-558 (LG-6 active-B&T-only guard @ 515-522, weekend reject @ 526-529, BK-4 capacity + override @ 530-535, BK-6 one-service-per-day @ 539-542); daySchoolCapacity @ mock/index.ts:215, daySchoolBookedCount @ mock/index.ts:221
- **Behavior:** Staff adds mid-stay day-school days for a lodging dog (LG-6: active Board & Train residents only). Creates one day-school booking per date; the override switch skips the day-school capacity check (BK-4); weekend days rejected; one-service-per-dog-per-day enforced (BK-6).
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** POST /bookings @ routes/bookings.ts:353 (nearest — owner-only day-program create, unusable as-is) · wire: BookingWire @ wire.ts:143-171 · contract: §A Amendment 2026-05-22 Day-10 POST /bookings body + gates @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:208-276; §H booking gates @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2399 · tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629; booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688; day_capacity @ schema.sql:815 (override target)
- **Request:** `{ category: 'day-school', lead_dog_id: string, dates: string[], location: string, override_capacity?: boolean, notes?: string }` **Response:** `BookingWire[] (each: id, dog_id, category='day-school', status, date, location, ...)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** INSERT bookings + booking_dogs per date; enqueue booking-reminder; NO credit_ledger debit and NO invoice (comp day); day_capacity assert skipped when override_capacity; avail:{location}:* cache invalidation. · **Errors:** not_found(404), conflict(409), 422 (validation: past/weekend date, capacity_full when override=false)
- **Action:** Build POST /staff/bookings (staff) — mid-stay day-school creation for a lodger. Body: { category: 'day-school', lead_dog_id: uuid, dates: 'YYYY-MM-DD'[1..30], location: location_key, override_capacity?: boolean, notes?: string }. Response: 201 BookingWire[] (one per date, ASC). Auth: staff. Reuse lib/createDayProgramBookings.ts core with two deltas: (1) cross-owner — resolve owner_id from the dog, not the principal; (2) override_capacity=true skips the day_capacity FOR UPDATE assert (dayCapacityRepository.assertCapacityWithinLock, called @ createDayProgramBookings.ts:223; pattern documented @ schema.sql:1900-1903). Gates: the payment/vaccine/agreement/eval BEFORE-INSERT triggers still fire (schema.sql:1693/1748/1789/1837) — a lodging dog has typically already cleared them, but a staff-comp bypass policy is an OPEN question. Skip credit debit + PAYG invoice (staff comp day → no credit_ledger row, no invoice). Reject weekend/closed days and enforce one-live-booking-per-(dog,date) via the existing overlap guard. Errors: not_found(404 dog), conflict(409), 422 (bad dates / capacity_full when override false). Idempotency-Key required. Why not reuse POST /bookings: it is requireOwner (bookings.ts:358 → staff gets 403), has NO capacity-override, and force-runs credit-debit-or-PAYG + the approval-divert park — none of which fit a staff cross-owner mid-stay comp day. Full route enumeration confirms no staff booking-create route exists anywhere in routes/.
  - _promote:_ Portal returns its own camelCase Booking[] (mock/index.ts:543-556 with client-minted id 'bk-{dogId}-{date}' + '7:45 AM' display time); align to BookingWire[] (snake_case, server uuid, scheduled_at ISO ts), not the mock shape.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Open questions:
    - Money model: mock creates a plain 'confirmed' day-school booking with NO credit debit or charge (mock/index.ts:543-556) — is a mid-stay day-school day comped (bundled into the stay), credit-debited, or PAYG-charged? No backend ruling found
    - Should the 4 booking gates (vaccine/agreement/eval/payment) be bypassed for a lodger staff already has on-site? Mock skips all gates and only checks weekend + capacity
    - Mock restricts the modal to ACTIVE Board & Train residents (LG-6, mock/index.ts:515-522) — not boarding; port this lead-dog/category guard to the backend or allow any dog?

### Supply-only routes (backend serves, portal does not call)
| Route | Where | Serves | Portal-relevant? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| POST /staff/bookings/:id/confirm | routes/staffBookings.ts:87 | SUPPLY-ONLY. Stamps confirmed_at (only when NULL); 409 on a cancelled booking, 200 no-op re-confirm. Applies to all categories incl. boarding/B&T. NOTE — the 'drives reminder scheduling' claim in the route's own comments is ADJUDICATED as aspirational: DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-3 confirms reminders are enqueued unconditionally at booking creation (lib/enqueueBookingReminders) and no worker reads confirmed_at. The portal's StaysApi models no 'confirm stay' verb today — stay confirmation happens through the request approve/confirm-payment flow — so this is unused by the Stays surface. | No portal demand in the Stays domain. Leave as-is; revisit if the portal adds an explicit stay-confirm action. |
| POST /staff/bookings/:id/attendance | routes/staffBookings.ts:202 | SUPPLY-ONLY. Per-dog check-in write (booking_dogs.attendance + checked_in_at + checked_in_by_staff_id); body { dog_id, status: 'attended'\|'no-show'\|'excused' }; returns AttendanceWire { booking_id, dog_id, attendance, checked_in_at }. This is the Check-in domain, not StaysApi — the portal's lodger presence flows through CheckInApi (event-sourced board), and a B&T resident is a DERIVED lodger row (daySchoolLodgers @ stayOccupancy.ts:62-77), never a stay attendance write here. | No StaysApi demand — belongs to the Check-in domain map. Listed here only because it lives in the owned staffBookings.ts file. |
| POST /staff/requests/:id/approve | routes/staffRequests.ts:137 | SUPPLY-ONLY (missed by the prior audit's enumeration). The ONLY route that sets a boarding stay's window: for category='boarding', scheduled_at + location + pickup_at are required, the booking is inserted, then setBoardingStayWindow (staffRequests.ts:509-523) stamps dropoff_at = scheduled_at and pickup_at (staffRequests.ts:288-297). For board-and-train it only flips the request to approved-awaiting-payment (no booking yet). This is the Requests domain, but it is load-bearing stay-creation supply: portal stay creation flows through BookingsApi.approveRequest, not StaysApi. | No StaysApi demand (stay creation is out of the StaysApi surface). Listed because the explore grep for boarding/stay routes surfaced it and the prior audit's routes_enumerated omitted it. |
| POST /requests/:id/confirm-payment | routes/requestConfirmPayment.ts:95 | SUPPLY-ONLY (missed by the prior audit's enumeration). The ONLY route that creates a board-and-train stay: owner pays, a bookings row with category='board-and-train' + dropoff_at/pickup_at is inserted (scheduled_at = dropoff_at, requestConfirmPayment.ts:43-46, 228-236), request flips to 'converted'. Body Zod refine enforces pickup_at strictly after dropoff_at (requestConfirmPayment.ts:83-85). Owner-scoped — no staff analogue. | No StaysApi demand. Listed because the explore grep surfaced it as the B&T stay-creation supply; the portal never calls it (owner-only payment flow). |

### Open questions
- Stay is NOT a first-class backend entity: a boarding/board-and-train stay = ONE bookings row (category IN ('boarding','board-and-train')) with the stay window in dropoff_at/pickup_at (schema.sql:652-653; CHECK gate @ 675). Those columns are INTERNAL and NOT emitted on BookingWire (bookingWire.ts:21-27 comment lists dropoff_at/pickup_at as internal state; BookingWire @ wire.ts:143-171 confirmed to omit them) — every Stay demand (list/get/updateTiming) requires promoting them onto the wire.
- NO nightly boarding capacity exists in the backend. day_capacity (schema.sql:815) is keyed (location, date) with school_openings/daycare_openings for DAY programs only; the portal's BOARD_CAP=15/night (types.ts:207), coversDate occupancy filter, and overCapacityNights guard (stayOccupancy.ts:37-48) have zero supply. Decide: add a per-night boarding-capacity table/rule or treat boarding as uncapped by design (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-4; BUG-25 is the mock's own B&T-vs-Boarding counting bug against that cap).
- kennel/suite assignment and per-stay belongings have NO backend column (only bookings.notes free text @ schema.sql:638); feeding/meds/vet live on the dog (dog_feeding @ schema.sql:404, dog_medications @ schema.sql:394, primary vet), not the stay. Confirm whether kennel + belongings are real requirements (additive bookings columns) or mock-only fiction (DRIFT-4).
- Stay CREATION has no StaysApi verb and no direct 'create stay' backend route: stays are born via POST /requests (requests.ts:239, category boarding/board-and-train) -> POST /staff/requests/:id/approve (boarding sets scheduled_at+dropoff_at+pickup_at, staffRequests.ts:288-297 via setBoardingStayWindow @ 509-523) or POST /requests/:id/confirm-payment (B&T, requestConfirmPayment.ts:95, owner-only $). POST /bookings only accepts day-school/day-care (bookings.ts:133). The portal creates stays through BookingsApi.approveRequest, not StaysApi — now captured as two SUPPLY-ONLY entries.
- backend booking_status = upcoming/past/cancelled only (schema.sql:88); the portal derives 5 StayStatus values and GET /staff/bookings excludes cancelled rows (findLiveActive @ bookingsRepository.ts:117-122) — a canceled-stays view and the in-residence/checking-out-today/checked-out split both depend on the proposed additive dropoff_at/pickup_at fields.
- B&T fixed-14-night block and exclusive-endISO-checkout are portal-only rules (stayOccupancy.ts:8-13, 93-95; BT_NIGHTS @ types.ts:206) with no backend enforcement or nights column (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-4).

## Group classes / Terms / Sessions / Enrollments <a id="classes"></a>
_Demand: ClassesApi @ client.ts:235-243 · Supply: groupClasses.ts, enrollments.ts · Logic: termStatus.ts · Business logic: group-classes-enrollments.md_

The backend group-class surface is entirely owner-facing (browse/enroll/withdraw) plus a single generic staff attendance verb, while the portal's `ClassesApi` demands a full staff term-CRUD + broadcast + cohort-session-roster surface that the Day-19 seam (enrollments.ts:81-84,210-215) anticipated but never built. Only one of the eight portal ops reuses a backend route cleanly (`setAttendance`, bucket A); two reuse-with-additive-fields (`list`, `termsFor`, bucket B); and five are bucket C — four requiring new staff routes (`sessionsFor`, `createTerm`, `updateDefaults`, `sendReminder`) plus `checkInWeek`, which is a client-only UX gate recommended not-to-build. Load-bearing mismatches recur across every entry: the class-id enum ('manners1' vs 'manners-1', DRIFT-22), the AttendanceMark enum ('present/absent/none' vs 'attended/no-show/excused', DRIFT-22), and the fact that all group-class wire shapes live outside wire.ts (DRIFT-26). Bucket tally: A=1, B=2, C=5, SUPPLY-ONLY=6.

### `classes.list` — read the class catalog
- **Portal:** `list(): Promise<GroupClass[]> @ client.ts:235` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:562-565 (returns db.groupClasses verbatim)
- **Behavior:** Return the full group-class catalog (portal's 4 GroupClass entries). Feeds the class-list screen and is the source for the editable defaults (weeks/capacity/price/schedule/instructor) that createTerm snapshots and updateDefaults edits.
- **Bucket:** B (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** GET /group-classes @ routes/groupClasses.ts:60 · wire: GroupClassWire @ src/lib/groupClassWire.ts:24 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §B GroupClass @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1985 / §C group-classes @ 2089-2093; shared+unscoped catalog rule is BUSINESS-LOGIC group-classes-enrollments.md:27 (its R1 — NOT the DATA-CONTRACT register's R1, which is PendingRequest multi-dog) · tables: group_classes @ schema.sql:452
- **Response:** `GroupClassWire[]: {key: group_class_key, name, weeks, price_per_dog_cents, capacity, age_range?, description, enrollment_type} (+ proposed optional-omit default_instructor_staff_id?, default_schedule? if the portal keeps those fields)`
- **Auth:** [public] (owner+staff, shared/unscoped, auth required) · **Errors:** unauthenticated 401
- **Action:** REUSE GET /group-classes for the read + ADDITIVE optional-omit wire fields for the two demanded-but-unbacked columns. The catalog read itself is shared owner+staff (groupClasses.ts:28-30,60-63, no per-user scoping) — but the endpoint CANNOT return schedule.{fay,ben} or defaultInstructorId (types.ts:305-306): no wire field (groupClassWire.ts:24-33) and no column (schema.sql:452-462), so field coverage is partial = B, not A. Exact additive change: add optional `default_instructor_staff_id?` + `default_schedule?` to GroupClassWire backed by the schema_change columns, omitted when null. MOBILE-SAFETY: additive-only — mobile's mapper is a plain TS cast that ignores unknown fields (groupClassRepository.ts:87-104 toClassFromApi, no strict parser), so extra optional fields cannot break parsing. ALIGN portal GroupClass -> GroupClassWire (priceCents->price_per_dog_cents, blurb->description); map portal class ids ('puppy'|'manners1'|'manners2'|'public', types.ts:299) to the backend enum ('puppy'|'manners-1'|'manners-2'|'public-pups', schema.sql:147) — DRIFT-22 (adjudicated, DISCREPANCIES.md:373). Alternative resolving the open question as 'schedule/instructor stay per-cohort' makes this pure A with zero changes.
  - **Schema change:** ONLY IF the portal keeps schedule.{fay,ben} + defaultInstructorId as class-level fields: additive nullable `default_instructor_staff_id uuid REFERENCES staff(id)` + a per-campus schedule (jsonb or child table) on group_classes (schema.sql:452-462 has neither). If they are scoped out (kept per-cohort), no DDL and this entry collapses to pure A reuse.
  - **Promote:** GroupClassWire — currently lives only in src/lib/groupClassWire.ts:24 and is hand-mirrored by mobile (groupClassRepository.ts:47-56 RawClassApi); promote into wire.ts so portal+mobile share one contract (DRIFT-26, adjudicated in DISCREPANCIES.md:397).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - schedule (per-campus time labels) and defaultInstructorId are portal-only class fields with no backend column — add to group_classes (see schema_change / updateDefaults entry) or keep the schedule concept purely per-cohort (cohorts.weekly_time + cohorts.location)? The answer decides B vs pure-A.
    - Portal GroupClass.enrolledDogIds is a class-level roster (types.ts:307, self-flagged 'prototype simplification; real model enrolls per TERM'); backend enrollment is per-cohort per-dog only — the class-level roster has no backend equivalent and should be dropped on alignment.

### `classes.termsFor` — list a class's terms (cohorts)
- **Portal:** `termsFor(classId: ClassId): Promise<ClassTerm[]> @ client.ts:236` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:567-575 (filters db.classTerms by classId, maps deriveTermStatus, sorts by startISO); status derivation logic/termStatus.ts:15-23
- **Behavior:** List the scheduled runs ('terms' = cohorts) of one class, each with derived status (tba/upcoming/current/completed), for the class-detail terms panel.
- **Bucket:** B (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** GET /group-classes/:key/cohorts @ routes/groupClasses.ts:80 · wire: CohortWire @ src/lib/groupClassWire.ts:35 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §B Cohort @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1998 (cohorts order by start_date ASC per BUSINESS-LOGIC group-classes-enrollments.md:27) · tables: cohorts @ schema.sql:508
- **Response:** `CohortWire[]: {id, class_key, location, start_date, end_date?, weekly_time, weeks, capacity, filled} (+ proposed optional-omit instructor_staff_id? if per-term instructor must persist)`
- **Auth:** [public] (owner+staff, shared/unscoped) · **Errors:** bad_request 400 (unknown class key, Zod enum), unauthenticated 401
- **Action:** REUSE GET /group-classes/:key/cohorts for the read + ADDITIVE change for the unbacked demand. The endpoint returns cohorts for a class to staff (verified 80-88), and TermStatus is DERIVED client-side from start_date+weeks+now (termStatus.ts:15-23) needing NO backend field — but three demanded ClassTerm fields are NOT covered, so this is B, not A: (a) instructorId — cohorts has NO instructor column (schema.sql:508-521); exact additive change is the nullable instructor_staff_id column + optional-omit CohortWire field; (b) enrolledDogIds roster — not on CohortWire and backend rosters are owner-scoped (GET /enrollments only); the staff roster is deliberately NOT added here — it is served by the sessionsFor C-entry; (c) tba — start_date is NOT NULL (schema.sql:512), no 'unscheduled cohort' concept exists. MOBILE-SAFETY: an optional-omit CohortWire field is additive-only and mobile's toCohortFromApi is a non-strict TS cast (groupClassRepository.ts:105+), so mobile keeps parsing. ALIGN: portal campus==location, time==weekly_time, startISO==start_date (wire ships a full timestamptz instant — portal derives its day-string + ClockTime label). CohortWire.filled has no portal field but is useful — adopt it.
  - **Schema change:** If the portal's per-term instructorId must persist: additive nullable `instructor_staff_id uuid REFERENCES staff(id)` on cohorts (schema.sql:508-521 has no instructor column). If portal 'tba' terms must exist server-side: relax cohorts.start_date NOT NULL (schema.sql:512) — otherwise forbid tba on the staff create path.
  - **Promote:** CohortWire — lives only in src/lib/groupClassWire.ts:35 (hand-mirrored by mobile, groupClassRepository.ts:58-68); promote to wire.ts (DRIFT-26).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Backend cohorts have no per-cohort instructor (the trainer lives on each weekly bookings.trainer_staff_id, schema.sql:637, and is NULL for group-class today, enrollments.ts:361). Does the portal's per-term instructorId need the additive cohorts.instructor_staff_id column, or is it derived from the weekly bookings' trainer?
    - Portal 'tba' term (no start date) has no backend model (start_date NOT NULL, schema.sql:512). Relax start_date to nullable, or forbid tba cohorts on the staff create path?

### `classes.sessionsFor` — list a term's weekly sessions + roster
- **Portal:** `sessionsFor(termId: TermId): Promise<ClassSession[]> @ client.ts:237` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:577-581 (filters db.classSessions by termId, sorts by index); ClassSession shape types.ts:332-341 (week index, topic, trainerId, held, Record<DogId,AttendanceMark>)
- **Behavior:** List the weekly sessions of one term as per-WEEK objects, each carrying date, topic, trainer, a 'held' flag, and a per-dog attendance map — the weeks/attendance grid.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** wire: (new — define in wire.ts) · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §A Amendment 2026-06-01 item 3 (BookingWire cohort_id?/group_class_name?) @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1693-1697 + Amendment 2026-05-23 (per-week booking materialization) @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:406-468 · tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629 (cohort_id :640, scheduled_at :635, CHECK cohort_id iff group-class :674); booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688 (attendance :696, checked_in_at :697)
- **Response:** `Proposed CohortSessionWire[]: [{cohort_id, week_index:int, session_date:string(ISO), dogs:[{booking_id:uuid, dog_id:uuid, attendance:'pending'|'attended'|'no-show'|'excused'}]}]`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Errors:** not_found 404 (unknown/soft-expired cohort), forbidden 403 (non-staff), unauthenticated 401
- **Action:** BUILD GET /staff/cohorts/:cohortId/sessions [staff] -> CohortSessionWire[] (week-folded, per-dog attendance map incl. the booking_id each attendance write targets). WHY NOT REUSE (verified by broad grep of all 41 route files): sessions are not a table — a 'session' is a per-(dog x week) bookings row (schema.sql:640,674) with per-dog booking_dogs.attendance (schema.sql:696); no route anywhere reads bookings by cohort. GET /staff/bookings (staffBookings.ts:68-78, findLiveActive) returns a FLAT, un-grouped, un-filtered live queue with no cohort filter, no week-fold, and no roster projection, so it can't render the portal's per-week ClassSession grid. This endpoint groups one cohort's bookings by week (computeCohortSessionDates cadence, lib/cohortSchedule.ts) and folds booking_dogs into the per-week attendance map. THINNER ALTERNATIVE (B): add ?cohort_id= to GET /staff/bookings and fold client-side — rejected because the week-grouped+roster shape is server-of-record and also feeds setAttendance's booking_id addressing. NOTE 'topic'/'trainerId' (mock ClassSession, types.ts:337-338) — topic has no backend column at all; trainer is bookings.trainer_staff_id (schema.sql:637), NULL for group-class today (enrollments.ts:361).
  - **Promote:** New CohortSessionWire shape should be defined in wire.ts from birth (avoids the mobile/portal hand-mirror trap of the other group-class shapes, DRIFT-26).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Portal ClassSession.topic (per-week curriculum label, types.ts:337) has no backend column anywhere — omit, or add a per-(class,week) curriculum table?
    - Should the session roster include withdrawn dogs? Backend tracks only live bookings; a withdrawn dog vanishes from the week grid.

### `classes.setAttendance` — set one dog's attendance for a session
- **Portal:** `setAttendance(sessionId, dogId, mark: AttendanceMark): Promise<ClassSession> @ client.ts:238` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:583-596 (held-gate guard GC-3, enrollment-membership check, writes session.attendance[dogId])
- **Behavior:** Set one dog's attendance for one weekly session and return the updated session.
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** POST /staff/bookings/:id/attendance @ routes/staffBookings.ts:202 · wire: AttendanceWire @ src/contracts/wire.ts:177 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §A Amendment 2026-05-28 (Day 19a — staff portal backend verbs) @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1526,1541-1546 pins the response shape; staffBookings.ts:31-36 labels it '§A Clarification 2026-05-28' ('pending' is default/unset, never a settable action) · tables: booking_dogs @ schema.sql:688 (attendance :696, checked_in_at :697, checked_in_by_staff_id :698)
- **Request:** `{dog_id: uuid, status: 'attended'|'no-show'|'excused'} (path :id = the (cohort,dog,week) booking_id; Idempotency-Key header required)` **Response:** `AttendanceWire: {booking_id, dog_id, attendance: 'attended'|'no-show'|'excused', checked_in_at}`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Stamps booking_dogs.attendance + checked_in_at + checked_in_by_staff_id (staffBookings.ts:233-238). NOTE: group-class attendance does NOT reset the 3-month re-eval clock (BUSINESS-LOGIC R20 @ group-classes-enrollments.md:52; bookingApprovalDivert.ts:14-27), and does NOT auto-write dog_completed_classes (BUG-8). · **Errors:** not_found 404 (booking missing/expired @ staffBookings.ts:222-225, or dog not on booking @ 239-241), conflict 409 (booking cancelled @ 226-231), invalid_payload 422 (bad status @ 267-273; errors.ts maps invalid_payload->422), forbidden 403 (requireStaff)
- **Action:** REUSE POST /staff/bookings/:id/attendance — already staff-scoped and does exactly per-dog attendance (staffBookings.ts:196-256); the mutation is fully covered, and the two mismatches are portal-side alignment, not endpoint gaps. ALIGN the enum: mock AttendanceMark 'present'|'absent'|'none' (types.ts:329) -> backend AttendanceStatus 'attended'|'no-show'|'excused' (wire.ts:79); 'none' maps to the DB default 'pending' (schema.sql:89,696) which is NOT settable via the endpoint. Portal gains a third value 'excused'. ADDRESSING: the endpoint targets a booking_id, not the mock's synthetic per-week sessionId ('ses-<term>-w<n>', mock/index.ts:632) — the portal must pass the (cohort,dog,week) booking_id supplied by the sessionsFor C-entry. RETURN SHAPE: the endpoint returns AttendanceWire, not a full session — the portal refetches/folds sessionsFor (client-side shape preference, not a data gap). The mock's held-gate (GC-3, only-edit-held-weeks) has no backend equivalent (backend allows attendance on any non-cancelled booking, staffBookings.ts:222-241) — see checkInWeek entry. Enum + parallel-model mismatch is adjudicated as DRIFT-22 (DISCREPANCIES.md:373).
  - **Promote:** AttendanceWire already lives in wire.ts:177 — portal should ADOPT it (drop the mock ClassSession return + present/absent/none enum).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Mock has only present/absent; backend has attended/no-show/excused. Confirm the portal UI should expose all three (add 'excused') on alignment.
    - Backend rejects attendance on cancelled bookings (409) but has no 'week not held yet' guard — the portal's held-gate becomes a client-only affordance.

### `classes.checkInWeek` — mark a week held
- **Portal:** `checkInWeek(sessionId: SessionId): Promise<ClassSession> @ client.ts:240` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:598-604 (flips session.held, guards double check-in); held semantics types.ts:339 ('done (date <= today) OR staff pressed Check in')
- **Behavior:** Mark a week 'held' (session.held=true), which unlocks attendance editing for that week in the portal.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629 (no 'held' column — grep of schema.sql for 'held' is clean); booking_dogs.attendance @ schema.sql:696 (default 'pending')
- **Auth:** [staff]
- **Action:** RECOMMEND DO NOT BUILD (bucket C only nominally). WHY NOT REUSE: nothing in the backend models a per-week 'held' state — there is no bookings/booking_dogs 'held' column (schema.sql:629-701), and attendance is writable on any non-cancelled booking with no held-gate (staffBookings.ts:222-241). The mock's 'held' is a client-only UX gate (GC-3) whose own definition is DERIVABLE ('session date <= today OR staff pressed check-in', types.ts:339) — i.e. the week's session_date <= today, or any booking_dogs.attendance for the week != 'pending'. Keep 'held' as client-local UI state computed from the sessionsFor payload. IF a durable staff 'held' fact is truly required later, it needs a new per-(cohort,week) artifact (sessions aren't a table) + a POST verb — YAGNI today.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Confirm the portal is willing to compute 'held' client-side (session_date<=today OR attendance already marked) rather than have the backend persist it — no backend 'held' concept exists and none is planned.

### `classes.createTerm` — create a term (cohort)
- **Portal:** `createTerm(input: CreateTermInput): Promise<ClassTerm> @ client.ts:241 (CreateTermInput 96-106)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:606-644 (validates start-date-required-unless-tba :609-611, snapshots weeks/capacity/instructor from class GC-4 :612-625, mints client id allocId('term') :614, auto-generates classSessions per week :629-642)
- **Behavior:** Staff creates a new scheduled run (cohort) of a class, snapshotting the class defaults (weeks/capacity/instructor) and generating the weekly sessions.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** wire: CohortWire @ src/lib/groupClassWire.ts:35 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §C mutation surface @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2089-2093 — no staff cohort-create verb exists; Day-19 seam anticipated at enrollments.ts:81-84 · tables: cohorts @ schema.sql:508 (INSERT); group_classes @ schema.sql:452 (defaults source)
- **Request:** `Proposed {class_key: group_class_key, location: 'fayetteville'|'bentonville', start_date: string(ISO instant), weekly_time: string, weeks?: int, capacity?: int} (Idempotency-Key required)` **Response:** `CohortWire: {id, class_key, location, start_date, end_date?, weekly_time, weeks, capacity, filled}`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Insert one cohorts row (filled=0). Should invalidate the anticipated groupclasses:*/cohorts:* caches (the exact seam flagged unbuilt at enrollments.ts:81-84,210-215). · **Errors:** bad_request 400 (bad class_key/location enum), invalid_payload 422 (weeks/capacity <= 0), forbidden 403 (non-staff), unauthenticated 401
- **Action:** BUILD POST /staff/cohorts [staff, idempotent] body {class_key, location, start_date, weekly_time, weeks?, capacity?} -> 201 CohortWire. WHY NOT REUSE (verified by enumerating every registered route in all 41 route files): no staff (or any) cohort/term WRITE route exists — the only cohort mutation anywhere is enrollments' filled bump; cohorts are created only by seed/tests today (BUSINESS-LOGIC group-classes-enrollments.md:79,117; the Day-19 'staff cohort edits' comments at enrollments.ts:81-84,210-215 anticipate this but it was never built). Defaults (weeks/capacity) snapshot from group_classes when omitted, mirroring the mock GC-4 and the cohorts.capacity 'snapshot of class cap' comment (schema.sql:516). DROP CreateTermInput.enrolledDogIds (client.ts:105): pre-enrolling dogs at cohort creation has NO backend path and would bypass the owner-only, money-charging, gate-checked POST /enrollments flow (BUSINESS-LOGIC R8 @ group-classes-enrollments.md:40; enrollments.ts:157-158 requireOwner, staff get 403).
  - **Schema change:** cohorts has no instructor column: if the portal's per-term instructorId must persist, add `instructor_staff_id uuid REFERENCES staff(id)` to cohorts (additive, nullable) — schema.sql:508-521. Also cohorts.start_date is NOT NULL (schema.sql:512): the mock's tba (start-less) term needs start_date relaxed to nullable OR tba forbidden on this path.
  - **Promote:** CohortWire into wire.ts (shared create response, DRIFT-26).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - tba cohorts: relax cohorts.start_date to nullable or forbid start-less cohorts on the staff path?
    - instructorId persistence: add cohorts.instructor_staff_id, or derive the term instructor from the weekly bookings' trainer_staff_id?
    - Staff pre-enroll (CreateTermInput.enrolledDogIds): does the portal actually need staff to enroll dogs (a new staff enroll verb that charges/gates), or is the roster always owner-driven via POST /enrollments?

### `classes.updateDefaults` — edit a class's defaults
- **Portal:** `updateDefaults(classId: ClassId, patch: ClassDefaultsPatch): Promise<GroupClass> @ client.ts:242 (patch 108-113)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:646-651 (Object.assign patch onto the class); ClassDefaultsPatch fields client.ts:108-113 (name/priceCents/weeks/capacity/blurb/schedule/defaultInstructorId)
- **Behavior:** Staff edits a class's default fields (name, price, weeks, capacity, blurb, schedule, default instructor); future terms snapshot the new defaults.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** wire: GroupClassWire @ src/lib/groupClassWire.ts:24 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT §C mutation surface @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2089-2093 — catalog is GET-only today; cache-invalidation contract groupClassesRepository.ts:44-67,86,105,132-135 (R3 numbering is the BUSINESS-LOGIC doc's, group-classes-enrollments.md:29) · tables: group_classes @ schema.sql:452 (UPDATE)
- **Request:** `Proposed partial {name?, price_per_dog_cents?, weeks?, capacity?, age_range?, description?, enrollment_type?} (Idempotency-Key required)` **Response:** `GroupClassWire: {key, name, weeks, price_per_dog_cents, capacity, age_range?, description, enrollment_type}`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** UPDATE group_classes; MUST invalidate Redis groupclasses:catalog / groupclasses:byKey:{key} / groupclasses:prereqs:{classKey} — 'a catalog edit (Day 19 portal verb) invalidates ALL three Day-8 group-class cache shapes via invalidatePattern(groupclasses:*)' (groupClassesRepository.ts:44-67; BUSINESS-LOGIC R3 @ group-classes-enrollments.md:29). · **Errors:** bad_request 400 (unknown class key — enum-validated), invalid_payload 422 (weeks/capacity/price <= 0), forbidden 403 (non-staff), unauthenticated 401
- **Action:** BUILD PATCH /staff/group-classes/:key [staff, idempotent] partial-body -> 200 GroupClassWire. WHY NOT REUSE (verified): the catalog is read-only server-side — groupClasses.ts registers only GETs (60-125), and a broad grep of every route file shows no group-class write path anywhere. ALIGN patch fields to snake_case wire columns (priceCents->price_per_dog_cents, blurb->description). Two patch fields have NO backing column (schedule, defaultInstructorId) -> see schema_change; either add columns or scope them out. MOBILE-SAFETY: mobile consumes GET /group-classes via a plain TS-cast mapper (groupClassRepository.ts:87-104) that ignores unknown fields; this route only WRITES + invalidates cache, and adding optional-omit columns to GroupClassWire is additive-only, so mobile keeps parsing.
  - **Schema change:** group_classes has NO schedule or default_instructor columns (schema.sql:452-462). To persist patch.schedule.{fay,ben} (per-campus meeting-time labels) and patch.defaultInstructorId, add `default_instructor_staff_id uuid REFERENCES staff(id)` + a per-campus schedule (e.g. `default_schedule jsonb` or a group_class_schedules child table) — additive. If these stay per-cohort-only, drop them from the patch.
  - **Promote:** GroupClassWire into wire.ts (shared edit response; any additive columns become optional-omit fields so mobile's non-strict mapper stays safe — MOBILE-SAFETY: additive-only, no rename/retype).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Persist schedule + defaultInstructor on group_classes (new columns) or keep schedule/instructor strictly per-cohort? Same gap that keeps classes.list at bucket B for the defaults panel.
    - Editing weeks/capacity on a class with live cohorts: cohorts snapshot their own weeks/capacity (schema.sql:515-516) so existing runs are unaffected — confirm that 'defaults apply to future terms only' is intended.

### `classes.sendReminder` — send a term reminder to owners
- **Portal:** `sendReminder(termId: TermId): Promise<{recipientCount: number}> @ client.ts:243` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:653-662 (dedupes enrolled dogs' ownerIds into a Set, returns its size as recipientCount)
- **Behavior:** Staff sends an ad-hoc reminder to every owner of a dog enrolled in a term; returns how many owners were notified.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** contract: DATA-CONTRACT §C mutation surface @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2089-2093 — no staff-initiated broadcast/reminder verb exists; automatic booking-reminder is scheduler-driven (§F lifecycle / R32) · tables: bookings @ schema.sql:629 (live cohort bookings -> distinct owner_id); scheduled_notifications (enqueue target)
- **Request:** `Proposed {} / no body (path :cohortId; Idempotency-Key required)` **Response:** `Proposed {recipient_count: int}`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Enqueue one ad-hoc reminder notification per distinct owner of a live-enrolled dog in the cohort; recipient_count = that distinct-owner count. Distinct from the AUTOMATIC per-session booking-reminder enqueued at enroll (BUSINESS-LOGIC R32 @ group-classes-enrollments.md:73, enqueueBookingReminders @ enrollments.ts:350-356). · **Errors:** not_found 404 (cohort missing/soft-expired), forbidden 403 (non-staff), unauthenticated 401
- **Action:** BUILD POST /staff/cohorts/:cohortId/reminder [staff, idempotent] -> {recipient_count}. WHY NOT REUSE (all re-verified this session): there is NO staff-initiated send/broadcast verb anywhere — announcements.ts registers a single GET (line 41, no create; the portal's AnnouncementsApi.create is likewise backend-less), the notifications routes are owner feed management only (read @ notifications.ts:114, read-all @ 144, delete @ 172), and the existing booking-reminder is enqueued automatically inside the enroll tx (enrollments.ts:350-356) — none is a staff trigger. recipient_count/recipientCount has NO precedent in routes/, contracts/, or DATA-CONTRACT.md (grep clean, re-run), so it is a fresh shape. Recipients = distinct owner_id of dogs with live (non-cancelled) bookings in the cohort (mirror the mock's owner-Set dedupe).
  - **Promote:** Define the {recipient_count} response in wire.ts (new shape).
- **Confidence:** INFERRED — The 'no staff broadcast verb exists' claim is VERIFIED (announcements GET-only, notifications owner-only, recipient_count grep clean); the endpoint path, body, and recipient_count semantics are a proposal not present in code.
  - _Open questions:_
    - What message does sendReminder deliver, on which channels (in-app feed vs push), and is it idempotent per (cohort, staff, day) so a double-click doesn't double-notify?
    - Should recipient_count count OWNERS (mock behavior) or enrolled DOGS? And should withdrawn dogs be excluded (they will be, since only live bookings count)?

### Supply-only routes (backend serves, portal does not call)
| Route | Where | Serves | Portal-relevant? |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET /group-classes/:key | routes/groupClasses.ts:66 | Single-class catalog read (shared owner+staff). Serves a class-detail fetch by key; 404 if not found. | SUPPLY-ONLY: no portal op maps (ClassesApi.list returns all classes at once). The portal MAY eventually want it to refresh one class after updateDefaults, but currently re-lists — low priority. |
| GET /cohorts/:id | routes/groupClasses.ts:91 | Single-cohort read by id (shared owner+staff). Returns one CohortWire; 404 if missing. | SUPPLY-ONLY: no portal op maps (termsFor returns all cohorts for a class). The portal MAY use it to refresh a single term (its capacity/filled) after createTerm or an enrollment — useful but not currently demanded. |
| GET /dogs/:id/group-eligibility | routes/groupClasses.ts:105 | Server-derived eligibility for one (dog, class): returns {class_key, eligible, missing_prereq_options?}. Owner-only, own dogs; staff and not-your-dog both get 404 (anti-enumeration). | SUPPLY-ONLY: owner-only by design (groupClasses.ts:112-114 returns 404 to staff principals). No portal op — the portal's prereq model is the mock prerequisiteClassId. If the portal ever shows per-dog eligibility on a staff roster, it would need a NEW staff-scoped eligibility read (this one deliberately 404s staff), not a reuse. NOTE: the eligibility SOURCE table has no production writer (BUG-8, DISCREPANCIES.md:72, CONFIRMED — adjudicated), so real dogs can never become eligible for prereq-gated classes until that write path lands. |
| POST /enrollments | routes/enrollments.ts:153 | Owner-only cohort enrollment: materializes \|dog_ids\| x weeks single-dog group-class bookings under a cohort row lock, runs eligibility/capacity/duplicate/gate checks, charges per dog (pay-now) or opens per-dog invoices (pay-later). Staff -> 403. | SUPPLY-ONLY: owner-only, money-charging, gate-checked (requireOwner, enrollments.ts:157-158). No portal op maps — and staff-side enrollment does not exist. The portal's createTerm.enrolledDogIds is NOT this endpoint; a staff enroll verb (that charges/gates or exempts) would be a separate build if ever needed. |
| GET /enrollments | routes/enrollments.ts:430 | Owner-only 'currently enrolled' list: one row per live (cohort, dog) with class/cohort display fields + derived payment_status + can_withdraw. No 'finished-cohort' filter (NOTE-19, DISCREPANCIES.md:751 — adjudicated). | SUPPLY-ONLY: owner-scoped (own dogs, requireOwner enrollments.ts:434-435). The portal needs a STAFF cohort ROSTER (all enrolled dogs of a cohort, cross-owner) to drive attendance — this owner-scoped endpoint does NOT provide it. That gap is covered by the sessionsFor C-entry (GET /staff/cohorts/:cohortId/sessions), which folds the cohort's live bookings into a per-dog roster. |
| POST /enrollments/:cohortId/withdraw | routes/enrollments.ts:450 | Owner-only per-dog withdraw before the first session: under the cohort lock soft-cancels the dog's weekly bookings, decrements filled, voids the pay-later invoice or refunds the charge. Returns {withdrawn, refunded_cents}. | SUPPLY-ONLY: owner-only and pre-first-session only (enrollments.ts:515-523). No portal op — and the shared cancel unconditionally rejects group-class for BOTH owner and staff (cancelBookingService.ts:74-79), so once a cohort starts NO code path can unenroll/refund. This gap is ALREADY ADJUDICATED: BUG-7 in DISCREPANCIES.md:65 (verified 2026-07-21, CONFIRMED, severity refined bug->drift: 'deferred-capability gap, not a broken code path') = domain doc Known drift #2 — the withdraw route's own 'staff can still cancel via the portal' comment (enrollments.ts:515-517) is false today. If the portal needs staff roster management (remove/refund a dog), that is a NEW staff verb, not a reuse. |

Supply-only entry-level open questions:
- (GET /dogs/:id/group-eligibility) If the staff portal wants to show why a dog is/ineligible on a cohort roster, a staff-scoped eligibility read is needed — the current endpoint 404s staff on purpose.
- (GET /enrollments) Portal cohort roster (cross-owner enrolled dogs) is unmet by this owner-scoped list — confirm it is served by the proposed staff sessions endpoint rather than a separate GET /staff/cohorts/:id/roster.
- (POST /enrollments/:cohortId/withdraw) Post-start withdraw/refund policy is undetermined in code (BUG-7 / drift #2): if the portal exposes staff unenroll, define the money policy (pro-rata / full / none) and whether it may act after the first session.

### Open questions
- Portal wiring DIRECTION is unresolved (BUSINESS-LOGIC group-classes-enrollments.md open questions): does the portal ADOPT the backend cohort model (dropping terms/held-weeks/class-defaults editing and its 'tba'/topic/instructor concepts), or does the backend GROW staff routes to match the portal's terms/sessions spec? Four of the eight ops (sessionsFor, createTerm, updateDefaults, sendReminder) are bucket C precisely because the backend never built the Day-19 staff cohort surface anticipated at enrollments.ts:81-84.
- Class-id enum mismatch is load-bearing across every entry: portal 'manners1'/'public' (types.ts:299) vs backend enum 'manners-1'/'public-pups' (schema.sql:147). Any wiring must map ids first — adjudicated as DRIFT-22 (DISCREPANCIES.md:373).
- AttendanceMark mismatch: portal 'present'/'absent'/'none' (types.ts:329) vs backend 'attended'/'no-show'/'excused' (+'pending' default) (wire.ts:79, schema.sql:89). Aligning adds 'excused' and makes 'none'->'pending' unsettable. Also DRIFT-22.
- No backend writer for dog_completed_classes (BUG-8, DISCREPANCIES.md:72, adjudicated CONFIRMED) means marking a term 'completed' (and thus feeding eligibility) has no path — if the portal's term-completion is meant to record completions, that write path must be designed (staff report verb, cohort-end worker, or manual portal verb).
- Wire-contract gap: GroupClassWire, CohortWire, GroupEligibilityWire, EnrollmentWire and the enrollment request/422 shapes are all OUTSIDE wire.ts v1.0.0 — adjudicated as DRIFT-26 (DISCREPANCIES.md:397, acknowledged backlog in CHANGELOG/STATUS.md) — every A/B/C entry here should promote its shape into wire.ts so portal+mobile stop hand-mirroring and check:contracts can guard drift.

## Reports / Report cards + Evaluations (verdicts) <a id="reports"></a>
_Demand: ReportsApi @ client.ts:246-254 (ops 247-253), evalGate.ts:9-15 (+ DogsApi list/get @ client.ts:183-186) · Supply: reports.ts, staffReports.ts · Logic: reportVerdict.ts, evalGate.ts · Business logic: reports-media.md, dogs-evaluations.md_

The portal's 7 ReportsApi ops assume a cross-owner staff report surface with a draft/sent lifecycle, a server-owned report-type/skill catalog, and portal-only vocabulary — almost none of which the backend supplies. There is NO staff report LIST/READ endpoint despite reports.ts:15-17 claiming `/staff/reports/*` is used for cross-owner access (only POST + PATCH exist), and the backend has NO draft state: POST /staff/reports creates a LIVE owner-visible report plus a report-published notification instantly (R2), so saveDraft/send/delete have no server analog. The recommended path keeps everything additive — client-held drafts (send = POST /staff/reports, delete = a new DELETE that sets the already-existing reports.expired_at), a net-new GET /staff/reports for list, a staff branch on GET /reports/:id for get, and client-side catalogs aligned to mobile reportPrograms.ts — while the whole portal report vocabulary is already adjudicated as DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-33 + BUG-12/13/14 + DRIFT-37. The Evaluations (verdicts) half of the domain is OPEN: no staff verb records an eval verdict and evaluation_status is owner-writable today (trust inversion, adjudicated BUG-10/BUG-11/Q1).

### `reports.list` — cross-owner staff list of report cards
- **Portal:** `list(params?: ReportListParams): Promise<Report[]> @ client.ts:247 (ReportListParams @ client.ts:115-119)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:666-677
- **Behavior:** Cross-owner staff list of report cards, filtered by dogId / typeId (report type) / status (draft|sent), sorted date desc then id asc. Mock: index.ts:666-677.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** wire: ReportWire @ wire.ts:309 · contract: DATA-CONTRACT.md:2095-2099 (§C Reports surface lists only the 4 owner reads + POST/PATCH /staff/reports; no staff report LIST) · tables: reports @ schema.sql:535
- **Request:** `query: { dog_id?: uuid, program?: report_program, page?: int }` **Response:** `ReportWire[] (wire.ts:309-328)`
- **Auth:** [staff]
- **Action:** PROPOSE GET /staff/reports?dog_id=&program=&page= [staff] -> ReportWire[] (cross-owner, live-only, newest first). Why not reuse: GET /dogs/:id/reports (reports.ts:49) is owner-scoped (staff principals get [] by design, reports.ts:55, R3) AND dog-scoped (requires a dogId, no typeId filter, no cross-owner), so it cannot back a staff cross-dog report list. Mobile-safe: brand-new /staff/* path, owner clients never touch it. The portal's status=draft|sent filter has NO backend analog (no draft state, R2 reports-media.md:60) and must be dropped or made client-side. The nonexistent portal-shaped GET /reports surface + draft/sent status is already ADJUDICATED as DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-33 (portal report model diverges wholesale) — this proposal is the wiring answer, not a new bug.
  - **Promote:** portal Report model (types.ts:409-436, camelCase, kind/status) -> align to wire ReportWire; the draft|sent status filter has no backend column to filter on (DRIFT-33)
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - reports.ts:15-17 asserts the staff portal 'uses /staff/reports/* for cross-owner access' but only POST + PATCH exist -- no staff read/list is implemented. How does the portal enumerate existing reports when wired? (reports-media.md:205 open Q.)
    - The portal status=draft|sent filter cannot map (backend has no draft state, R2).

### `reports.get` — fetch one report by id (staff, cross-owner)
- **Portal:** `get(id: ReportId): Promise<Report> @ client.ts:248` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:679-682 (requireReport @ index.ts:135-138, isReportDeleted @ 131-133)
- **Behavior:** Fetch one report by id for staff (cross-owner). Mock: index.ts:679-682 (404 if missing or soft-deleted).

- **Auth:** [owner] (existing) + add [staff] cross-owner

Today staff principals get a hard 404 (anti-enumeration, reports.ts:125-127); add a staff branch that reads the row cross-owner, mirroring the GET/DELETE /media/:id staff-access amendment (DATA-CONTRACT.md:1614-1618 -- 'a [staff] principal may now read + soft-expire ANY live media row (cross-owner trusted)'). Mobile-safety: mobile principals are owners and keep the existing owner-scoped path unchanged (reports.ts:128-137); only the staff arm changes (404 -> row).

  - _Open questions:_

### `reports.types` — report-type catalog
- **Portal:** `types(): Promise<ReportType[]> @ client.ts:249` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:684-687
- **Behavior:** Return the report-type catalog (kind cur|ses, layout grid|flat, stage 1-5, labels, colors) for the 7 portal report types. Mock: index.ts:684-687; shape types.ts:357-370; ids types.ts:346-353.

Instead ALIGN the portal ReportType vocabulary to mobile's canonical reportPrograms.ts + wire ReportProgram literals: portal-only ids 'leash'/'house'/'private'/'boardtrain' -> 'loose-leash'/'house-manners'/'private-lesson'/'board-train-session' (adjudicated as DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-33). If a shared reference catalog is ever wanted it is an unauthenticated GET /report-programs, never a staff verb. Broad-grep confirmed: no catalog/types endpoint exists anywhere in src/routes/.

- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Should the backend ever own a report-program/skill reference catalog, or does it stay client-canonical in mobile reportPrograms.ts? (Skill-key governance open Q, reports-media.md:206.)

- **Portal:** `saveDraft(input: ReportDraftInput): Promise<Report> @ client.ts:251 (ReportDraftInput @ client.ts:126-128)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:697-724; verdict is pure client display (reportVerdict.ts:13-52, R12) and only informs verdict_headline -- the backend never computes a verdict

## Credits / Ledger / Lots / Credit packages / Expiry <a id="credits"></a>
_Demand: credits.balanceFor/lotsForDog/ledgerForOwner @ client.ts:264-267 · Supply: credits.ts, staffCreditExpiry.ts, creditPackages.ts · Logic: creditLedger.ts · Business logic: credits-packages.md_

The entire credits domain is absent from wire.ts v1.0.0 (NOTE-12 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:709) — CreditsWire, the full-lot shape, CreditLedgerEntryWire, CreditExpirySettingWire and CreditPackageWire are all inline route types, hand-duplicated in mobile, with no portal copy. Of the 3 assigned portal read ops, only `balanceFor` reuses an existing route (bucket B on GET /dogs/:id/credits, today owner-only and returning aggregates + expiring-only lots), while `lotsForDog` and `ledgerForOwner` are net-new staff C builds — the portal is fully greenfield in this domain in real mode (credits-packages.md mock/real divergence #9 @ credits-packages.md:211; src/api/http/ confirmed empty). Four SUPPLY-ONLY routes round out the domain: the staff credit-expiry-settings GET/POST (not portal-mirrored yet), the credit-package catalog GET (editor demand owned by domain 11), and the owner-only Stripe purchase POST. Headline mismatch: the portal models credits with mock quirks that contradict backend runtime (DRIFT-13 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:319 — CreditMode 'dayschool'!='school', per-lot unitPriceCents/invoiceId, event kinds including a nonexistent 'expire'), and any build-out must align to backend shapes, never mint the mock shapes.

### `credits.balanceFor` — read derived per-dog credit balance
- **Portal:** `balanceFor(dogId: DogId, mode: CreditMode, campus: Campus): Promise<CreditBalance> @ client.ts:266-267` · mock logic: creditLedger.ts balanceFor:70-93, doc comment :69 (scopes lots by dog/mode/campus + lotUsable:49-51, sums lotRemaining:24-46, tracks nextExpiry); mock/index.ts credits.balanceFor:838-843; CreditBalance shape types.ts:537-543

Target: staff principal permitted in addition to owner.` **Response:** `{ dog_id, location, school: int, daycare: int, expiring_lots: [{ mode: 'school'|'daycare', remaining: int, expires_at: iso }], warning_lead_days: int } (CreditsWire credits.ts:39-58) — portal folds: available = school|daycare selected by mode; next_expiry = min(expires_at) over expiring_lots filtered to that mode`
- **Auth:** [owner] today -> add [staff] · **Side effects:** None (read). Backend read-through Redis display cache, 60s TTL (CREDITS_TTL_SEC, creditsRepository.ts:62; R34) — display-only, never authoritative. Note for the staff branch: R34's balance cache key is owner-scoped (credits:{dogId}:{ownerId}:balance:{loc}), so the staff arm needs its own key or a cache bypass. · **Errors:** 404 not_found (dog missing; for owners collapses not-yours into missing for id-anti-enumeration, credits.ts:18-23,78-80); 404 to any staff principal today (credits.ts:70-72, R28); 400 bad_request (missing/invalid location, credits.ts:107-113). Staff branch would 404 only on a truly-missing dog.
- **Action:** B — add a staff-principal branch to GET /dogs/:id/credits returning the SAME CreditsWire for any dog at the location (cross-owner), replacing the current `principal.kind !== 'owner' -> 404` (credits.ts:70-72). Mobile-safety: the owner arm is untouched — still owner-scoped, still 404-collapses not-yours into missing; the staff arm is a new branch, wire shape identical, so the mobile client sees zero change. Portal HTTP adapter maps mode 'dayschool'->'school' + campus 'Fayetteville'->'fayetteville' and folds the both-modes wire to one (mode,campus) CreditBalance.
  - **Promote:** Promote CreditsWire { dog_id, location, school, daycare, expiring_lots[], warning_lead_days } into wire.ts — today inline at credits.ts:39-58 and hand-duplicated in mobile mobile/src/repositories/creditsRepository.ts:58-81, no portal copy (NOTE-12 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:709). Additive minor bump. Adjacent adjudicated drift: mobile types expiring_lots/warning_lead_days optional though the server always emits them (DRIFT-14 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:325).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Alignment: portal CreditMode 'dayschool' vs backend 'school' (adjudicated DRIFT-13 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:319); portal Campus 'Fayetteville'/'Bentonville' (types.ts:14) vs backend location slug 'fayetteville'/'bentonville' — adapter translation, target wire is backend's.
  - Adding a cross-owner staff branch to an owner route whose 404-collapse is a deliberate anti-enumeration control (credits.ts:18-23, R28): acceptable as additive, or should staff balance come from a dedicated staff endpoint (the lotsForDog C endpoint, from whose lots balance folds — modulo never-expiring pool credits that carry no expiring lot)? Both work; B is the minimal change.

SUPPLY-ONLY — no assigned portal op writes it. Ready for a future staff editor (staff-scoped, idempotent, band-validated). |
| GET /credit-packages | creditPackages.ts:118 | Active per-location credit-package catalog on Chicago-today (effective-dated); any authenticated principal (reference data, requirePrincipal only @ creditPackages.ts:122). | Portal catalog/editor demand (OfferingsApi.packages()/savePackage() @ client.ts:290-291) is owned by domain 11 — cross-ref; not among this domain's 3 assigned ops. Portal's Package type (types.ts:627-641) is an unrelated mock world (adjudicated DRIFT-12 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:313). SUPPLY-ONLY — no staff package-editor WRITE endpoint exists (R25, DRIFT-12; stale 'active = false' comment @ creditPackages.ts:48-50 is wrong, DRIFT-11 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:307); a savePackage C is domain 11's to propose. |
| POST /credit-packages/:key/purchase | creditPackages.ts:142 | Owner-only synchronous Stripe purchase: create+confirm a PaymentIntent off the owner's stored card, write the charges row, and (only on succeeded) the credit_ledger purchase lot with alumni/1-credit/window expiry stamping (R7/R8/R11); optional quantity 1-100 buys N units in one charge/one lot (R9). | Staff CANNOT purchase (requireOwner @ creditPackages.ts:147). No assigned portal staff op maps. SUPPLY-ONLY — owner-facing money path; requireOwner (creditPackages.ts:147) blocks staff outright. |

### Open questions
- Entire credits domain is absent from wire.ts v1.0.0 (only payment?:'credits'|'payg' at wire.ts:216; version at wire.ts:35; NOTE-12 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:709) — CreditsWire/CreditLotWire/CreditPurchaseWire/CreditExpirySettingWire/CreditPackageWire are all inline route types, hand-duplicated in mobile, no portal copy; promoting any is an additive minor wire bump needing orchestrator sign-off vs staying inline.
- Portal models credits with mock quirks contradicting backend runtime (adjudicated DRIFT-13 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:319): CreditMode 'dayschool'!='school'; per-lot unitPriceCents/invoiceId vs backend price-pinned-via-package_id + charge_id; event kinds consume/return/refund-to-credit/adjust/expire vs backend ledger_reason; NO 'expire' event (passive lapse, R2). Any portal build-out must ALIGN to backend shapes — never mint the mock shapes.
- No staff-scoped per-dog credit-lots read and no per-owner credit-ledger read exist — portal is fully greenfield in this domain in real mode (credits-packages.md mock/real divergence #9 @ credits-packages.md:211; src/api/http/ confirmed empty); lotsForDog + ledgerForOwner are net-new staff C builds, balanceFor is the only reuse candidate (B on GET /dogs/:id/credits).
- balanceFor can be served either by adding a staff branch to GET /dogs/:id/credits (B, minimal) OR by folding the proposed staff credit-lots C endpoint (lots -> available + nextExpiry; note never-expiring pool credits contribute to available but appear as expires_at-null lots); which the reconciliation picks is open.
- Staff manual credit-adjustment ('adjustment' ledger_reason) has no write path — only the Stripe payment_failed handler writes it (R16; credits-packages.md:217, DISCREPANCIES.md:979); if the portal ever needs a 'comp a credit'/import-fix tool that is a further staff C, outside the 3 assigned read ops.
- Portal packages()/savePackage() catalog CRUD demand (client.ts:290-291) and any staff package-editor write endpoint (none exists backend-side, R25/DRIFT-12) are owned by domain 11 (offerings) — cross-ref, not adjudicated here.
- No portal UI exists for the staff credit-expiry-settings routes (route comment 'NOT portal-mirrored yet', staffCreditExpiry.ts:19); whether one is planned is undetermined — already adjudicated @ DISCREPANCIES.md:982.
- No DELETE endpoint — a per-location override, once created, can be re-valued but never removed to fall back to the org default; intentional or gap is undetermined — already adjudicated @ DISCREPANCIES.md:980 and credits-packages.md:218.
- Is a staff package-editor endpoint planned backend-side? The cache comment names an invalidation pattern for one but no route exists (credits-packages.md:215, creditPackagesRepository.ts:62-64) — contract-first says backend moves first; belongs to domain 11.

## Invoices / Refunds / Payments / PAYG / Stripe <a id="invoices"></a>
_Demand: InvoicesApi.list/get/refund/remind @ client.ts:257-260 (InvoiceListParams 130-136, RefundInput 138-145) · Supply: invoices.ts, staffInvoices.ts, paymentMethods.ts, stripeWebhook.ts, requestConfirmPayment.ts · Logic: refundGuard.ts · Business logic: payments-invoices.md_

The portal exposes four staff invoice ops (InvoicesApi.list/get/refund/remind @ client.ts:257-260), but the backend has almost no staff invoice surface: the only staff read (GET /staff/invoices @ staffInvoices.ts:71) is a parked-only worklist that 400s without `?parked`, and there is no staff invoice detail, no staff refund verb, and no manual remind route anywhere in src/routes/. So one op is bucket B (widen the parked worklist into a real staff list) and three are bucket C (new endpoints — get, refund, remind). The deeper mismatch is that the portal's entire money model diverges from backend runtime (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-17): the portal has multi-line numbered invoices (INV-2048), statuses Open|Paid|Overdue|Refunded|Void, and invoice-keyed card|credit|cash refunds, while the backend ships single-amount numberless invoices (status open|paid|void) with charge-keyed card-only refunds — all four ops must ALIGN to the shipped charges/refunds model, not the portal spec. wire.ts v1.0.0 contains no invoice/refund/payment shapes at all, so every wire shape here is inline and must be promoted.

### `InvoicesApi.list` — staff invoice worklist + search
- **Portal:** `list(params?: InvoiceListParams): Promise<Invoice[]> @ fetchd_staff_portal_desktop/src/api/client.ts:257 (InvoiceListParams @ client.ts:130-136)` · mock logic: mock invoices.list @ src/api/mock/index.ts:741-767; InvoiceListParams @ client.ts:130-136; Invoice/InvoiceStatus @ src/api/types.ts:441-465
- **Behavior:** Staff worklist + search of invoices filtered by owner, campus, status, kind (offering|retail) and free-text (invoice number / owner name / dog name), newest-first (issuedDate desc, number desc — mock/index.ts:764-766).
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** GET /staff/invoices @ routes/staffInvoices.ts:71 · wire: ParkedInvoiceWire @ routes/staffInvoices.ts:41-50 (inline, NOT in wire.ts — wire.ts v1.0.0 contains no invoice/refund/payment shapes, re-verified by grep) · contract: §C.5 item 5 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1335-1346; §I @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2454-2478 · tables: invoices @ .claude/backend/schema.sql:1114-1145 (invoice_status enum open|paid|void @ schema.sql:92)
- **Request:** `query { owner_id?: uuid, status?: 'open'|'paid'|'void', purpose?: 'payg'|'package'|'board-train'|'membership'|'group-class', parked?: any (flag), search?: string }` **Response:** `StaffInvoiceWire[] = [{ id, owner_id, amount_cents, status:'open'|'paid'|'void', purpose, dog_id, booking_id, cohort_id, due_at, issued_at, paid_at, paid_charge_id, auto_charge_attempts }]`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read-only) · **Errors:** today: 400 bad_request when `?parked` omitted (staffInvoices.ts:78-82) — must be relaxed so an unfiltered/other-filter list is legal
- **Action:** B — the ONLY staff invoice read that exists (GET /staff/invoices @ staffInvoices.ts:71) is a parked-only worklist: it requires `?parked`, 400s otherwise (staffInvoices.ts:78-82), and delegates to invoicesRepository.findParked (staffInvoices.ts:83) whose predicate is `status='open' AND next_attempt_at IS NULL`, ordered due_at ASC (invoicesRepository.ts:383-388; the minAttempts arg is ignored by design — BL known drift #8). Additive change: (1) make `parked` OPTIONAL (drop the 400) so it returns all invoices when absent; (2) add optional query filters `owner_id`, `status`, `purpose`, `search`; (3) widen the row to StaffInvoiceWire (add paid_at, paid_charge_id, booking_id, cohort_id, issued_at); (4) decide ordering for the unfiltered list (portal wants issued desc; the parked worklist today is due_at ASC). Mobile-safety: /staff/* is staff-scoped and the mobile app never calls staff surfaces (BL cross-repo map @ payments-invoices.md:206-207 marks them n a); making a required param optional and adding optional filters is backward-compatible with the existing parked worklist consumer, so nothing breaks. Note: the query schema is .strict() (staffInvoices.ts:34-39), so new filter params are server-additive, not silently ignored today.
  - **Promote:** Widen ParkedInvoiceWire → StaffInvoiceWire and lift it into src/contracts/wire.ts; the portal's rich Invoice model (types.ts:452-465) must ALIGN to it rather than mint a parallel portal-only invoice shape.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Portal filters by `campus` (Campus='Fayetteville'|'Bentonville', types.ts:14) but the invoices table has NO campus/location column (schema.sql:1114-1145) — location only exists on the linked booking/charge; a campus filter needs a booking join or a schema_change and cannot be served additively today.
    - Portal `kind: 'offering'|'retail'` (client.ts:134) has no backend equivalent — invoice.purpose is payg|package|board-train|membership|group-class (charge_purpose @ schema.sql:148); there is NO 'retail' concept server-side. The shop/retail domain is portal-only mock per DISCREPANCIES (the domain itself is labeled 'Shop / Retail Items (portal-only)'; BUG-28 is a separate in-mock save bug within it). How should kind map / is retail invoicing in scope?
    - Portal InvoiceStatus adds 'Overdue' and 'Refunded' (types.ts:441) which the backend invoice_status (open|paid|void @ schema.sql:92) does not have — 'Overdue' is a derived/date view and 'Refunded' lives on the linked charge, not the invoice (schema.sql:1119, BL R32 @ payments-invoices.md:106). Already adjudicated as DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-17 — vocabulary must align to the shipped model, not the portal spec.
    - Portal Invoice is multi-line (items: InvoiceLine[], types.ts:445-461) with an immutable human `number` ('INV-2048') and dogIds[]; backend invoices are single-amount, no line items, no number, single dog_id. `items`/`number`/`refundedCents` cannot be sourced from the current schema (DRIFT-17).

### `InvoicesApi.get` — one full invoice for the detail/refund modal
- **Portal:** `get(id: InvoiceId): Promise<Invoice> @ client.ts:258` · mock logic: mock invoices.get @ src/api/mock/index.ts:769-772 (requireInvoice @ index.ts:107-111); refundGuard.remainingRefundableCents/isFullyRefunded @ src/api/mock/logic/refundGuard.ts:10-16
- **Behavior:** Staff fetch of one full invoice for the detail/refund modal — line items, total, cumulative refundedCents, derived status, and enough to drive Remind/Refund actions.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** — (none; proposed, see Action) · wire: — · contract: §C.5 item 5 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1335-1346; §I @ 2454-2478 · tables: invoices @ schema.sql:1114; refunds @ schema.sql:1215-1228; charges @ schema.sql:1082-1099
- **Request:** `path param { id: uuid }` **Response:** `StaffInvoiceDetailWire { id, owner_id, amount_cents, currency, status:'open'|'paid'|'void', purpose, dog_id, booking_id, cohort_id, request_id, membership_id, payment_method_id, paid_charge_id, issued_at, due_at, paid_at, next_attempt_at, auto_charge_attempts, refunds:[{ id, charge_id, amount_cents, status:'pending'|'succeeded'|'failed', reason, created_at }], refunded_cents (derived = Σ succeeded refunds on paid_charge_id) }`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (read-only) · **Errors:** 404 not_found (invoice absent)
- **Action:** PROPOSED: GET /staff/invoices/:id [staff] → StaffInvoiceDetailWire (shape above). Errors: 404 not_found. Auth: requireStaff (owner→403), mirroring staffInvoices.ts:76. No gates (read). Why not reuse: GET /invoices (invoices.ts:79) is OWNER-scoped and returns LedgerEntryWire ledger rows (not a single invoice; staff get an empty list, invoices.ts:81); GET /staff/invoices?parked (staffInvoices.ts:71) is a parked-only LIST with no :id read. Re-verified this session by enumerating every quoted route path under src/routes/: no GET /invoices/:id or /staff/invoices/:id exists anywhere. refunded_cents/derived status must be computed by joining the invoice's paid_charge_id to refunds rows — the invoice row itself has no refund state (schema.sql:1114-1145).
  - **Promote:** Share the StaffInvoiceWire base shape with InvoicesApi.list; add refunds[]+refunded_cents only on the :id detail.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - `items`/`number`/`dogIds[]` on the portal Invoice have no backend source (single-amount, numberless invoices — DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-17) — the detail modal's line-item list cannot be populated without a schema_change or a synthesized single line.
    - Should the detail also surface the payment method's brand/last4? charges carry NO payment_method_id link (schema.sql:1082-1099; BL known drift #9 — intentionally absent, flagged as requiring a frozen-schema change), so 'which card paid' is unavailable; only invoices.payment_method_id (the bound card, may differ from what actually charged) is joinable.

### `InvoicesApi.refund` — issue a refund against an invoice
- **Portal:** `refund(input: RefundInput): Promise<Refund> @ client.ts:259 (RefundInput @ client.ts:138-145)` · mock logic: mock invoices.refund @ src/api/mock/index.ts:774-808; refundGuard.assertRefundAllowed/applyRefund @ src/api/mock/logic/refundGuard.ts:19-40; RefundMethod @ types.ts:467; RefundReason @ types.ts:469-470; Refund @ types.ts:472-482; RefundModal standalone mode @ src/features/invoices/components/RefundModal.tsx:98-104
- **Behavior:** Staff issues a refund against an invoice: amount, method (card=original payment | credit=account credit | cash), reason enum, optional note, optional ticked line indices; server enforces Σ refunds ≤ captured.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** — (none; proposed, see Action) · wire: — · contract: §I Refund rules @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2470-2478 (partial refunds allowed, cumulative cap, money-back vs credit-back distinct); BL R9/R35 @ payments-invoices.md:60,112 · tables: refunds @ schema.sql:1215-1228 (charge_id NOT NULL, amount_cents CHECK >0, reason free text, refund_status pending|succeeded|failed @ schema.sql:151); charges @ schema.sql:1082; invoices @ schema.sql:1114
- **Request:** `{ charge_id: uuid } XOR { invoice_id: uuid }, amount_cents: int>0, reason?: string, note?: string` **Response:** `RefundWire { id, charge_id, owner_id, amount_cents, status:'pending', reason, created_at }`
- **Auth:** [staff] [idempotent] · **Side effects:** INSERT refunds row status='pending' in-tx; post-commit stripe.createRefund + refundsRepository.markStripeId (mirrors the R35 pattern: settleInvoiceCharge.ts:85-93 doc + invoices.ts:177-191 postCommit); charge flips to 'refunded' only when Σ succeeded ≥ amount via charge.refund.updated webhook (stripeEventHandlers.ts:499-513). No booking gates fired. · **Errors:** 404 not_found (charge/invoice absent), 409 conflict (invoice not 'paid' / has no paid_charge_id — nothing captured to refund), 422 refund_exceeds_captured (amount_cents > charge.amount_cents − Σ non-failed refunds, R9), 400 bad_request (both/neither of charge_id|invoice_id)
- **Action:** PROPOSED: POST /staff/refunds [staff][idempotent] body { charge_id XOR invoice_id, amount_cents:int>0, reason? } → 201 RefundWire (status:'pending'). Resolve invoice_id → invoices.paid_charge_id (409 if not paid); enforce R9 cap = charge.amount_cents − Σ non-failed refunds (422); createPending in-tx, Stripe refund post-commit, webhook reconciles. Auth requireStaff (owner→403). Why not reuse: re-verified this session that refundsRepository.createPending has exactly FOUR call sites, all side-effects — booking cancel (cancelBookingService.ts:131, money-back branch ~125-147), group-class withdraw (enrollments.ts:556, block 550-570), duplicate invoice settle (settleInvoiceCharge.ts:149-161), duplicate membership subscribe (memberships.ts:309-320) — and route-path enumeration confirms NO route lets staff refund an arbitrary charge/invoice. BL open question @ payments-invoices.md:261 ('Staff-initiated refunds') flags exactly this gap.
  - **Promote:** RefundWire → wire.ts; align the portal Refund (types.ts:472-482) to charge-keyed refunds (drop invoiceId as the key; keep an invoice_id echo for display only).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Portal refunds are keyed to INVOICES with methods card|credit|cash (types.ts:467); backend refunds are keyed to CHARGES and are CARD-ONLY (Stripe refund to the original PI, schema.sql:1215-1228) — already adjudicated as DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-17. method='credit' (portal writes a credit_ledger 'refund-to-credit' event, mock/index.ts:793-806) belongs in credit_ledger, not refunds; method='cash' has NO backend representation. Which methods does v1 support server-side, and where does credit-back / cash-refund get recorded?
    - Portal RefundModal has a STANDALONE mode (invoice=null, 'Money back to any client', RefundModal.tsx:98-104) that is toast-only because the ApiClient has no invoice-less refund verb — a charge_id-keyed POST /staff/refunds supports this, but confirm the portal wants owner/charge-level refunds not tied to an invoice.
    - Portal reason is an enum (RefundReason, types.ts:469-470: Returned item|Canceled session|Overcharge|Service issue|Goodwill|Other); refunds.reason is free text (schema.sql:1222). Adopt the enum or keep free text?
    - itemsReturned (line indices, client.ts:144) is UI-only amount prefill (mock only echoes it, index.ts:786) and has no server meaning given single-amount invoices — safe to accept-and-ignore or drop.
    - NEW (found this session): DATA-CONTRACT §C @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2160 documents a `GET /refunds` refund-history endpoint, but no such route exists anywhere in src/routes/ (grep confirmed) — doc-vs-code drift. Any staff refund verb should decide whether that promised owner-facing refund read ships alongside it.

### `InvoicesApi.remind` — send a payment reminder to the owner
- **Portal:** `remind(id: InvoiceId): Promise<void> @ client.ts:260` · mock logic: mock invoices.remind @ src/api/mock/index.ts:810-817; refundGuard.canRemind @ src/api/mock/logic/refundGuard.ts:43-46 (blocks Paid/Refunded/Void)
- **Behavior:** Staff sends a dunning/payment reminder to the owner for a collectable invoice; blocked on Paid/Refunded/Void (mock canRemind).
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** — (none; proposed, see Action) · wire: — · contract: §C.5 item 5 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1335-1346; BL R13 notify-on-park @ payments-invoices.md:68; Notifications section @ payments-invoices.md:185-189 · tables: invoices @ schema.sql:1114; scheduled_notifications (the push-dunning channel the worker uses) / notifications (feed)
- **Request:** `path { id: uuid }, body {} (optional { note?: string })` **Response:** `200 { ok: true } (or 204 No Content)`
- **Auth:** [staff] [idempotent] · **Side effects:** enqueue an owner notification (type 'payment-reminder' or reuse 'payment-failed' copy, deep_link_path '/account/billing') (Phase-2 WIP retargets these to `/account/invoices` with structured kind `invoice`). Precedent is scheduledNotificationsRepository.enqueueIdempotent — the worker's park push (invoiceAutoCharge.ts:371-392) enqueues a scheduled_notifications row with dedupe_key `payment-failed:<invoiceId>` delivered feed+push on the next scheduler tick; a staff remind should ride the same lane with e.g. dedupe `staff-remind:<invoiceId>:<day>` so repeat taps don't spam. · **Errors:** 404 not_found (invoice absent), 409 conflict (invoice not 'open' — already paid/void; backend has no Refunded/Overdue invoice state)
- **Action:** PROPOSED: POST /staff/invoices/:id/remind [staff][idempotent] body {} → 200 {ok:true}. Gate: invoice.status must be 'open' else 409; enqueue an owner payment-reminder via scheduledNotificationsRepository.enqueueIdempotent (the dedupe-keyed feed+push lane the park notification uses, invoiceAutoCharge.ts:371-392 — NOT notificationsRepository.enqueue, which is the direct feed-only insert). Auth requireStaff (owner→403). Why not reuse: the ONLY dunning notification today is the auto-charge worker's single automatic 'payment-failed' push fired once on PARK, deduped `payment-failed:<invoiceId>` (R13, invoiceAutoCharge.ts:351-392, MAX_AUTO_CHARGE_ATTEMPTS=4 @ invoiceAutoCharge.ts:59); route enumeration confirms NO manual/staff-triggered remind route exists — the notification infra is present but has no staff verb to re-nudge an owner.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Mock canRemind blocks Paid/Refunded/Void (refundGuard.ts:43-46), but backend invoice_status has no 'Refunded'/'Overdue' — collectability collapses to status='open'. Should remind be allowed on a PARKED open invoice (which already got one auto payment-failed push), and does a manual remind reset/interact with the `payment-failed:<invoiceId>` dedupe?
    - Reminder channel/copy: reuse the worker's 'payment-failed' push + '/account/billing' deep link, or a distinct 'payment-reminder' type? (worker copy is failure-framed; a staff nudge on a not-yet-failed invoice reads differently.) (Phase-2 WIP retargets these to `/account/invoices` with structured kind `invoice`.)
    - Rate-limit / idempotency window for repeat staff taps (per-day dedupe key?) to avoid owner spam.
