### Open questions
- The portal's ENTIRE invoice/money model diverges from backend runtime (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-17 — re-confirmed @ DISCREPANCIES.md:343; BL payments-invoices.md:241 drift #2): portal has multi-line numbered invoices (INV-2048), InvoiceStatus Open|Paid|Overdue|Refunded|Void, invoice-keyed refunds with card|credit|cash methods, and a MoneyEvent audit log; backend has single-amount numberless invoices, status open|paid|void, charge-keyed card-only refunds, and no MoneyEvent. All four InvoicesApi ops must ALIGN to the shipped charges/refunds model — do not build to the portal docs/backend-logic.md §4.7-4.8 spec.
- No backend column carries an invoice's campus/location (schema.sql:1114-1145, re-read this session) — the portal's campus filter/column needs a booking join or a schema_change; escalate before promising it.
- 'retail' invoices (kind=offering|retail, client.ts:134) have no backend concept — the shop/retail domain is portal-only mock (DISCREPANCIES shop domain is labeled portal-only; BUG-28 is a separate in-mock Service-item save bug within it). Confirm whether retail invoicing is in product scope before wiring the kind filter.
- Refund method beyond card: 'credit' (account credit) belongs in credit_ledger, not the refunds table; 'cash' has no backend representation. Decide where credit-back and cash refunds are recorded, or restrict v1 staff refunds to card (original payment).
- Manual staff 'remind' vs the worker's automatic single payment-failed-on-park push (R13): does a staff nudge coexist with / reset the `payment-failed:<invoiceId>` dedupe, and what channel+copy (reuse 'payment-failed'/'/account/billing' or a new 'payment-reminder' type)? (Phase-2 WIP retargets these to `/account/invoices` with structured kind `invoice`.)
- payments_enabled kill switch (BL open question @ payments-invoices.md:259, re-confirmed) is referenced in stripe.ts:13-15 + schema comments but has no reading code — if the portal adds money-moving verbs (refund), clarify whether they must also honor this gate.
- NEW: DATA-CONTRACT.md:2160 documents `GET /refunds` (owner refund history) but no such route exists in src/routes/ (verified by grep this session) — doc-vs-code drift to reconcile when designing the staff refund surface.

## Memberships <a id="memberships"></a>
_Demand: OfferingsApi (client.ts:287-294; packages() @ client.ts:290, savePackage() @ client.ts:291), Package.subscription @ types.ts:636-640 · Supply: memberships.ts, staffMemberships.ts · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: memberships.md_

All five backend membership routes are SUPPLY-ONLY: the mock portal has zero ApiClient membership namespace (client.ts re-verified — no membership/subscribe references), so the entire §J.1 lifecycle — owner subscribe/list/cancel and staff pause/resume — is portal-demand-less today. The only membership-adjacent portal demand is the offerings catalog's `Package.subscription` flag (offerings.savePackage/packages), a mock-parallel subscription product (30-day-from-first-use, upfrontPct discount, 1-36mo terms) that is incompatible with the backend §J.1 model (3/6/9/12mo, calendar-month-from-subscribe, no upfront discount) and is a credit-package CATALOG concern belonging to domain 08/11, not this membership lifecycle. Staff pause/resume are API-ready but their portal UI is explicitly parked (PARKED-FEATURES §4b) — bucket-A reuse when built, the only prep being promotion of the route-inline MembershipWire/MembershipCreateWire/StaffMembershipWire shapes into wire.ts (none exist in v1.0.0; DRIFT-21). Headline mismatch: no portal contracts.ts, wires hand-mirrored with no drift guard, and the portal conflates catalog config with the membership lifecycle.

### `offerings.savePackage / offerings.packages` — staff configure a credit package's subscription eligibility (mock-parallel, off-domain)
- **Portal:** `OfferingsApi @ client.ts:287-294: packages(): Promise<Package[]> @ client.ts:290; savePackage(input: SavePackageInput): Promise<Package> @ client.ts:291; Package.subscription?: { renews; upfrontPct } @ types.ts:636-640; subChipLabel @ src/features/offerings/lib/offeringFormat.ts:105 / subscriptionSummary @ offeringFormat.ts:110 (feature lib, not src/api); PackageBuilderModal.tsx builder UI (src/features/offerings/components/)` · mock logic: mock savePackage @ src/api/mock/index.ts:963; packages() @ index.ts:958; CreditLot.expiresAt 'subscription lots: 30-day expiry per cycle' comment @ types.ts:511 (also mock-30-day, not §J period-end)
- **Behavior:** The ONLY membership-adjacent demand in the mock portal: staff configure a credit package's subscription eligibility via the offerings catalog — Package.subscription = { renews: number[] (term options in months, []=one cycle), upfrontPct: number (pay-upfront discount, 0=monthly only) }. This is a mock-PARALLEL subscription product (30-day cycle STARTING at first credit use, staff-authored 1-36mo terms per PackageBuilderModal.tsx:79-80, upfront-pay discount, credits expire on a 30-day timer) that is INCOMPATIBLE with the backend §J.1 model (terms exactly 3/6/9/12, period = clamped calendar month FROM the subscribe instant, no upfront discount, expiry at billed period end). It models CATALOG configuration of subscribable packages, NOT the owner's active membership lifecycle (subscribe/list/cancel/pause/resume) — of which the portal has ZERO demand.
- **Bucket:** OPEN
- **Backend:** null · wire: null · contract: §J.1 (the real membership model the portal flag contradicts) DATA-CONTRACT.md:2544-2598; Amendment 2026-07-16 DATA-CONTRACT.md:940-993; DRIFT documented memberships.md:127 (drift item 2) · tables: backend credit_packages @ schema.sql:986-998 (catalog: key/location/mode/credits/price_cents/label/is_popular/effective window) — NOT memberships; the portal Package.subscription flag has no backend column (verified: no renews[]/upfrontPct/is-subscribable in the DDL). Adjudicate in domain 08/11.
- **Request:** `portal mock shape (NOT a wire target): SavePackageInput = Omit<Package,'id'|'sold'> & { id?: PackageId } @ portal src/api/client.ts:163, with Package.subscription?: { renews: number[]; upfrontPct: number } @ types.ts:636-640` **Response:** `portal mock: Package (incl. subscription flag). No backend §J shape corresponds — the §J membership lifecycle has no 'subscribable-package-config' endpoint (verified: creditPackages.ts registers only GET + POST purchase @ 118/142; no renews/upfront/subscription-config supply anywhere in routes/); credit_packages catalog config belongs to domain 08/11 (credits/credit-packages), not this domain`
- **Auth:** [staff] (portal offerings config; mock-only — no backend supply) · **Side effects:** mock-only (in-memory db); no money, no membership rows · **Errors:** null
- **Action:** OPEN (deferred to domain 08/11, cross-ref per orchestrator note): this is portal DEMAND but it is (a) an offerings/credit-package CATALOG-config concern, not the membership lifecycle, and (b) a mock-parallel product model incompatible with §J.1. Package.subscription itself carries no owner/invoice linkage; the adjacent mock CreditLot DOES carry invoiceId (types.ts:508) but as purchase provenance, not a membership-lifecycle link. The membership DOMAIN itself has NO portal demand (no ApiClient membership namespace — client.ts verified). ALREADY-ADJUDICATED: this exact incompatibility is DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-20 (DISCREPANCIES.md:361-365, severity drift) — 'portal-backend integration must not implement the mock model'; which model wins remains undecided there too. Resolving whether the backend should ever supply 'subscribable-package config' is a product/domain-08/11 decision. Flag, do not relitigate.
- **Promote:** Do NOT mint a portal-only membership shape from Package.subscription. Whether staff should be able to configure subscription-eligibility on packages at all (renews[]/upfrontPct) is a credit-package-catalog product decision; if kept, it belongs to domain 08/11's credit_packages surface, aligned to backend catalog columns — the §J membership model does not use these fields
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - The portal's Package.subscription models a mock-parallel subscription product (30-day-from-first-use, upfrontPct discount, 1-36mo terms) incompatible with §J.1 (3/6/9/12mo, calendar-month-from-subscribe, no upfront discount); which model wins on any real portal-backend integration is undecided (memberships.md:127; adjudicated as DRIFT-20 in DISCREPANCIES.md:361-365 without a winner)
  - Whether the backend should expose ANY staff endpoint to configure subscription-eligibility on credit packages (renews[]/upfrontPct) — no backend supply exists (verified broad grep of routes/); belongs to domain 08/11 credit-package catalog
  - Boundary: keep membership (owner's active subscription lifecycle) distinct from credit-package catalog config (offerings.savePackage) — the portal conflates them via Package.subscription

### Supply-only routes (backend serves, portal does not call)
| Route | Where | Serves | Portal-relevant? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

Renewals are self-billed via the invoice auto-charge lane, NOT Stripe Billing. | No — real owner-scoped endpoint, no portal demand today; portal will most plausibly never call this owner-side verb, at most read-only visibility of the result; not wire-covered. Owner-scoped read only. | No today — a portal "memberships on this owner/dog" read view would be a bucket-C new build (staff-scoped list, StaffMembershipWire[]); no such demand exists in the mock portal. | `DELETE /memberships/:id` | routes/memberships.ts:410 | Owner cancels an active membership: status flip active->canceled (never a delete), future rolls stop, already-granted credit lots stay usable, no proration/refund of the current month. Does NOT void an already-rolled open invoice. | No — owner-only cancel verb (no staff cancel verb exists — memberships.md:93); portal will not serve it. | `POST /staff/memberships/:id/pause` | routes/staffMemberships.ts:62 | Staff pauses an active membership: stamps paused_at + paused_by_staff_id; the roll scan skips paused rows (paused stays status='active'). Pause is staff-mediated only — no owner pause verb; the owner app routes 'pause' to a pre-filled staff message thread (R10, memberships.md:36). | Yes eventually — the explicitly-parked staff-portal pause verb (API-ready since 2026-07-14; portal UI parked, PARKED-FEATURES §4b @ 122-127); bucket-A reuse of this exact endpoint when built, only prep is promoting the wire shapes. Open q: whether membership shapes get promoted into wire.ts before the parked portal pause/resume UI lands is unrecorded (memberships.md:139). |
| `POST /staff/memberships/:id/resume` | routes/staffMemberships.ts:107 | Staff resumes a paused membership: the clock stopped while paused, so current_period_end and ends_at shift forward by the pause gap in one atomic UPDATE (repo resume), then the route moves the paid month's membership-grant lot expiry to the shifted period end via shiftMembershipGrantExpiry (alumni NULL-expiry lots never match). Owner keeps the paid-but-unused remainder. | Yes eventually — parked staff-portal resume verb (API-ready, portal UI parked, PARKED-FEATURES §4b); bucket-A reuse of this exact endpoint when built, only prep is promoting the wire shapes into wire.ts. Open q: voiding a membership invoice has no staff verb today; membershipRoll flags that if one lands it must re-align the clock like resume/late-settle or ticks catch-up-bill the frozen gap (memberships.md:136). |

### Open questions
- The mock portal has NO ApiClient membership namespace at all (re-verified: client.ts has zero membership/subscribe references) — the entire §J.1 membership lifecycle (subscribe/list/cancel/pause/resume) is portal-demand-less today; all 5 backend routes are SUPPLY-ONLY.
- Staff pause/resume (staffMemberships.ts) are API-ready and explicitly parked for the portal (PARKED-FEATURES.md:122-127, §4b); when built they are bucket-A reuse, but the prerequisite promotion of MembershipWire/MembershipCreateWire/StaffMembershipWire from route-inline into wire.ts (re-verified: wire.ts v1.0.0 has zero membership shapes) is unscheduled and has no drift guard (memberships.md:120-122,139; adjudicated as DRIFT-21 in DISCREPANCIES.md:367-371).
- Boundary honesty (orchestrator note): the portal's only membership-adjacent modeling — Package.subscription (offerings, types.ts:636-640) + CreditLot 'subscription lots' comment (types.ts:511) — is a credit-package CATALOG concern (domain 08/11), not the membership domain, AND a mock-parallel product model incompatible with §J.1 (adjudicated as DRIFT-20, DISCREPANCIES.md:361-365); adjudication of that shape and any backend catalog-config supply is deferred to domain 08/11.
- No staff-scoped read/list-by-owner/dog membership endpoint exists (re-verified via broad grep of all route files); if the portal eventually needs read visibility it is a bucket-C build returning the owner_id-augmented StaffMembershipWire.
- No owner verb swaps the payment method on an existing membership (dead card parks the rolled invoice and freezes the subscription until staff intervene or owner cancels+resubscribes) and no staff verb voids a membership invoice — both flagged edges with no portal or backend supply (memberships.md:136,138).
- Stale schema COMMENT ON TABLE memberships — at schema.sql:1985 in the current working tree (DISCREPANCIES DRIFT-19 and memberships.md drift item 1 both cite 1980, a stale line number from the 2026-07-20 snapshot) — still says 'Stripe subscription-backed' / 'Day School': contradicts §J.1 (self-billed, stripe_subscription_id NULL, daycare too); doc-vs-code drift, already-adjudicated as DRIFT-19 (DISCREPANCIES.md:355-359).

## Offerings: Reservation types, Packages, Retail, Rates <a id="offerings"></a>
_Demand: OfferingsApi @ client.ts:287-294 (resTypes/updateResType/packages/savePackage/retail/setRetailVisibility @ client.ts:288-293) · Supply: rates.ts, staffRates.ts · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: portal-shop-retail.md, credits-packages.md_

Demand outruns supply across this domain. The reservation-type/offering-config catalog (6 portal configs mapping 1:1 onto 6 of the locked 7-value service_category enum) has real supply only for its per-campus PRICE slice (service_rates via GET/POST /staff/rates), plus partial read supply for the group-class slice (group_classes, deliberately outside service_rates) and day-program capacity (day_capacity per-date overrides + a code default) — everything else (schedule windows, offered flags, visibility, pre-visit email, check-in questions) has no backend home, so resTypes/updateResType are OPEN. Packages splits too: GET /credit-packages is a shared authenticated catalog read (active-at-today, single-location, day-program credit packs, no sold/visibility/subscription) so packages() is OPEN, while savePackage() is a clean C (no package-editor endpoint anywhere; DRIFT-11/12/20 adjudicated). Retail has zero backend supply (re-proven by grep), making both retail ops C requiring a new retail_items table (schema_change D; DRIFT-64/65). All 5 rate routes in rates.ts+staffRates.ts are SUPPLY-ONLY — the portal has no dedicated RatesApi — and are the price backbone the future offerings editor consumes, with a portal/backend location-key mismatch ('fay'/'ben' vs 'fayetteville'/'bentonville') noted for any promote work.

### `offerings.resTypes` — read the offering-config catalog
- **Portal:** `resTypes(): Promise<ReservationType[]> @ client.ts:288 (OfferingsApi @ client.ts:287-294; ReservationType @ types.ts:607-622)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:945-948 (returns db.resTypes verbatim)
- **Behavior:** Read the full offering-config catalog: 6 reservation types (= service categories) each with name/service/description/color, weekly schedule (days + labeled windows), per-campus {offered, capacity, priceCents}, priceUnit, portalVisible, preVisitEmail, checkInQuestions[]. Fixtures: rt-dayschool/daycare/boarding/boardtrain/private/groupclass (fixtures.ts:2825-2962).
- **Bucket:** OPEN (+ schema change: D)
- **Backend:** GET /staff/rates @ staffRates.ts:129 · wire: StaffRateWire @ wire.ts:366 (price slice only) · contract: §B Rate Δ 2026-05-20 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1968; staff rate editor amendment 2026-06-20 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:305 · tables: service_rates @ schema.sql:1298 (price); group_classes @ schema.sql:452 (group-class name/capacity/price, read-only supply); day_capacity @ schema.sql:815 (per-date openings, not default capacity); no offering-config table exists
- **Request:** none **Response:** `(price slice only) StaffRateWire[]: { id, category, location, amount_cents, unit, effective_from, effective_to, note, created_by_staff_id }. Full offering-config response shape is UNDEFINED (no supply).`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none · **Errors:** none
- **Action:** OPEN. The per-campus PRICE slice has real supply: GET /staff/rates returns every service_rates row active or scheduled at Chicago-today (a track can yield >1 row: current + scheduled). Group-class slice has partial read supply too: group_classes carries name/weeks/price_per_dog_cents/capacity/description (schema.sql:452-462), read via GET /group-classes @ groupClasses.ts:60 — and its price is deliberately NOT in service_rates (schema.sql:1293-1295 comment: separate pre-existing catalog). Day-program capacity exists only as per-date day_capacity overrides (schema.sql:815-823) + a code default (weekday=3, weekend=0 — lib/availability.ts:40-47), NOT as an editable per-(category,location) default. Everything else (schedule windows, perCampus.offered, portalVisible, preVisitEmail, checkInQuestions, name/description for non-group categories) has no backend table or route. service_category is a LOCKED 7-value enum (schema.sql:84-87) and the 6 portal resTypes map 1:1 onto 6 of its values (evaluation unmodeled) — reservation types are EDITED, never created/deleted; not a CRUD catalog. Genuinely unresolved whether the backend should own offering-config (new tables) or leave non-price config client-hardcoded. Why-not-reuse: no offering-config table/route exists; GET /staff/rates yields price rows only, not offering objects.
  - **Schema change:** D (proposed, OPEN): offering_config keyed (category, location) for offered/default_capacity/portal_visible/pre_visit_email + a check_in_questions child table — ONLY if backend takes ownership; undecided.
  - **Promote:** Align the price sub-object to StaffRateWire/RateWire (category+location+amount_cents+unit); never mint a portal-only price shape. Note key mismatch: portal perCampus keys are 'fay'/'ben' (types.ts:617); backend LocationKey is 'fayetteville'/'bentonville' (schema.ts:17).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Should reservation-type/offering config (schedule, per-campus offered/default-capacity, portalVisible, preVisitEmail, checkInQuestions) become backend-owned tables, or stay client-hardcoded with only price backed by service_rates? service_category is a locked enum — no create/delete of reservation types.
  - Portal ReservationType is a whole-object camelCase model with a per-campus price map ('fay'/'ben' keys); backend models price as N per-(category,location) service_rates rows with 'fayetteville'/'bentonville' slugs — needs decomposition + slug mapping.
  - The group-class reservation type double-homes: its name/capacity/price live in group_classes (intentionally outside service_rates) — which catalog would an offerings editor write?
  - perCampus.capacity for day programs collides with the existing day_capacity per-date override model + code default — is a static default-capacity config even wanted?

### `offerings.updateResType` — patch one existing reservation type
- **Portal:** `updateResType(id: ResTypeId, patch: ResTypePatch): Promise<ReservationType> @ client.ts:289 (ResTypePatch = Partial<Omit<ReservationType,'id'>> @ client.ts:160)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:950-956
- **Behavior:** Patch config for one EXISTING reservation type (service category): any subset of name/schedule/perCampus{offered,capacity,priceCents}/portalVisible/preVisitEmail/checkInQuestions. Mock Object.assigns the patch onto the found row; unknown id -> ApiError('not-found') (mock/index.ts:950-956).
- **Bucket:** OPEN (+ schema change: D)
- **Backend:** POST /staff/rates @ staffRates.ts:155 · wire: StaffRateWire @ wire.ts:366 · contract: §B Rate 2026-06-20 amendment (staff rate editor) @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:305 · tables: service_rates @ schema.sql:1298; group_classes @ schema.sql:452 (group-class price, no write route)
- **Request:** `(price slice, real POST /staff/rates body @ staffRates.ts:63-79) { category: ServiceCategory, location: LocationKey, amount_cents: int, unit: RateUnit, effective_from?: 'YYYY-MM-DD', note?: string }. Non-price patch fields have no target body.` **Response:** `StaffRateWire { id, category, location, amount_cents, unit, effective_from, effective_to, note, created_by_staff_id } (price slice)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Append-only supersede: closes prior open window's effective_to + inserts new row (or same-day void+reinsert); runs under withServiceRateLock advisory lock (staffRates.ts:187); back-dating rejected; no cache wipe — keysToInvalidate () => [] (staffRates.ts:182-184), service_rates read direct from pg. · **Errors:** invalid_payload (back-date; non-per-day unit for day-school/day-care), bad_request, not_found
- **Action:** OPEN. The perCampus.priceCents dimension REUSES POST /staff/rates (B-grade for that slice): effective-dated append-only supersede per (category, location). Caveat: group-class price is NOT in service_rates — its live catalog is group_classes.price_per_dog_cents (schema.sql:456), which has NO write route (groupClasses.ts is read-only, 5 GETs). Everything else in the patch (schedule, capacity, offered, portal_visible, pre_visit_email, check_in_questions, name/description) has NO write endpoint anywhere (verified: no offering/config write in any routes/ file). Why-not-reuse: POST /staff/rates covers price only. Mobile-safety: POST /staff/rates is staff-scoped + Idempotency-Key required, REJECTS non-'per-day' unit for day-school/day-care (staffRates.ts:169) because PAYG bills those categories per booking date straight off amount_cents (staffRates.ts:54-61), and rejects back-dating (staffRates.ts:163) — the append-only model protects past bookings' as-charged history.
  - **Schema change:** D (proposed, OPEN): same offering_config additions as resTypes if backend owns non-price config.
  - **Promote:** Align the price edit to POST /staff/rates: portal should emit category+location+amount_cents+unit(+effective_from), NOT a perCampus priceCents map; drop the whole-object PATCH shape for pricing; map 'fay'/'ben' to backend location slugs.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Same offering-config ownership question as resTypes — the non-price bulk of this patch has no backend home.
  - Portal patches the whole offering as one object; a real integration must fan a price change out to per-(category,location) rate writes and reject unit changes that break PAYG.
  - Group-class price edits would need a group_classes write path (none exists), not POST /staff/rates.

### `offerings.packages` — staff read of purchasable package catalog
- **Portal:** `packages(): Promise<Package[]> @ client.ts:290` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:958-961
- **Behavior:** Staff catalog-management read of purchasable packages across 6 categories (dayschool/daycare/group/boarding/boardtrain/private) with priceCents, unitLabel, derived sold count, isCustom, visibility {mode: all|alumni|select|hidden, dogIds?}, and subscription {renews[], upfrontPct} for DS/DC packs (types.ts:627-641).
- **Bucket:** OPEN
- **Backend:** GET /credit-packages @ creditPackages.ts:118 (owned by domain 08 — cited, not re-enumerated) · wire: CreditPackageWire @ creditPackages.ts:53 (route-inline; not in wire.ts) · contract: §B CreditPackage Δ 2026-05-20 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1929; credits-packages.md R25 @ :79 · tables: credit_packages @ schema.sql:986
- **Request:** none **Response:** `Align to CreditPackageWire + staff additions: { key, location, mode, credits, price_cents, label, is_popular, effective_from, effective_to, sold }`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Errors:** bad_request (missing location)
- **Action:** OPEN (C-leaning). GET /credit-packages exists but is a shared purchase-catalog read — NOT owner-only (requirePrincipal only; 'any authenticated principal; catalog is shared' @ creditPackages.ts:122) — so staff CAN call it, but it still fails the management-view need: requires ?location= (one location per call, packagesQuerySchema @ creditPackages.ts:92), returns only rows active at Chicago-today (no scheduled/history), day-program credit packs only, and CreditPackageWire (creditPackages.ts:53) carries no sold/visibility/subscription/effective-window fields. Why-not-reuse: (1) shape incompatible — credit_packages models key/location/mode/credits/price_cents/label/is_popular/effective windows, sharing nothing with the portal's category/unitLabel/visibility/subscription model (adjudicated: DRIFT-12 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:313); (2) the portal models boarding/private/B&T/group 'packages' that are NOT credit packs — credits exist only for day programs (credits-packages.md:3). Adjudicated adjacent bug: the route header's 'retirement is active=false' comment is stale — retirement is the closed effective window (DRIFT-11 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:307). Proposed C: GET /staff/credit-packages returning active+scheduled rows + derived sold (from credit_ledger purchase lots) for the day-program slice.
  - **Promote:** Align portal Package (day-program slice) to CreditPackageWire — key/location/mode/credits/price_cents/label/is_popular; do NOT mint a parallel portal-only Package shape. sold is DERIVED, never stored (matches R25/R35).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - How to model the portal's non-day-program 'packages' (boarding/private/board-train/group) — credit_packages cannot represent them (credits are day-program only). Separate offering-bundle concept? DRIFT-12.
  - Portal Package.visibility (all/alumni/select/hidden + dogIds) and derived sold have no credit_packages equivalent.

### `offerings.savePackage` — create/update a package's catalog config
- **Portal:** `savePackage(input: SavePackageInput): Promise<Package> @ client.ts:291 (SavePackageInput = Omit<Package,'id'|'sold'> & { id?: PackageId } @ client.ts:163 — omitting id creates a custom pkg; sold is derived)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:963-974 (Object.assign on update, never overwrites sold; allocId('pkg') + sold:0 on create)
- **Behavior:** Create (id omitted -> allocId('pkg') + sold:0) or update (id present -> Object.assign, sold preserved) a package's catalog config: name/category/priceCents/unitLabel/visibility/subscription (mock/index.ts:963-974).
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** none · wire: CreditPackageWire @ creditPackages.ts:53 (route-inline; not in wire.ts) · contract: §J.1 (memberships) @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:940/2132; §B CreditPackage @ :1929; credits-packages.md R25(:79)/R35(:89) · tables: credit_packages @ schema.sql:986
- **Request:** `{ key: string, location: LocationKey, mode: 'school'|'daycare', credits: int>0, price_cents: int>=0, label: string, is_popular?: bool, effective_from?: 'YYYY-MM-DD' } (day-program slice)` **Response:** `CreditPackageWire { key, location, mode, credits, price_cents, label, is_popular }`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Effective-dated supersede (append-only); would need a NEW catalog-cache wipe (creditpackages:{location}:* — R35 notes none exists today because no write path exists). · **Errors:** invalid_payload, bad_request, conflict (window overlap)
- **Action:** BUILD NEW (staff-scoped). Gates: none. Why-can't-reuse: NO package-editor endpoint exists anywhere — verified by broad grep of all routes/ (creditPackages.ts has only GET /credit-packages @ :118 and POST /credit-packages/:key/purchase @ :142, an owner purchase); credits-packages.md R35 (:89) 'no package-editor endpoint ... no write-path invalidation'; catalog is seed-only (scripts/seed-dev.ts:505-517); DRIFT-12 (@ DISCREPANCIES.md:313) confirms. Note stale route-header comment claiming an active flag is adjudicated DRIFT-11 (@ :307) — do not implement an active flag.

  - DRIFT-20 (@ DISCREPANCIES.md:361): the portal subscription model (30-day cycle from FIRST CREDIT USE, staff-authored terms 1-36mo, upfrontPct discount) is INCOMPATIBLE with backend §J.1 memberships (terms exactly 3/6/9/12mo, calendar-month periods from subscribe instant, no upfront discount). A real editor must NOT implement the mock model.

  - Mock 'custom' creation (id omitted, client-minted 'pkg-' id) is a demand signal, not the target contract.

### `offerings.retail` — read the retail-goods catalog

- **Auth:** [staff] · **Errors:** network

Requires a new retail_items table (schema_change D). Why-can't-reuse: ZERO retail/shop/offering code exists in the backend — independently re-verified this session: case-insensitive grep for retail|offering over fetchd_backend/src returns nothing; portal-shop-retail.md R1 (:23) records the same; wire coverage 'Zero' (:100); adjudicated as DRIFT-64 (@ DISCREPANCIES.md:625) and DRIFT-65 (@ :631). Planned mapping (spec only) R15 (:37): GET /offerings/retail.

Purely additive; no existing table covers retail goods.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Where do 'visible' items actually surface — no client shop exists in mobile or anywhere (portal-shop-retail.md open Qs).
  - How a retail SALE becomes a kind:'retail' invoice — no checkout/POS/invoice-creation API exists in any repo; retail invoice lines have no retailItemId FK (R13).
  - Sales tax on retail goods vs services — the spec's #1 deferred decision.
  - Inventory semantics: stock is a dead field today (DRIFT-65) — does a sale decrement, does a 'Returned item' refund restock, low-stock/auto-hide?

The append-only rows ARE the history. | Rate change-history/audit view. The portal has no price-history UI today; a future offerings pricing-history panel would consume it. Idempotency-Key required; back-dating rejected (staffRates.ts:163); day-school/day-care forced to per-day (staffRates.ts:169 — PAYG bills off amount_cents). | The price-WRITE backbone for updateResType's perCampus.priceCents (cross-ref the updateResType entry). Not a dedicated portal op today; the portal embeds price editing inside the offering patch. |
| POST /staff/rates/:id/void | staffRates.ts:209 | Staff soft-void one rate entry (a scheduled future change or the current rate); reopens its predecessor so no pricing gap opens (no-trap guarantee); returns the voided row. Not consumed by any portal op today. |

### Open questions
- Should reservation-type / offering config (schedule, per-campus offered/default-capacity, portalVisible, preVisitEmail, checkInQuestions, name/description) become backend-owned tables, or stay client-hardcoded with only price backed by service_rates? service_category is a LOCKED 7-value enum (schema.sql:84-87) and the 6 portal resTypes map 1:1 onto 6 of them (evaluation not modeled) — reservation types are edited, never created/deleted, so this is not a CRUD catalog. Nuance verified this session: the group-class slice already has read supply in group_classes (name/capacity/price_per_dog_cents, deliberately outside service_rates), and day-program capacity already exists as per-date day_capacity overrides + a code default — an offerings editor must reconcile with both, not just service_rates.
- How does the portal's broad 'Package' concept map to backend? credit_packages (schema.sql:986) model ONLY day-school/day-care credit packs; the portal's boarding/private/board-train/group 'packages' and its visibility (all/alumni/select/hidden+dogIds) + derived sold have no backend home (DRIFT-12 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:313).
- The portal package SUBSCRIPTION model (30-day cycle from first credit use, terms 1-36mo, upfrontPct) is incompatible with backend §J.1 memberships (3/6/9/12mo, calendar-month, no upfront discount) — DRIFT-20 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:361; a real editor must not implement the mock model.
- The entire Retail/Shop domain is unbuilt on the backend (no table, no route, no wire shape — portal-shop-retail.md R1 @ :23, DRIFT-64/65 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:625/631): the whole pipeline (retail sale -> kind:'retail' invoice, sales tax, inventory/stock, client-facing surface, delete/archive, Gingr import) is undecided.
- Portal has no dedicated RatesApi — all 5 rate routes are SUPPLY-ONLY today; confirm the future offerings price editor is where they get consumed (updateResType price slice -> POST /staff/rates). Location-key mapping also unresolved: portal perCampus uses 'fay'/'ben', backend LocationKey is 'fayetteville'/'bentonville' (schema.ts:17).

## Booking rules, Flags, Groups, Cancel-window settings <a id="rules"></a>
_Demand: RulesApi/FlagsApi/GroupsApi @ client.ts:297-300, 304-307, 311-314 (input types 165/167/169; ApiClient 327-346) · Supply: staffCancelWindow.ts · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: bookings-scheduling.md (rules), dogs-evaluations.md (flags context), portal-staff-operations.md_

All 12 portal ops in this domain (`rules.*`, `flags.*`, `groups.*`) are bucket C: nothing in the backend serves any of them — proven by full CREATE TABLE enumeration of schema.sql (no booking_rules / flag-catalog / dog-flag-join / groups / group_members tables) and a broad grep + full route enumeration of every file under src/routes/. The only supply in this domain is the two staffCancelWindow.ts routes, and both are SUPPLY-ONLY: the portal ApiClient (client.ts:327-346) has no cancel-window namespace, so backend supply exists with only latent demand. Headline mismatches: the portal models a configurable 15-condition block/approve/warn rules engine that collides with the backend's four unbypassable BEFORE-INSERT gate triggers (schema.sql:1673-1839) plus two hardcoded divert reasons (bookingApprovalDivert.ts:48); flags and groups have no backend model at all; and cancel-window is the one existing staff-tunable-setting precedent that the future rules/flags/groups mutations should copy.

### `rules.list` — list booking-rule catalog (7 seeds + custom)
- **Portal:** `list(): Promise<BookingRule[]> @ client.ts:297` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:990-1001 (list 991-994, add 996-1001), seed shapes src/api/mock/fixtures.ts:3166-3238; type src/api/types.ts:682-692, RuleCondition types.ts:654-669
- **Behavior:** Lists the booking-rule catalog: 7 non-deletable seeds (vax/eval/waiver/balance/prereq/intake/notice; fixtures.ts:3166-3238, 'notice' seeded OFF with n:24) plus any custom rules, each with cond/service-scope/mode(block|approve|warn)/on-off/optional n. The portal renders this as a configurable gate engine over a 15-value RuleCondition union (types.ts:654-669).
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** contract: DATA-CONTRACT.md:805 (3-month staleness 'new booking rule'); no rules-engine surface in §C
- **Response:** `BookingRuleWire[] where BookingRuleWire = { id, condition, name, description, service, mode: 'block'|'approve'|'warn', enabled, custom, threshold_n? } (TARGET snake_case; mock fields are cond/desc/svc/on/n)`
- **Auth:** [staff]
- **Action:** C: GET /staff/booking-rules [staff] -> 200 BookingRuleWire[]. Why not reuse: no rules table or rules endpoint exists anywhere in the backend (verified by full CREATE TABLE enumeration of schema.sql and broad grep of all src/routes/*.ts); the four gates are unbypassable BEFORE-INSERT triggers (schema.sql:1673-1839: assert_payment_guarantee/assert_vaccines_current/assert_agreements_signed/assert_dog_evaluation_passed) and the divert logic is TWO hardcoded reasons (bookingApprovalDivert.ts:48 'reevaluation-stale'|'not-spayed-neutered'), neither queryable nor configurable. A 'list' would have to synthesize the fixed gates as read-only pseudo-rules — the whole configurable-engine concept is unbuilt and partly conflicts with locked invariants. Already-adjudicated drift: DISCREPANCIES.md:423 and :976 record the portal BookingRule engine as unwired parallel-universe spec (D7 bookings-scheduling.md:233) — this is a known, ledgered divergence, not a newly discovered gap.
  - schema change: NEW booking_rules table (id, condition, name, description, service, mode, enabled, custom, threshold_n, updated_at, updated_by_staff_id) if a configurable engine is actually adopted — but see open_questions: this may relitigate locked gate triggers.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open:_ Does Fetch'd intend to build a configurable block/approve/warn rules engine at all, or is the portal's 15-condition engine a wishlist superseded by the backend's fixed gates + fixed divert? Explicitly unresolved (DISCREPANCIES.md:976; D7 bookings-scheduling.md:233).
  - _Open:_ Seed conditions map onto backend mechanisms but only partially: vax->assert_vaccines_current, eval->assert_dog_evaluation_passed, waiver->assert_agreements_signed, prereq->group-class prereq system; balance/intake/notice have NO backend equivalent.

### `rules.add` — create a custom booking rule
- **Portal:** `add(input: AddBookingRuleInput): Promise<BookingRule> @ client.ts:298 (AddBookingRuleInput = Omit<BookingRule,'id'|'custom'> @ client.ts:165)` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:996-1001 (client-minted id allocId('rule'), custom:true)
- **Behavior:** Creates a custom booking rule (custom=true server-set); duplicates are allowed (BK-1, mock index.ts:998 comment). Body omits id/custom.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Request:** `{ condition, name, description, service, mode: 'block'|'approve'|'warn', enabled, threshold_n? }` **Response:** `BookingRuleWire (custom: true)`
- **Auth:** [staff] [idempotent] · **Errors:** validation (missing name/condition/service/mode)
- **Action:** C: POST /staff/booking-rules [staff] { condition, name, description, service, mode, enabled, threshold_n? } (Idempotency-Key required, per withMutation convention) -> 201 BookingRuleWire with custom:true. Why not reuse: nothing writes booking rules; the backend has no rules store, only hardcoded trigger gates (broad routes grep confirms no rules route in any file). New table + route required.
  - schema change: Same NEW booking_rules table as rules.list (custom rows only distinguished by custom=true).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open:_ A custom rule whose condition duplicates a locked gate (e.g. a second 'vax' rule set to mode:'warn') has no coherent backend meaning — the gate cannot be softened. Needs a product ruling on which conditions are user-authorable vs read-only.

### `rules.toggle` — enable/disable a rule
- **Portal:** `toggle(id: RuleId): Promise<BookingRule> @ client.ts:299` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:1003-1009 (rule.on = !rule.on)
- **Behavior:** Flips a rule's on/off flag (enables/disables the gate). Applies to both seeds and custom rules.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Request:** `{ enabled: boolean } (prefer explicit idempotent set over a toggle verb)` **Response:** `BookingRuleWire`
- **Auth:** [staff] [idempotent] · **Side effects:** Disabling a rule changes future booking-gate behavior — but backend gates are triggers that cannot be disabled per-row today. · **Errors:** not_found (unknown rule id)
- **Action:** C: PATCH /staff/booking-rules/:id [staff] { enabled: boolean } -> 200 BookingRuleWire. Proposed as an explicit-set PATCH rather than the mock's stateful toggle so it is idempotent. Why not reuse: no rules row exists to toggle; and disabling the vaccine/eval/agreement gates would relitigate locked health/legal invariants (BEFORE-INSERT triggers, schema.sql:1673-1839), so a real toggle can only apply to genuinely tunable conditions.
  - schema change: booking_rules.enabled column toggled; no new table beyond rules.list's.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open:_ Toggling OFF a locked gate (vaccine 2026-05-19 health rule, eval, agreement) contradicts a dated locked amendment — conflict, not a build item. Which conditions are actually toggleable is undecided.

  - schema change: NEW groups (id, name, kind, rule) + group_members (group_id, member_id) tables. 'dog'|'owner' kind mirrors the mock; smart 'alumni' rows store rule='alumni' and derive members from dog_completed_programs (alumni = all 5 curriculum programs completed).
  - promote: The smart 'alumni' rule must derive from the backend's authoritative alumni source (dog_completed_programs, BL dogs-evaluations.md:20 'Alumni status is derived from these rows — no stored flag') — never a stored dog.alumni boolean (the mock's shape); align member derivation, not a parallel flag.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open:_ No backend model for dog/owner groups at all; announcement audience-targeting by group is also unbuilt (announcements target only category+location). Whether groups become a first-class entity or just an announcement-audience helper is undecided.

### Supply-only routes (backend serves, portal does not call)

| Route | Where | Serves | Portal-relevant? |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET /staff/cancel-window | staffCancelWindow.ts:85 | SUPPLY-ONLY. GET /staff/cancel-window [staff] lists every service_category's current free-cancel policy (always 7 rows per the schema seed: hours_before, updated_at, updated_by_staff_id). updated_by_staff_id NULL = still the seeded 48h default. Owner principals get 403. This is the read half of the one existing staff-tunable-setting precedent (§I). | SUPPLY-ONLY: no portal demand op today (client.ts has no cancel-window namespace). LATENT demand — the portal will eventually need a cancel-window editor (BL bookings-scheduling.md:193 marks it MISSING); when built it should CONSUME this endpoint. This route is also the cited pattern precedent for the (unbuilt) rules/flags/groups staff-tunable surfaces. |
| PATCH /staff/cancel-window/:category | staffCancelWindow.ts:104 | SUPPLY-ONLY. PATCH /staff/cancel-window/:category [staff] updates one category's hours_before (int 1..720), stamping updated_at=now() and updated_by_staff_id (cancelWindowSettingsRepository.updateHoursFor @ cancelWindowSettingsRepository.ts:94-102). Requires Idempotency-Key. Affects FUTURE bookings only — existing rows keep their stamped cancel_deadline_at. Owner principals 403; a category outside the service_category enum fails path validation; an enum-valid category with no seeded row is 404. | SUPPLY-ONLY: no portal demand op today. LATENT demand — this is the mutation the future portal cancel-window editor would call. It is the canonical staff-tunable-PATCH template (parseOrThrow + requireStaff + requireIdempotencyKey + withMutation) the proposed rules/flags/groups mutations should copy. |

Supply-only detail (not lost from the audit):
- **GET /staff/cancel-window** — wire: CancelWindowSettingWire[] = { category: service_category, hours_before, updated_at, updated_by_staff_id: string|null } (interface @ staffCancelWindow.ts:55-60, toWire @ 62-74) · contract: DATA-CONTRACT.md:2454-2478 (§I cancel window, locked 2026-05-19 / staff-tunable 2026-05-26); endpoint listed at DATA-CONTRACT.md:2067 · tables: cancel_window_settings @ schema.sql:844 (PK category, CHECK hours_before > 0 @ :846; seeded 48h flat across 7 categories @ :851-858) · auth: [staff] · errors: 403 (owner principal via requireStaff, staffCancelWindow.ts:90) · mock: No portal op consumes this; the portal mock has NO CancelWindowApi (ApiClient interface @ client.ts:327-346 has no such namespace). The mock instead encodes advance-notice as the booking-rules 'notice' condition (fixtures.ts:3227-3237), which is booking-ahead lead, NOT cancellation lead. · promote: CancelWindowSettingWire is route-local, not in wire.ts (confirmed; also recorded at BL bookings-scheduling.md:212) — when the portal builds its cancel-window editor, promote the shape into wire.ts so both repos share it.
  - _Open:_ When the portal builds a cancel-window UI it must map the mock's display-cased ServiceCategory ('Day School') to the backend service_category enum ('day-school'); and reconcile the mock's booking-rules 'notice' concept (advance-booking hours) with cancellation-window hours — they are different axes.
- **PATCH /staff/cancel-window/:category** — request: { hours_before: number } (int, min 1, max 720; .strict) @ staffCancelWindow.ts:44-53; :category path validated against the service_category enum @ 40-42 · response: CancelWindowSettingWire (updated row) · contract: DATA-CONTRACT.md:2454-2478 (§I staff-tunable amendment 2026-05-26); DATA-CONTRACT.md:2067 · tables: cancel_window_settings @ schema.sql:844 · auth: [staff] [idempotent] · errors: 403 (owner, requireStaff @ 109); validation (non-enum category via path schema @ 40-42,110; hours_before out of 1..720 @ 44-53,111); 404 not_found (enum-valid category missing its seeded row, staffCancelWindow.ts:137-140) · side effects: Changes future bookings' stamped cancel_deadline_at; no cache invalidation (keysToInvalidate returns [], staffCancelWindow.ts:125); withMutation idempotency (staffCancelWindow.ts:112,114-119).

### Open questions
- Rules engine architecture conflict (dominant open question): the portal models a configurable block/approve/warn booking-rules engine with 15 conditions (RuleCondition @ portal types.ts:654-669); the backend has NO rules store and instead enforces four unbypassable BEFORE-INSERT gate triggers (payment/vaccine/agreement/eval, schema.sql:1673-1839) plus TWO hardcoded divert reasons (reevaluation-stale, not-spayed-neutered, bookingApprovalDivert.ts:48). Whether Fetch'd will build a configurable engine, and whether toggling/mode-changing the locked gates is even permissible, is explicitly unresolved (DISCREPANCIES.md:976; D7 bookings-scheduling.md:233). All four rules.* entries are bucket C but this conflict must be adjudicated before any build — a toggle that softens the vaccine gate would relitigate a locked 2026-05-19 health rule.
- Flags vs dog_descriptors: staff dog-flags (colored staff-applied pills on dogs) and dog_descriptors (owner-selected intake traits stored on requests) overlap structurally but not semantically; dog_descriptors itself has no read endpoint yet (DATA-CONTRACT.md:1858) and the intake_field_settings/dog_descriptors staff editors are unbuilt (BL dogs-evaluations.md:224 Q6). Decide whether to unify into one staff-label catalog or build dog_flags separately (this domain proposes separate + a new dog_flags join for per-dog application).
- Flag applications have no per-dog storage: the only per-dog flag column is alumni_attendance_flagged_at (single-purpose, schema.sql:278, with its own dedicated clear verb staffAlumni.ts:211); a generic dog_flags(dog_id, flag_id) join table is needed, and whether flag_ids should be promoted onto the shared Dog wire (cross-ref domain 02; BL dogs-evaluations.md:227 Q9) or a dedicated resource is open.
- Groups are entirely unmodeled backend-side: no groups/group_members tables, and announcements target only category+location (announcements.ts:31-33,41-49; target_location @ schema.sql:1554) — no audience-by-group. The smart 'alumni' group must derive from dog_completed_programs (BL dogs-evaluations.md:20), never a stored dog.alumni boolean (the mock's types.ts:89). Whether groups are a first-class entity or an announcement-audience helper is undecided.
- Cancel-window has backend supply (GET/PATCH /staff/cancel-window) but NO portal demand op in client.ts today; both routes are SUPPLY-ONLY with latent demand (BL bookings-scheduling.md:193 marks the portal UI MISSING). The mock instead expresses advance-notice as the booking-rules 'notice' condition (a different axis from cancellation lead). A future portal cancel-window editor must also translate display-cased ServiceCategory to the service_category enum, and CancelWindowSettingWire should be promoted from route-local into wire.ts when consumed.

---

## Staff roster, Shifts, Clock in/out, Pay periods, Time off <a id="staff"></a>
_Demand: StaffApi @ src/api/client.ts:193-201 (staff.list/shifts/clockIn/clockOut/payPeriods/timeOff/requestTimeOff, client.ts:194-200; StaffMember types.ts:212-220, Shift :222-230, PayPeriod :232-238, TimeOffRequest :240-245) · Supply: none owned — EXPLORE: grep src/routes/ + schema.sql for staff, shift, clock, pay, time_off, payroll; wire.ts for StaffRole/staff shapes · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: portal-staff-operations.md_

This domain is backend-greenfield: all 7 portal staff ops have zero backend supply — no routes, no wire shapes, and DATA-CONTRACT.md is silent on staff HR; only the base `staff` table (schema.sql:233-244), the `staff_role` enum (:135), and `locations` (:172-188) exist server-side. Every op correctly sits in bucket C: four surfaces (shifts, pay-periods, time-off, and the derived clock punches) require NEW tables, while the base roster reuses the existing `staff` table with only an ADDITIVE availability column. Headline mismatches are role-vocabulary drift (portal permissions `owner`|`manager`|`trainer` vs backend staff_role `owner-shanthi`|`trainer`|`office` — no `manager`, DRIFT-56/DRIFT-43), campus-as-display vs `locations.slug` FK (DRIFT-60), no per-staff hourly rate anywhere server-side (analytics fabricates it from role keywords, NOTE-45/NOTE-49), and zero visibility enforcement exposing everyone's punches/pay to any viewer (DRIFT-57). Contract-first: new wire shapes + routes + tables land in fetchd_backend before the portal implements src/api/http; nothing ripples to mobile (no surface).

### `staff.list` — return the full staff roster
- **Portal:** `list(): Promise<StaffMember[]> @ src/api/client.ts:194 (StaffApi @ client.ts:193-201; StaffMember @ src/api/types.ts:212-220)` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:252-255 (returns db.staff); fixtures src/api/mock/fixtures.ts:370-422; mock signed-in account CURRENT_STAFF='staff-shanthi' @ src/api/mock/index.ts:60; the me-view pin ME_STAFF_ID='staff-shanthi' lives at src/features/staff/lib/me.ts:9 (NOT in mock/index.ts)
- **Behavior:** Returns the full staff roster (everyone). Portal RosterCard/StaffScreen fold each member's today-shift punches into a derived status. StaffMember carries id, name, role (free display string), campus ('Fayetteville'|'Bentonville', types.ts:14), isTrainer, availability.weekdays?, permissions ('owner'|'manager'|'trainer', types.ts:219).
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** tables: staff @ .claude/backend/schema.sql:233-244 (id, supabase_uid, name, role staff_role, location→locations.slug, image_path, active, created_at, expired_at); staff_role enum @ schema.sql:135; locations @ schema.sql:172-188 (slug PK + display_name, seeds 'fayetteville'/'bentonville' @ 186-188)
- **Request:** (none) **Response:** `StaffMemberWire[]: { id: string(uuid), name: string, role: 'owner-shanthi'|'trainer'|'office', location: string(locations.slug), image_path: string|null, active: boolean, is_trainer: boolean(derived role==='trainer'), availability: { weekdays: int[] }|null }`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (pure read). Feeds portal-side dashboard 'On shift' and analytics Time clock read models — no backend side effects. · **Errors:** none (empty list valid); 401 if unauthenticated
- **Action:** BUILD NEW: GET /staff -> StaffMemberWire[], preHandler requireStaff(principal, 'read the staff roster') — matches the doc-only R23 mapping 'staff.list → GET /staff' (docs/backend-logic.md:1146). Why not reuse: NO endpoint returns a staff list — staffRepository (src/db/repositories/staffRepository.ts:16-79) exposes only findNamesByIds (:24), resolveTrainerNames (:43), findParticipantsByIds (:65); GET /me (routes/me.ts:162) returns only the single signed-in principal. The staff table exists but is unexposed. ALIGN response to StaffRole (owner-shanthi|trainer|office) + location slug — the portal's permissions:'owner'|'manager'|'trainer' and campus:'Fayetteville'|'Bentonville' must reconcile to the backend enum+slug (DRIFT-56/DRIFT-43/D4), and isTrainer becomes derived (role==='trainer').
  - **Schema change:** No change for base roster (staff table already covers id/name/role/location/image_path/active). ADDITIVE only for availability: either staff.availability jsonb (weekday int[]) or a staff_availability(staff_id uuid, weekday smallint) child table. availability has no column today (re-verified: grep of schema.sql for availability/weekday = zero hits).
  - **Promote:** StaffMemberWire — reuse existing StaffRole (wire.ts:74) and extend the ThreadParticipantWire { id,name,role,image_path } pattern (wire.ts:253-258) into a full roster shape; do NOT mint the portal's parallel StaffMember (campus/permissions/isTrainer soup, types.ts:212-220).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Role vocabulary reconciliation: portal permissions 'owner'|'manager'|'trainer' (types.ts:219) vs backend staff_role 'owner-shanthi'|'trainer'|'office' (schema.sql:135 / wire.ts:74) — no 'manager' exists. Adjudicated as DRIFT-56 (and DRIFT-43, three vocabularies incl. mobile) in DISCREPANCIES.md:577/499; D4 @ portal-staff-operations.md:138. Identity is owned by auth-accounts.md; blocks any permission gate. (Separately, DRIFT-45 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:511 records roster CONTENT drift — who exists with what role across the three repos — the seed data itself disagrees.)
  - campus dimension: portal uses display 'Fayetteville'|'Bentonville'; backend staff.location is a locations.slug FK. Adjudicated as DRIFT-60 (campus exists only client-side). Note locations.display_name already exists server-side (schema.sql:186-188 seeds 'Fayetteville, AR'/'Bentonville, AR') — a slug↔display source exists, though its strings differ from the portal's bare campus names.
  - Roster shows everyone's data unconditionally in the mock; should it be manager/owner-gated once auth exists? Documented matrix marks all reads open to all roles (docs/backend-logic.md:1077 via portal-staff-operations.md:156); zero enforcement adjudicated as DRIFT-57.

### `staff.shifts` — list scheduled work blocks with punches
- **Portal:** `shifts(params?: ShiftListParams): Promise<Shift[]> @ src/api/client.ts:195 (ShiftListParams {staffId?,from?,to?} @ client.ts:171-175; Shift @ types.ts:222-230)` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:257-267 (filter staffId + from/to, sort date then staffId)

- **Request:** `query: { staff_id?: string(uuid), from?: string(YYYY-MM-DD), to?: string(YYYY-MM-DD) }` **Response:** `ShiftWire[]: { id: string(uuid), staff_id: string(uuid), work_date: string(YYYY-MM-DD), scheduled_start: string(timestamptz), scheduled_end: string(timestamptz), clocked_in_at: string(timestamptz)|null, clocked_out_at: string(timestamptz)|null }`

Why not reuse: NO shifts table, route, or wire shape exists anywhere in the backend (re-verified across all src/routes/*.ts — the only 'shift' hits are membership period gap-shifting in staffMemberships.ts — plus schema.sql, wire.ts, DATA-CONTRACT.md); schedules live only as portal fixtures (buildShiftsFixture @ fixtures.ts:2656-2752). Contract-first: ShiftWire + staff_shifts table must land in fetchd_backend before the portal implements src/api/http.
  - **Schema change:** D (NEW): CREATE TABLE staff_shifts ( id uuid PK DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), staff_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES staff(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT, work_date date NOT NULL, scheduled_start timestamptz NOT NULL, scheduled_end timestamptz NOT NULL, clocked_in_at timestamptz, clocked_out_at timestamptz, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() ). Index (staff_id, work_date); UNIQUE(staff_id, work_date) only if one-shift-per-day (portal-staff-operations.md R5 @ :27 — implicit, unenforced) is confirmed — see open question on multi-shift.
  - **Promote:** ShiftWire — new shape in wire.ts. Punches modeled as absolute timestamptz per DATA-CONTRACT R5 (DATA-CONTRACT.md:27, 'Absolute timestamptz on the wire'); the portal's ClockTime '8:30 AM' display string is derived at the edge, NOT carried on the wire.

  - No API creates/edits/deletes Shift rows anywhere (schedules are fixtures only) — who authors the schedule, and does StaffMember.availability.weekdays constrain it? (portal-staff-operations.md open question 5 @ :151)

  - Wire time representation: timestamptz (DATA-CONTRACT.md:27 R5) vs portal ClockTime display strings — confirm display conversion lives at the edge (:155).

### `staff.clockIn` — punch in today's shift
- **Portal:** `clockIn(staffId: StaffId): Promise<Shift> @ src/api/client.ts:196` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:269-278 (find shift where staffId+date===todayISO; guard clockedInAt; set clockTimeFromAt(nowStamp()))
- **Behavior:** Punches in for the caller's shift scheduled today: finds today's shift for staffId, sets clockedInAt to Chicago wall-time; errors if no shift today (portal R1) or already clocked in (R2). Returns the mutated Shift.

No backend notifications/ledger/Stripe touch this domain. · **Errors:** 404 not_found (no shift scheduled today); 409 invalid_transition (already clocked in). Mock codes: ApiError('not-found') @ index.ts:272, ApiError('invalid-transition') @ index.ts:274.

Aligns to R23's documented POST /staff/:id/clock-in (docs/backend-logic.md:1148) but staff_id MUST be principal-derived (never path/body-supplied) so staff can't punch in others — the mock has no such guard: clockIn takes any staffId (index.ts:269) and the UI just passes ME_STAFF_ID (src/features/staff/lib/me.ts:9; MeCard.tsx:88). Why not reuse: no clock/shift surface exists at all (re-verified).
  - **Schema change:** D: uses staff_shifts.clocked_in_at (see staff.shifts schema_change; no additional table).
  - **Promote:** ShiftWire (shared with staff.shifts).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Own-timesheet enforcement: mock lets any caller clockIn any staffId (no auth, DRIFT-57); target must bind staff_id to the principal.
  - Punch corrections (missed clock-out / forgot to clock in) have no manager fix-up path — is the never-clocked-out fallback acceptable for pay (portal R6 vs R15 formulas — their contradiction is adjudicated as BUG-23, CONFIRMED 2026-07-21)?

  - **Schema change:** D (NEW): CREATE TABLE staff_pay_periods ( id uuid PK DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), staff_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES staff(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT, period_start date NOT NULL, period_end date NOT NULL, hours numeric(6,2) NOT NULL, gross_cents integer NOT NULL, status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('paid','pending')) ). ALTERNATIVELY a derived VIEW over staff_shifts once the boundary policy + a rate source exist (production intent, R11 / docs/backend-logic.md:1018).
  - **Promote:** PayPeriodWire — new shape. Replace the portal's opaque `period` string with structured period_start/period_end (kills the NOTE-42/D6 semi-monthly-vs-rolling ambiguity) and add a server id (kills the id-less (staffId,period) key, NOTE-44/D8).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Pay-period boundary policy (semi-monthly vs biweekly, whose calendar) and who/what flips pending→paid are undefined everywhere; only 'aggregated from shifts in production' exists as intent (docs/backend-logic.md:1018). Shape ambiguity adjudicated as NOTE-42.
  - Gross-pay derivation: is gross_cents = hours × a real per-staff rate? Where do rates live (new staff.hourly_rate_cents column, an HR system)? No rate field exists anywhere (D9/NOTE-45); analytics' whole money model is placeholder policy (NOTE-49).
  - Should pay analytics (rates, labor cost) be manager/owner-gated once auth exists? Zero enforcement today is adjudicated DRIFT-57.

### `staff.timeOff` — list a staff member's PTO requests
- **Portal:** `timeOff(staffId: StaffId): Promise<TimeOffRequest[]> @ src/api/client.ts:199 (TimeOffRequest @ types.ts:240-245)` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:295-298 (filter db.timeOff by staffId); Approved/Denied seed-only buildTimeOffFixture @ fixtures.ts:2797-2819
- **Behavior:** Lists a staff member's PTO requests filtered by staffId. TimeOffRequest = staffId, range (display string 'Jul 3 – Jul 6'), type (free string, UI composes 'Vacation · 4 days'), status ('Approved'|'Pending'|'Denied'). No id. Approved/Denied come only from fixtures — no approve/deny API exists.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** tables: MISSING — no time-off/PTO table (re-verified: grep schema.sql time_off = zero hits).
- **Request:** `path/query: staff_id (target principal-scoped)` **Response:** `TimeOffRequestWire[]: { id: string(uuid), staff_id: string(uuid), start_date: string(YYYY-MM-DD), end_date: string(YYYY-MM-DD), type: 'vacation'|'personal'|'sick', status: 'approved'|'pending'|'denied', created_at: string(timestamptz) }`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** none (pure read) · **Errors:** none (empty list valid); 401 unauthenticated
- **Action:** BUILD NEW: GET /staff/time-off?staff_id -> TimeOffRequestWire[], preHandler requireStaff (doc-only R23 maps GET /staff/:id/time-off @ docs/backend-logic.md:1151). Why not reuse: no time-off table/route/wire shape exists (re-verified). ALIGN to structured range + enum type rather than the mock's flat display strings.
  - **Schema change:** D (NEW): CREATE TABLE staff_time_off ( id uuid PK DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), staff_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES staff(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT, start_date date NOT NULL, end_date date NOT NULL, type text NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('vacation','personal','sick')), status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('approved','pending','denied')), created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() ).
  - **Promote:** TimeOffRequestWire — new shape. Replace the portal's flat `range` string with structured start_date/end_date and the free `type` string with an enum (the count is derived, not encoded into the string); add a server id (kills id-less (staffId,range) key + duplicate-row collisions, NOTE-44/D8).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Time-off approve/deny has NO API, no designated approver role, no notification path, and no revoke — Approved/Denied exist only in fixtures (the modal even promises 'Your request goes to a manager for approval', TimeOffRequestModal.tsx:79); a full workflow must be designed alongside this read.
  - Does approved time off block shift scheduling / interact with the shift list?
  - PTO balance ('46 hrs available') is hardcoded (TimeOffCard.tsx:30) with no source — accrual model undefined (portal-staff-operations.md open question 4).

### `staff.requestTimeOff` — create a PTO request
- **Portal:** `requestTimeOff(input: TimeOffInput): Promise<TimeOffRequest> @ src/api/client.ts:200 (TimeOffInput {staffId, range:string, type:string} @ client.ts:177-181)` · mock logic: src/api/mock/index.ts:300-315 (guard staff exists + range/type non-blank; push status 'Pending', no dedupe); UI composition TimeOffRequestModal.tsx:15-44 (ME_STAFF_ID passed as staffId @ :42)
- **Behavior:** Creates a PTO request: validates staffId exists and range+type non-blank, pushes {staffId, range, type, status:'Pending'}. No dedupe — identical resubmissions create duplicate rows. The UI pre-formats range as 'formatDay(from) – formatDay(to)' and encodes the day count into type ('Vacation · 4 days'); types offered Vacation|Personal|Sick (TimeOffRequestModal.tsx:15).
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** tables: MISSING — depends on proposed staff_time_off table (see staff.timeOff).
- **Request:** `body: { start_date: string(YYYY-MM-DD), end_date: string(YYYY-MM-DD), type: 'vacation'|'personal'|'sick' } — staff_id derived from principal; NOT the mock's flat {range,type} strings` **Response:** `TimeOffRequestWire (the created row, status 'pending', server-allocated id + created_at)`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Side effects:** Inserts a pending row; portal invalidates only staffKeys.timeOff(staffId) (verified: useTimeOff.ts:14-21). No approval notification exists (open question). · **Errors:** 404 not_found (unknown staff — but target derives staff from principal, so effectively N/A); 400/422 validation (end_date < start_date, missing/invalid type). Mock: ApiError('not-found') @ index.ts:303, ApiError('validation') @ index.ts:305.
- **Action:** BUILD NEW: POST /staff/time-off, body { start_date, end_date, type } for principal.staffId, insert status 'pending' -> TimeOffRequestWire (doc-only R23 maps POST /staff/:id/time-off @ docs/backend-logic.md:1152). Server should decide dedupe policy (mock has none — duplicate rows possible, NOTE-44). Why not reuse: no time-off surface exists (re-verified). ALIGN request body to structured dates + enum type rather than the mock's flat {range,type} display strings.
  - **Schema change:** D: uses staff_time_off (see staff.timeOff; no additional table).
  - **Promote:** TimeOffRequestWire (shared with staff.timeOff). Request body carries structured dates + enum type; server derives the day count and status defaults to 'pending'.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - Dedupe policy: mock creates duplicate rows on identical resubmission (no id, colliding composite React keys) — adjudicated NOTE-44/D8; the backend needs server ids + a dedupe/idempotency decision.
  - staff_id in TimeOffInput is body-supplied in the mock (any staff for any staff — UI happens to pass ME_STAFF_ID, me.ts:9); target must bind to the principal for own-timesheet requests.
  - Approval routing + requester notification are unspecified (modal copy promises manager approval, TimeOffRequestModal.tsx:79 — nothing implements it).

### Open questions
- Whole domain is backend-greenfield: shifts, clock/punch, pay-periods, and time-off have zero supply — no routes (re-verified by enumerating every /staff/* path string across src/routes/: bookings, cancel-window, credit-expiry-settings, dogs(+alumni/session-count), invoices, memberships pause/resume, rates(+history/void — SERVICE pricing, not pay), reports, requests, threads; none are HR), no tables (schema.sql grep clean), no wire shapes (wire.ts grep clean; WIRE_CONTRACT_VERSION '1.0.0' @ wire.ts:35), and DATA-CONTRACT.md is silent (only wall-clock/date-shift semantics hits). Contract-first: new wire shapes + routes + tables land in fetchd_backend BEFORE the portal implements src/api/http (empty; portal has no contracts.ts/sync — adjudicated DRIFT-55/58). Nothing ripples to mobile (no surface).
- Role/identity reconciliation blocks permission gating: portal permissions 'owner'|'manager'|'trainer' (types.ts:219) vs backend staff_role 'owner-shanthi'|'trainer'|'office' (schema.sql:135 / wire.ts:74) — no 'manager'; AddStaffModal offers a third vocabulary. Adjudicated DRIFT-56 + DRIFT-43 (and D4 @ portal-staff-operations.md:138); roster CONTENT drift is separately DRIFT-45. Identity owned by auth-accounts.md — do not relitigate here, route to that domain.
- No per-staff hourly rate exists anywhere server-side (analytics fabricates it from role-name keywords, aggregate.ts:182-189 — adjudicated NOTE-45, money-math policy NOTE-49/D9). The backend's /staff/rates is per-location service pricing (staffRates.ts:19-27), not staff pay. Gross-pay derivation and any labor-cost figure need a real rate source (new staff.hourly_rate_cents column or an HR/rate table) before pay-periods can be more than seeded numbers.
- Pay-period boundary policy (semi-monthly vs biweekly, Chicago calendar) and the pending→paid transition are undefined everywhere; only 'aggregated from shifts in production' (docs/backend-logic.md:1018) exists as intent. Shape ambiguity adjudicated NOTE-42.
- Time-off approval workflow is entirely absent (no approve/deny API, no approver role, no notification, no revoke) — Approved/Denied statuses are fixture-only (buildTimeOffFixture @ fixtures.ts:2797-2819); must be designed alongside the read/create endpoints.
- Shift authoring is absent: no create/edit/delete Shift API (schedules are fixtures), no punch-correction/missed-clock-out path, and multi-shift days / overnight shifts break the one-shift-per-day (portal doc R5) + ClockTime wall-string model. Punches should become timestamptz per DATA-CONTRACT.md R5 (@:27) with Chicago-wall-time display derived at the edge (portal's ClockTime string should not survive the wire). The portal's two contradictory worked-hours formulas (R6 vs R15) are adjudicated BUG-23 (CONFIRMED) — the server formula must be single-sourced.
- campus alignment: portal StaffMember.campus is display 'Fayetteville'|'Bentonville'; backend staff.location is a locations.slug FK. Adjudicated DRIFT-60 (campus client-side only). locations.display_name (schema.sql:186-188, 'Fayetteville, AR'/'Bentonville, AR') already provides a server-side slug↔display source, though strings differ from the portal's bare names.
- Visibility gating: the mock exposes everyone's punches + rates + labor cost to any viewer (adjudicated DRIFT-57 — documented matrix vs zero enforcement); the documented §5.2 matrix marks all reads open to all staff roles (docs/backend-logic.md:1077, surfaced at portal-staff-operations.md:156) — likely unintended for pay data.
