## Messaging / Threads <a id="messaging"></a>
_Demand: MessagesApi @ client.ts:270-274 (threads 271, thread 272, send 273, markRead 274) · Supply: threads.ts, staffThreads.ts · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: messaging.md_

The demand side is the STAFF portal, so every MessagesApi op maps to the backend's `/staff/threads*` surface — the owner `/threads*` twins are the five SUPPLY-ONLY entries, consumed only by the mobile owner app. Wire already owns ThreadWire + MessageWire (v1.0.0), so the read ops lean on existing shapes and the desktop portal's parallel MessageThread/Message/MessageTopic (types.ts:546-574) should be RETIRED (pre-contract model, DRIFT-38/D2), not extended. Verification changed one structural conclusion: the thread queue (messages.threads) is B, not A — GET /staff/threads serves the queue but ThreadWire omits `owner_id`, which the portal UI consumes in three places; every other bucket held. Three recurring mismatches drive the domain: (a) the portal embeds `messages[]` in the thread while the wire normalizes ThreadWire vs MessageWire[]; (b) the portal addresses sends by (ownerId,topic) route-or-create while the backend addresses threads by id and has no runtime create (R4/NOTE-30); (c) staff-vs-owner read-state semantics are inverted (DRIFT-39), making markRead the one true build-new (C), gated on adjudication. Most "gaps" here are already-adjudicated ledger items (DRIFT-38/39/40/41/43, NOTE-29/30, BUG-15) — flagged inside each affected entry so nobody re-litigates them as new findings.

### `messages.threads` — staff cross-owner thread queue
- **Portal:** `threads(): Promise<MessageThread[]> @ client.ts:271` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:847-851 (messages.threads)
- **Behavior:** Staff cross-owner thread queue: every live thread, newest last-message first. Portal renders topic-filter pills + per-thread unread badge + last-message preview + the OWNER's name per row (MessageThreadList.tsx:98 ownerMap.get(thread.ownerId)).
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** GET /staff/threads @ routes/staffThreads.ts:53 · wire: ThreadWire @ wire.ts:260 · contract: §B Thread @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1865-1868; §C Messaging @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2103-2105; verb 3 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1547-1553 · tables: threads @ schema.sql:1347 (owner_id column exists @ 1349); thread_dogs @ schema.sql:1365 (related_dog_ids); messages @ schema.sql:1372 (unread_count derive)
- **Response:** `ThreadWire[] = [{ id, category, title, sub_text, participant:{ id, name, role, image_path? }, related_dog_ids: string[], last_message, last_message_at, unread_count }] + PROPOSED additive owner_id: string`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Errors:** 403 forbidden (owner principal hitting staff route, requireStaff @ principalNarrows.ts:55-62)
- **Action:** ADDITIVE: the queue itself is served — GET /staff/threads returns all live threads cross-owner, requireStaff-gated, sorted last_message_at DESC (threadsRepository.findLive:48-54), matching the portal sort (mock/index.ts:849-850). But ThreadWire carries NO owner identity, and the portal demand is load-bearing on it: list row shows the owner's name (MessageThreadList.tsx:98), ChatModal header shows '{ownerName} · Owner' (MessagesScreen.tsx:114, ChatModal.tsx:74), and send routes by ownerId (MessagesScreen.tsx:118). related_dog_ids x GET /staff/dogs is only a partial workaround (related_dog_ids may be empty per §B Thread). Add owner_id (optionally owner_name) to the staff queue response — either an additive optional field on ThreadWire (minor version bump; mobile-safe: redundant/ignored by the owner app, both clients resync via sync:contracts) or a staff-specific projection. Rest is client-side shaping: read denormalized last_message/last_message_at instead of t.messages[last].
- **Promote:** Adopt wire ThreadWire (wire.ts:260) in the portal; retire portal-only MessageThread (types.ts:566-574). Map: topic->category (same 4 literals: types.ts:548 = wire.ts:57), unreadCount->unread_count, subText->sub_text; add participant/related_dog_ids/last_message/last_message_at + the proposed owner_id; drop embedded messages[] (fetched separately).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open Q:_ owner_id on the staff queue: extend ThreadWire itself (owner app ignores it) or introduce a staff-specific StaffThreadWire projection? so `owner_id` is a desktop-portal-driven additive.

### `messages.thread` — fetch one thread with messages (staff scope)
- **Portal:** `thread(id: ThreadId): Promise<MessageThread> @ client.ts:272` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:853-856 (messages.thread)
- **Behavior:** Fetch one thread by id WITH its full message list embedded (portal ChatModal reads thread.messages, ChatModal.tsx:87). Staff scope, cross-owner.
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** GET /staff/threads/:id/messages @ routes/staffThreads.ts:67 · wire: ThreadWire @ wire.ts:260; MessageWire @ wire.ts:285 · contract: §B Message @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1870; Day-19b staff messages read @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1576-1580 · tables: threads @ schema.sql:1347; messages @ schema.sql:1372; message_attachments @ schema.sql:1400
- **Response:** `Split to align with wire: metadata = ThreadWire (single); messages = MessageWire[] = [{ id, thread_id, sender_id, sender_name?, text, attachments?, sent_at, is_read }]`
- **Auth:** [staff] · **Errors:** 404 not_found (thread absent — existsLive check, staffThreads.ts:74-77); 403 forbidden (owner principal, requireStaff @ staffThreads.ts:72)
- **Action:** ADDITIVE: the MESSAGES half is fully served by existing GET /staff/threads/:id/messages (cross-owner MessageWire[], A). Missing: thread METADATA by id for staff — the owner twin GET /threads/:id (threads.ts:79) 404s for staff (threads.ts:82-84, R2), and no GET /staff/threads/:id exists (re-verified: staffThreads.ts registers exactly 3 routes; broad grep of all 40 route files finds no other /staff/threads* registration). Add GET /staff/threads/:id [staff] -> ThreadWire, mirroring the owner by-id read but cross-owner + requireStaff-gated; it should carry the same owner_id additive as messages.threads (staff metadata without owner identity has the identical gap). Mobile-safe: brand-new staff route, touches no owner endpoint. Portal then composes ThreadWire + MessageWire[]. (Alternatively reuse the /staff/threads list-cache entry for metadata and add nothing — see open_questions.)
- **Promote:** Portal thread(id) must stop returning an embedded-messages MessageThread; adopt ThreadWire + a separate MessageWire[] fetch (wire.ts:260/285). Message.from ('owner'|'staff') -> sender_id + sender_name presence (is-me keyed off sender_name absence, R11 @ messaging.md:46).
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open Q:_ No GET /staff/threads/:id (single-thread metadata, staff scope) exists — add it, or have the portal reconstruct metadata from the GET /staff/threads list cache and only fetch messages by id?

### `messages.send` — staff composes or replies
- **Portal:** `send(input: SendMessageInput): Promise<MessageThread> @ client.ts:273 (SendMessageInput @ client.ts:147-154)` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:858-897 (messages.send: MS-1 route-or-create @ 869-885, MS-2 channel opt-out filter @ 865-867, MS-3 text-or-attachment @ 861-864)
- **Behavior:** Staff composes OR replies. Portal routes by (ownerId, topic): one thread per owner per topic, create-if-absent (MS-1); text OR >=1 attachment (MS-3); channels multi-select filtered to opted-in (MS-2); stores message from:'staff'; marks thread read on send (mock/index.ts:895).
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** POST /staff/threads/:id/messages @ routes/staffThreads.ts:88 · wire: MessageWire @ wire.ts:285 · contract: verb 3 @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1547-1553; owner send Day-19e @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1764-1770; POST /threads deferred @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2104 · tables: messages @ schema.sql:1372; threads @ schema.sql:1347 (last_message bump); message_attachments @ schema.sql:1400 (only if staff attachments land)
- **Request:** `TARGET (staff reply, existing thread): { text: string } (trimmed, 1-4000, strict — staffThreads.ts:32-34). Proposed additive for staff attachments: { text?: string, attachment_media_ids?: string[] } mirroring the owner send (threads.ts:48-59). Path :id = thread uuid.` **Response:** `MessageWire = { id, thread_id, sender_id, sender_name, text, sent_at, is_read, attachments? } (201)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Bumps threads.last_message/last_message_at same-tx (staffThreads.ts:118); enqueues a message-received notification to the owner (deep_link_path '/messages/:threadId' @ staffThreads.ts:126, sender_staff_id=actor, staffThreads.ts:121-128). Requires Idempotency-Key (staffThreads.ts:95, R17). NOTE: that deep link is an already-adjudicated CONFIRMED bug — BUG-15/D1, DISCREPANCIES.md:129-133 — the owner app has no /messages/:id route (uses /chat/[threadId]). · **Errors:** invalid_payload (empty/invalid body, staffThreads.ts:96,155); not_found (thread absent, staffThreads.ts:109-110); 403 forbidden (owner principal, requireStaff @ staffThreads.ts:93)
- **Action:** PRIMARY PATH = REUSE POST /staff/threads/:id/messages for the staff text reply — the portal already holds the thread id (openThread @ MessagesScreen.tsx:35,112) but its mock routes by (ownerId,topic) instead (MessagesScreen.tsx:50-51); that routing is a mock quirk already adjudicated as portal-spec-only (DRIFT-41 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:487-489 / R5) — send by thread id. Genuinely missing supply, all mobile-safe additive-or-deferred: (1) ADDITIVE attachment_media_ids on the staff reply body (mirror owner arm threads.ts:48-59) — blocked on the deferred staff-media rework (message-attachment purpose is owner-arm-only, staff POSTing it 403s, media.ts:38-42,437; staff-author upload deferred, DATA-CONTRACT.md:1567); (2) create-if-absent compose is BLOCKED on deferred POST /threads (R4, DATA-CONTRACT.md:2104; adjudicated as mock theater, NOTE-30 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:817-821); (3) channels has no backend model (D2/DRIFT-38). Why not straight A: the op bundles create + attachments + channels the reply route does not provide.
- **Promote:** Portal send should return MessageWire (the created message) not the whole MessageThread — client invalidates/refetches. Drop SendMessageInput.channels + attachment 'pdf' kind (media_kind is image|video only, schema.sql:153; adjudicated NOTE-29/D10 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:811-815) on wire adoption.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open Q:_ Staff thread-creation (route-or-create, MS-1) is deferred — POST /threads is 'still deferred' (DATA-CONTRACT.md:2104, R4); the compose branch has no supply. Already in ledger as NOTE-30 (compose is mock theater).
  - _Open Q:_ One-thread-per-(owner,topic) is portal-spec only — already adjudicated as DRIFT-41 (DISCREPANCIES.md:487): no unique index on (owner_id,category); only threads_source_ref_uidx (source, external_ref) @ schema.sql:1361-1362; seed/mobile fixtures carry multiple sessions threads per owner. Must be re-adjudicated only if a compose verb lands.
  - _Open Q:_ Staff attachments: staff reply is text-only (R8; adjudicated NOTE-29); message-attachment media purpose is owner-only (media.ts staff 403) and staff-author media upload is deferred (DATA-CONTRACT.md:1567); portal mock also allows 'pdf' (D10).
  - _Open Q:_ Channels multi-select / opt-out (SendMessageInput.channels, ChannelPref @ types.ts:113) has no backend concept (D2/DRIFT-38) — drop, or model delivery channels server-side?

### `messages.markRead` — staff clears a thread's unread badge
- **Portal:** `markRead(id: ThreadId): Promise<MessageThread> @ client.ts:274` · mock logic: mock/index.ts:899-904 (messages.markRead — sets unreadCount=0; NO backend counterpart)
- **Behavior:** Staff opens a thread -> clear its unread badge (portal fires on chat open, MessagesScreen.tsx:40). Portal semantics: 'msgs after staff's last read' (types.ts:572) — a staff-side read track.
- **Bucket:** C (+ schema change)
- **Backend:** POST /threads/:id/read @ routes/threads.ts:122 · contract: owner mark-read Day-19e @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1752-1763; §B Thread unread_count derived @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:1867 · tables: threads @ schema.sql:1347 (proposed staff_last_read_at column); messages @ schema.sql:1372
- **Request:** `PROPOSED: no body; Idempotency-Key header. Path :id = thread uuid.` **Response:** `PROPOSED: 204 No Content (mirror the owner mark-read; portal invalidates /staff/threads to recompute the badge)`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** Records staff read position (proposed). Requires Idempotency-Key. · **Errors:** PROPOSED: 404 not_found (thread absent); 403 forbidden (owner principal); idempotency replay
- **Action:** BUILD NEW: POST /staff/threads/:id/read [staff], Idempotency-Key required, 204. Why not reuse POST /threads/:id/read: owner-scoped (staff -> 404, threads.ts:125-127, R13) and it marks OWNER-facing read state (exactly the R12 unread set — staff messages the owner hasn't read) — the OPPOSITE actor and semantics. C re-proven this session: broad grep across ALL 40 files in src/routes/ finds no staff thread-read verb anywhere (only notification reads, notifications.ts:114/144, a different resource). Single read_at column (schema.sql:1379), owner-only verb. The gap is an ALREADY-ADJUDICATED drift, not a new finding: DRIFT-39 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:475-479 / D3 @ messaging.md:160 — 'No staff mark-read verb or staff-side read column exists'. Needs a schema decision (see schema_change) + cross-repo adjudication — do not ship unilaterally.
- **Promote:** New concept for the contract: a staff-side read marker (e.g. threads.staff_last_read_at) and a staff-facing unread count distinct from owner-facing ThreadWire.unread_count. Would add a wire field if adopted.
- **Schema change:** Proposed (adjudication-gated): add threads.staff_last_read_at timestamptz (or a per-participant thread_reads table) — no existing column for staff read state (verified schema.sql:1347-1390). Additive, non-breaking.
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open Q:_ Staff-side read state is UNMODELED — already adjudicated as DRIFT-39/D3: backend unread_count is owner-facing (R12, schema.sql:1389-1390); the portal's badge + markRead want STAFF-unread, which the desktop portal type defines as the opposite (types.ts:572). Requires a schema/contract decision (staff_last_read_at vs per-side read marks) before this verb can exist.

This owner arm IS the reference for the proposed staff attachment_media_ids additive. Keep as-is. |

### Open questions
- Staff-side read state is UNMODELED — already adjudicated as DRIFT-39 (DISCREPANCIES.md:475-479) / D3 (messaging.md:160): backend unread_count is owner-facing (R12, schema.sql:1389-1390) while the portal's 'N unread' badge and markRead want STAFF-unread (types.ts:572 defines it as the opposite). Single read_at track, owner-only mark-read verb. Blocks messages.markRead — needs a schema decision (threads.staff_last_read_at or per-participant thread_reads) + cross-repo adjudication before building.
- Staff thread-creation is deferred (R4): POST /threads is 'still deferred' (DATA-CONTRACT.md:2104); threads exist only via seed (seed-dev.ts insert into threads @ ~1065). The portal send's route-or-create compose branch (MS-1) has no supply, and one-thread-per-(owner,topic) is portal-spec ONLY — already adjudicated as DRIFT-41 (DISCREPANCIES.md:487-489): no unique index on (owner_id,category) (only threads_source_ref_uidx @ schema.sql:1361-1362); seed/mobile carry multiple sessions threads per owner. Must adjudicate (unique-index+route-or-create vs free threads) before any compose verb lands.
- Owner identity on the staff thread surface: ThreadWire has no owner_id, but the desktop portal's list row / chat header / send routing all consume thread.ownerId (MessageThreadList.tsx:98, MessagesScreen.tsx:114,118). Additive owner_id (+owner_name?) on ThreadWire vs a staff-specific projection — needs a wire decision (drives both messages.threads and the proposed GET /staff/threads/:id).
- Staff attachments on send: staff reply is text-only today (R8, staffThreads.ts:32-34; adjudicated NOTE-29 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:811-815); message-attachment media purpose is owner-arm-only (staff POSTing it 403s, media.ts:38-42,437) and staff-author media upload is deferred (DATA-CONTRACT.md:1567-1569). Portal mock also permits attachment kind 'pdf' which media_kind (image|video, schema.sql:153) cannot represent (D10). Will staff attachment support ever land?
- Channels multi-select / opt-out model (SendMessageInput.channels @ client.ts:153, ChannelPref @ types.ts:113) has no backend representation (D2/DRIFT-38). Drop on wire adoption, or model delivery channels + opt-outs server-side?
- participant.role vocabulary drift (DRIFT-40 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:481, DRIFT-43 @ 499): wire StaffRole 'owner-shanthi'|'trainer'|'office' (wire.ts:74) vs portal StaffMember.permissions 'owner'|'manager'|'trainer' (types.ts:219) vs mobile domain 'owner'|'trainer'|'staff'. Adjudicated for mobile (messageRepository.toParticipantRole) but not for the desktop portal — it must translate when adopting ThreadParticipantWire (wire.ts:253).
- message-received deep link (BUG-15 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:129-133, CONFIRMED in ledger @ 1132; D1): staff reply enqueues deep_link_path '/messages/:threadId' (staffThreads.ts:126) but the owner app has no such route (uses /chat/[threadId]) — one side must change before staff replies deep-link correctly — **FIXED in the uncommitted Phase-2 WIP** (staffThreads.ts now emits `/chat/:threadId` + structured kind/id); still live at committed HEAD `578d13d`. Not portal-demand but rides the same staff send verb.

## Announcements, School events, Notifications, Device tokens <a id="announcements"></a>
_Demand: AnnouncementsApi @ client.ts:277-280 (list :278, create :279), EventsApi @ client.ts:282-285 (list :283, create :284); CreateAnnouncementInput @ client.ts:156, CreateSchoolEventInput @ client.ts:158 · Supply: announcements.ts, events.ts, notifications.ts, device-tokens.ts · Logic: none dedicated · Business logic: notifications-events.md_

Of the 4 portal ops, `announcements.list` is A (reuse GET /announcements with shape alignment; mismatch adjudicated as DRIFT-47/D5), `events.list` is B (catalog reuse of GET /events plus an additive staff roster read — the portal renders per-event `signedUpDogIds` that GET /events/rsvps deliberately soft-empties for staff), and both creates (`announcements.create`, `events.create`) are C — no announcement/event write route exists anywhere in `src/routes/`, verified by broad grep. The other 11 routes are SUPPLY-ONLY owner-scoped surfaces (notifications feed/unread-count/read/read-all/dismiss, device-token register/revoke, event RSVPs and single-event reads). The whole domain (Notification/Announcement/Event/EventRsvp/DeviceToken) is absent from `wire.ts` v1.0.0, so there is no generated drift guard and edits to `lib/*Wire.ts` are contract changes in disguise. Backend @ HEAD 07025b3 'feat(notifications): Phase 1' lands the three notification-mutation verbs (read/read-all/dismiss), closing adjudicated BUG-18/D1.

### `announcements.list` — read the announcements catalog
- **Portal:** `list(): Promise<Announcement[]> @ src/api/client.ts:278 (AnnouncementsApi @ 277-280)` · mock logic: mock announcements.list @ src/api/mock/index.ts:908-913 (sort by sentAt desc)
- **Behavior:** Staff portal reads the announcements catalog for the Announcements screen (mock sorts sent history newest-first by sentAt).
- **Bucket:** A
- **Backend:** GET /announcements @ routes/announcements.ts:41 · wire: AnnouncementWire @ src/lib/announcementWire.ts:35 (NOT in wire.ts v1.0.0 — whole domain absent, verified: zero Announcement/Event/Notification/DeviceToken hits in contracts/wire.ts; notifications-events.md:236) · contract: §C Notifications & announcements @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2115 (GET /announcements?location= is the ONLY announcements route); §B Event/Announcement @ :2021-2027 · tables: announcements @ schema.sql:1534
- **Request:** `query ?location=fayetteville|bentonville (optional; LOCATION_SLUGS @ src/db/schema/schema.ts:17); no body` **Response:** `AnnouncementWire[] : [{ id, category, title, body?, published_at, deep_link_path?, cta?{label,kind,target} }]`
- **Auth:** [staff] (owner+staff both; authed — authHook only @ announcements.ts:43, no requireOwner) · **Side effects:** read-through cached 300s under ann:{location|'all'} (TTL @ announcementsRepository.ts:41, key @ :57-58, readThrough @ :76-97) · **Errors:** 400 bad_request (invalid ?location, parseListQuery @ announcements.ts:54-60); 401 unauth
- **Action:** REUSE GET /announcements: staff already read it (R30 @ notifications-events.md:89 — authHook not requireOwner; owners AND staff see the same live list). ALIGN the portal to AnnouncementWire (category/published_at/cta) and DROP the mock's audience/channels/recipientCount/color/scheduledFor — authoring artifacts absent from the read shape ('color' is a portal-only tint, recipientCount is a pre-send preview never persisted — no such column exists on announcements; channels/scheduledFor have no backend model); sentAt maps to published_at. This model mismatch is ALREADY-ADJUDICATED drift: DRIFT-47 (DISCREPANCIES.md:523) / D5 (notifications-events.md:256) — 'from-scratch reconciliation, not a swap'. Do NOT mint a parallel portal Announcement type.
  - _Promote:_ Promote AnnouncementWire (announcementWire.ts:35) into wire.ts — kills the portal mock Announcement (types.ts:579) vs mobile hand-rolled RawAnnouncement duplication (ownership table @ notifications-events.md:240-247); domain entirely uncovered by wire.ts v1.0.0
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Portal sent-history rows render recipientCount + sentAt (AnnouncementsScreen.tsx:30-32); the read wire has published_at but no recipient count and no column persists one — portal drops it, or a recipient-count surface (schema + emit) must be adjudicated.
    - Portal 'audience' (All clients / campus / group name) has no analogue in the read wire; only target_location (slug FK) exists server-side and is deliberately NOT emitted (announcementWire.ts:18-19).

### `announcements.create` — compose + publish an announcement
- **Portal:** `create(input: CreateAnnouncementInput): Promise<Announcement> @ client.ts:279; CreateAnnouncementInput = Omit<Announcement,'id'|'sentAt'|'recipientCount'> @ client.ts:156` · mock logic: mock announcements.create @ index.ts:915-926 (validate title @ 917, allocId('ann') @ 920, recipientCount @ 922 via announcementRecipientCount @ index.ts:1163)
- **Behavior:** Staff compose + publish a school-wide/targeted announcement (title, body, audience, channels, expiry, optional scheduled send); mock returns the row with a pre-send recipientCount.
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** (none) · wire: AnnouncementWire @ announcementWire.ts:35 · contract: no POST /announcements in §C (GET-only @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2115); notifications-events.md OQ3 (:267) + DRIFT-47 (DISCREPANCIES.md:523) / D5 (:256) · tables: announcements @ schema.sql:1534
- **Request:** `TARGET: { category: announcement_category, title, body?, target_location?: 'fayetteville'|'bentonville', is_pinned?: boolean, deep_link_path?, cta?{label,kind:'enroll'|'route'|'external',target} }` **Response:** `AnnouncementWire (201): { id, category, title, body?, published_at, deep_link_path?, cta? }`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** MUST invalidate the ann:* read-through cache post-commit — invalidatePattern('ann:*') per the cache map (cache.ts:29); the helper EXISTS (invalidatePattern @ cache.ts:131) but nothing announcement-side calls it because no write route exists (R31 @ notifications-events.md:90). NO audit row on INSERT — audit_capture fires AFTER UPDATE OR DELETE only (schema.sql:1657-1658); announcements IS in the trigger set (:1654) so later edits/expires are audited. · **Errors:** 400 bad_request (missing title / invalid category / partial cta violates announcements_cta_all_or_none CHECK @ schema.sql:1550-1553), 401, 403 (owner)
- **Action:** BUILD POST /announcements [auth, staff]. Why not reuse: NO announcement write route exists anywhere in the backend (verified broad grep of all 41 files in src/routes/ — announcements/events verbs exist only in announcements.ts + events.ts, all reads except owner RSVPs); announcementsRepository has only findLive (announcementsRepository.ts:61-98). Proposed: POST /announcements, body {category: announcement_category ('urgent'|'team'|'class'|'event'|'promo'|'report' @ schema.sql:134), title, body?, target_location? ('fayetteville'|'bentonville', NULL=all), is_pinned?=false, deep_link_path?, cta? {label,kind,target} all-or-none per CHECK} → 201 AnnouncementWire; requireStaff + Idempotency-Key + withMutation; 400/401/403; NO booking gates; MUST bust ann:* post-commit (invalidatePattern @ cache.ts:131). Mapping caveats: portal 'audience' collapses only partially to target_location (slug or NULL) — group-name audiences unmappable; portal 'channels' (email/sms/push) + 'scheduledFor' have NO backend counterpart (type 'announcement' has no fan-out producer — R18 @ notifications-events.md:65 / NOTE-36 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:853 / D7; no scheduled-announcement mechanism — D8 @ :259 / NOTE-37 @ DISCREPANCIES.md:859). Recipient-count preview would be a separate GET (mock computes client-side: computeRecipientCount @ useRecipientPreview.ts:30-53). Gap is already-adjudicated: DRIFT-47/D5 records that portal spec §5.3 maps POST /announcements and 'neither exists in the backend'.
  - _Promote:_ AnnouncementWire → wire.ts (same as list)
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Where does announcement authoring land — backend POST vs ops-seeded content? (notifications-events.md OQ3 @ :267; unresolvable from code).
    - Portal channels (email/sms/push) + scheduledFor have no backend model — drop them, or build delivery/scheduling surfaces?
    - audience→target_location is lossy (group-name audiences unmappable to the single location-slug FK).

### `events.list` — read the events catalog (+ per-event roster)
- **Portal:** `list(): Promise<SchoolEvent[]> @ client.ts:283 (EventsApi @ 282-285)` · mock logic: mock events.list @ index.ts:930-933 (returns db.events)
- **Behavior:** Staff portal reads the events catalog for the Events screen — INCLUDING a per-event signed-up-dog roster rendered on every card and in the detail modal.
- **Bucket:** B
- **Backend:** GET /events @ routes/events.ts:53 · wire: EventWire @ src/lib/eventWire.ts:36 (NOT in wire.ts v1.0.0) · contract: §C Events @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2109; §B Event grew spots_filled + capacity? @ :2021-2027 · tables: events @ schema.sql:1429; spots_filled batched from event_rsvps @ :1450 + event_rsvp_dogs @ :1461
- **Request:** `no query, no body` **Response:** `EventWire[] : [{ id, name, date, duration_minutes, location{label,address,latitude,longitude}, description?, is_recurring, capacity?, spots_filled }]`
- **Auth:** [staff] (owner+staff both; authed — authHook @ events.ts:53) · **Side effects:** not read-through cached (cache-noop, R41 @ notifications-events.md:103; events.ts:100) · **Errors:** none beyond 401 (200 for any authed principal)
- **Action:** PARTIAL REUSE + additive change (flipped from A — partial coverage). REUSE GET /events for the catalog (staff already read it; R35 @ notifications-events.md:97 — all live events incl. past, oldest-first via orderBy asc(starts_at) @ eventsRepository.ts:51); ALIGN portal to EventWire — mock campus/spotsLabel/color/time don't exist backend-side (campus → flat loc_*→nested location{}; time folds into starts_at→wire 'date'; spotsLabel derives from capacity+spots_filled) — adjudicated drift DRIFT-47/D5. NOT COVERED: the roster. EventCard renders signedUpDogIds on every list row (EventCard.tsx:18-19) and EventDetailModal maps them (:25,:61), but NO staff-accessible endpoint supplies rosters — GET /events exposes only spots_filled (a count) and GET /events/rsvps HARDCODES [] for staff (events.ts:69; R37 @ notifications-events.md:99 'they organize, not RSVP'). Exact additive change: give the staff branch of GET /events/rsvps a real read (all live RSVPs; optionally an additive owner_id key on EventRsvpWire) or add GET /staff/events/rsvps [staff]. Mobile-safety: owner-principal responses are byte-identical either way (mobile runs owner principals only) and any EventRsvpWire key addition is additive (wire semver minor) — but flipping the DELIBERATE staff soft-empty (route comment events.ts:33-35) needs adjudication.
  - _Promote:_ Promote EventWire + EventRsvpWire (eventWire.ts:36,48) into wire.ts — kills portal mock SchoolEvent (types.ts:592) vs mobile RawEvent/RawRsvp duplication
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Portal 'campus' (Fayetteville/Bentonville/Both locations) has no backend column — events carry free-text loc_label/loc_address, not a campus enum; a staff events view keyed by campus cannot filter on the wire today.
    - Staff roster read shape: widen GET /events/rsvps' staff branch (contradicts the R37 comment) vs a new staff-scoped route — needs adjudication before the portal Events screen can be wired.

### `events.create` — create a school event
- **Portal:** `create(input: CreateSchoolEventInput): Promise<SchoolEvent> @ client.ts:284; CreateSchoolEventInput = Omit<SchoolEvent,'id'|'signedUpDogIds'> @ client.ts:158` · mock logic: mock events.create @ index.ts:935-941 (validate name @ 937, allocId('evt'), signedUpDogIds=[])
- **Behavior:** Staff create a new school event; mock composer collects name/date/time/campus/capacity (+description/portalOpen which are dropped — no home on the input type, comment @ CreateEventModal.tsx:35-36).
- **Bucket:** C
- **Backend:** (none) · wire: EventWire @ eventWire.ts:36 · contract: no POST /events in §C (GET + owner RSVP-writes only @ DATA-CONTRACT.md:2109-2110); notifications-events.md OQ3 (:267) + DRIFT-47/D5 · tables: events @ schema.sql:1429 (+ event_series @ :1418 if series-linked)
- **Request:** `TARGET: { name, starts_at (ISO tz), duration_minutes, location{label,address,latitude,longitude}, description?, capacity?, is_recurring?, series_id? }` **Response:** `EventWire (201): { id, name, date, duration_minutes, location{...}, description?, is_recurring, capacity?, spots_filled:0 }`
- **Auth:** [staff] (+idempotent) · **Side effects:** NO audit row on INSERT (audit_capture is AFTER UPDATE OR DELETE only @ schema.sql:1657-1658; events IS in the trigger set @ :1653 so later edits/expires are audited); events not read-through cached (R41) — nothing to invalidate · **Errors:** 400 bad_request (missing name/starts_at/location), 401, 403 (owner)
- **Action:** BUILD POST /events [auth, staff]. Why not reuse: NO event write route exists — verified broad grep of all src/routes/ files: the only /events verbs are the 3 GETs + 2 owner-only RSVP writes in events.ts; eventsRepository has no insert. Proposed: POST /events, body {name, starts_at (ISO tz), duration_minutes, location:{label,address,latitude,longitude}, description?, capacity? (NULL=uncapped soft cap per schema.sql:1440-1445), is_recurring?=false, series_id?} → 201 EventWire (spots_filled 0); requireStaff + Idempotency-Key + withMutation; 400/401/403; NO gates. Mapping caveats: the portal composer supplies only name/date/time/campus/capacity (payload @ CreateEventModal.tsx:39-45) — a real create needs full loc_label/loc_address/lat/lng (portal 'campus' enum → a location record) and duration_minutes, neither collected today. spotsLabel/color are derived/display, never create inputs. Gap is already-adjudicated: DRIFT-47/D5 records portal spec §5.3's POST /events as nonexistent backend-side.
  - _Promote:_ EventWire → wire.ts (same as list)
- **Confidence:** VERIFIED
  - _Open questions:_
    - Portal event-create supplies campus+time but not address/lat/lng/duration — the events row requires loc_* + duration_minutes; the composer needs new fields or a campus→location lookup.
    - Does staff authoring also create recurring series (event_series) or only one-offs? is_recurring vs series_id authoring convention is unenforced (OQ8 @ notifications-events.md:270).

Staff/portal push is an explicit future extension point, not this route. |
| DELETE /device-tokens/:token | routes/device-tokens.ts:105 | Owner soft-expires a token (sign-out / opt-out) — sets expired_at; 204 on success, 404 when not a live row for this owner (missing + not-yours collapse — enumeration defense; token never echoed, device-tokens.ts:124-127). Owner-only (staff 403). No portal demand. Request: path :token (1-512 chars); no body; Response: 204 No Content; [owner] (+idempotent); 404 (missing/not-yours), 400 (invalid param), 403 (staff), 401; Idempotency-Key required; withMutation; softExpireByToken @ device-tokens.ts:120; not cached; device_tokens.expired_at @ schema.sql:1566. | SUPPLY-ONLY: no portal op; owner-device token revocation. Adjudicated NOTE-38 (DISCREPANCIES.md:865) / D10 (notifications-events.md:261) — no worker sweeps orphaned tokens, so a failed sign-out DELETE leaves a stale live row. |

### Open questions
- Announcement/event AUTHORING has no backend home (GET-only; announcementsRepository/eventsRepository have no insert; broad grep of all src/routes/ confirms no write route anywhere) — both create ops are bucket C. Backend POST vs ops-seeded content is unresolved (notifications-events.md OQ3; DRIFT-47/D5 — already-adjudicated drift).
- Portal Announcement/SchoolEvent models are incompatible with backend AnnouncementWire/EventWire (audience/channels/recipientCount/scheduledFor and campus/spotsLabel have no backend columns) — adjudicated as DRIFT-47/D5: any real wiring is a from-scratch reconciliation aligning to the backend shapes, not a mock swap.
- Portal 'audience' (group/campus) → backend target_location (single location-slug FK) is lossy: group-name and 'All clients' audiences don't map; a recipient-count preview endpoint (mock computes client-side, computeRecipientCount @ useRecipientPreview.ts:30-53) would be new.
- Portal event-create collects only name/date/time/campus/capacity; the events row requires loc_label/loc_address/lat/lng + duration_minutes — the composer needs new fields or a campus→location lookup.
- Staff events roster: the portal renders per-event signedUpDogIds (EventCard.tsx:18-19) but GET /events/rsvps hardcodes [] for staff (events.ts:69, deliberate per R37) — the events.list B entry proposes a staff-branch widening or a new staff-scoped read; needs adjudication. Likewise no staff attendee-ADD verb exists despite R39 allowing staff to exceed the soft cap.
- Whole domain (Notification/Announcement/Event/EventRsvp/DeviceToken) is absent from wire.ts v1.0.0 (verified: zero hits; notifications-events.md:236) — no generated drift guard; edits to lib/*Wire.ts are contract changes in disguise. Any portal wiring should promote these shapes into wire.ts with a WIRE_CONTRACT_VERSION minor bump.

# Global roll-ups

## Roll-up 1 — NEW endpoints to build (bucket C) <a id="rollup-1"></a>

All staff-scoped (`requireStaff`), snake_case wire, mutations idempotent (`Idempotency-Key`) per existing staff-route conventions. Full signatures, bodies, gates, and error codes live in each op's entry. Grouped by build theme; **(gated)** = do not build before its roll-up-5 adjudication.

**Directory reads (unblock most portal screens):**
1. `GET /staff/dogs/:dogId` — staff single-dog detail (meds, feeding, notes, eval fields); `GET /dogs/:id` 404s staff by design. (§2)
2. `GET /staff/owners` — cross-owner client directory; **no owner-listing route exists anywhere today**. (§2)
3. `GET /staff/owners/:ownerId` — staff owner detail; `GET /me` is self-only. (§2)
4. `GET /staff/reports` — cross-owner report list; `reports.ts:15-17` promises `/staff/reports/*` reads but only POST + PATCH exist. (§7)
5. `GET /staff/invoices/:id` — invoice detail + `refunds[]` + derived `refunded_cents` for the refund modal. (§9)
6. `GET /staff/dogs/:id/credit-lots` — per-dog credit lots (all modes/campuses, incl. never-expiring). (§8)
7. `GET /staff/owners/:id/credit-ledger` — per-owner credit movements across dogs' lots. (§8)
8. `GET /staff/cohorts/:cohortId/sessions` — week-folded attendance roster (incl. per-write `booking_id`). (§6)

**Money verbs:**
9. `POST /staff/refunds` — charge-keyed staff refund (`charge_id` XOR `invoice_id`), R9 cumulative cap, Stripe post-commit + webhook reconciliation (UI must render "pending" until webhook lands — BUG-5 precedent). (§9)
10. `POST /staff/invoices/:id/remind` — staff dunning nudge via the scheduled-notifications lane; needs an idempotency/dedupe-window ruling. (§9)

**Requests:**
11. `POST /staff/requests/:id/reopen` — undo-of-**deny** only (`cancelled`+`approved_by_staff_id` → `submitted`); undo-of-approve is deliberately not proposed (would reverse booking/credit/notification effects). Needs a state-machine ruling — the transition is currently forbidden. (§4)
12. `POST /staff/requests/approve-all` — bulk approve with per-request `decisions[]` + partial-success envelope; **likely YAGNI** (per-category scheduling makes a no-arg bulk impossible) — confirm intent before building. (§4)

**Stays:**
13. `PATCH /staff/bookings/:id/timing` — re-time a stay (`dropoff_at`/`pickup_at`, pickup>dropoff, re-stamp `cancel_deadline_at`, re-anchor 24h reminder). (§5)
14. `POST /staff/bookings` — staff cross-owner booking create (v1 scope: mid-stay day-school days for a lodger; capacity override switch; comp/credit/PAYG money model needs a ruling). (§5)

**Check-in (gated on the model decision, roll-up 5 #2):**
15. `GET /staff/checkin-board` — day board from bookings ⋈ `booking_dogs.attendance` (or event fold if that model wins). (§3)
16. `POST /staff/checkin` — (dog, date, location) → attendance write; check-out and stepwise undo verbs are **OPEN** until the model is picked. (§3)
17. `GET /staff/dashboard` — per-(date, location) snapshot; composable except check-in fold, shifts, thread location. (§3)

**Group classes:**
18. `POST /staff/cohorts` — create a term/cohort, snapshotting class defaults; `tba`/instructor persistence needs the §6 schema decisions. (§6)
19. `PATCH /staff/group-classes/:key` — edit class defaults + bust `groupclasses:*` caches. (§6)
20. `POST /staff/cohorts/:cohortId/reminder` — ad-hoc reminder → `{ recipient_count }` (INFERRED semantics — channel/idempotency open). (§6)

**Reports:**
21. `DELETE /staff/reports/:id` — soft delete via existing `reports.expired_at`, 204, 404 anti-enum. (§7)
22. *(secondary, only if server-side drafts are mandated)* `POST /staff/reports/:id/publish`. Primary model: client-held drafts; send = existing `POST /staff/reports`; types/skills/saveDraft need **no server endpoint** (R14 client-side catalogs). (§7)

**Messaging:**
23. `POST /staff/threads/:id/read` — staff read marker (needs the staff-read-state schema decision). (§14)

**Content authoring:**
24. `POST /announcements` — staff compose/publish (cache-busting `ann:*`); authoring-vs-ops-seeded needs adjudication (DRIFT-47). (§15)
25. `POST /events` — staff event create (full location shape required, not just campus). (§15)

**Offerings/retail (gated: retail scope + package mapping, roll-up 5 #11-13):**
26. `POST /staff/credit-packages` — create/supersede day-program credit packages (reprice = close+insert, R25). (§11)
27. `GET /staff/retail-items` + 28. `PATCH /staff/retail-items/:id/visibility` — retail catalog read/visibility toggle; **whole retail domain is backend-greenfield**. (§11)

**Rules / Flags / Groups (all gated, roll-up 5 #3):**
29-32. `GET|POST /staff/booking-rules`, `PATCH|DELETE /staff/booking-rules/:id` — configurable-rules engine conflicts with the locked gate triggers; adjudicate first. (§12)
33-36. `GET|POST /staff/dog-flags`, `DELETE /staff/dog-flags/:id`, `PUT /staff/dogs/:dogId/flags/:flagId` — staff label catalog + per-dog application (vs `dog_descriptors` unification question). (§12)
37-40. `GET|POST /staff/groups`, `DELETE /staff/groups/:id`, `PUT /staff/groups/:id/members` — groups are entirely unmodeled; first-class entity vs announcement-audience helper undecided. (§12)

**Staff HR (whole domain greenfield, roll-up 5 #15):**
41. `GET /staff` — roster as `StaffMemberWire[]`. 42. `GET /staff/shifts`. 43. `POST /staff/clock-in`. 44. `POST /staff/clock-out` — principal-bound punches. 45. `GET /staff/pay-periods`. 46. `GET /staff/time-off` + 47. `POST /staff/time-off`. (§13)

Not double-counted: `checkin.checkOut`/`checkin.undo` (OPEN, folded into #15-16's decision), `classes.checkInWeek` (client-side, do not build), `reports.types/skills/saveDraft` (no endpoint by design).

9. `GET /reports/:id` — add a `[staff]` cross-owner branch (staff currently hard-404, `reports.ts:125-127`); owner path byte-identical. (§7)
10. `GET /dogs/:id/credits` — staff-principal branch returning the same `CreditsWire` cross-owner (replaces the deliberate `!owner → 404`); owner arm untouched. Alternative: fold balance from the new credit-lots endpoint — pick one. (§8)
11. `GET /staff/invoices` — make `?parked` optional (drop the 400), add optional `owner_id/status/purpose/search` filters, widen `ParkedInvoiceWire` → `StaffInvoiceWire` (`paid_at`, `paid_charge_id`, `booking_id`, `cohort_id`, `issued_at`); decide unfiltered ordering. Existing parked-worklist consumer keeps working. (§9)

## Roll-up 3 — schema migrations (D) <a id="rollup-3"></a>

All additive; none relitigate the locked register R1-R8 or dated amendments. Conditional items say what decision triggers them.

**Unconditional (if their feature is built at all):**
1. `staff_shifts` table (id, staff_id FK, work_date, scheduled_start/end timestamptz, clocked_in_at/out_at) — shifts/clock. UNIQUE(staff_id, work_date) only if one-shift-per-day is confirmed. (§13)
2. `staff_time_off` table (staff_id, start/end_date, type enum vacation|personal|sick, status enum default pending). (§13)
3. `staff_pay_periods` table — or a derived view over `staff_shifts` once a boundary policy + rate source exist (preferred production intent). Requires a real pay-rate source (`staff.hourly_rate_cents` or rate table) — none exists anywhere. (§13)
4. `retail_items` table (location?, name, price_cents, stock, visible, created_at) — only real gap for retail read/toggle. (§11)

**Gated on adjudication:**
5. Check-in model: append-only `checkin_events` table **or** `booking_dogs.checked_out_at` + an "expected"/un-mark arm on the attendance verb (roll-up 5 #2). (§3)
6. Rules engine: `booking_rules` table — only if a configurable engine is adopted at all (roll-up 5 #3); soft-expire over hard delete. (§12)
7. Flags: `dog_flags` catalog + `dog_flags(dog_id, flag_id)` join (UNIQUE pair) — pending the `dog_descriptors` unification question. (§12)
8. Groups: `groups` + `group_members` tables; smart `alumni` derives from `dog_completed_programs`, never a stored boolean. (§12)
9. Group classes: nullable `cohorts.instructor_staff_id`; `group_classes.default_instructor_staff_id` + per-campus `default_schedule`; relax `cohorts.start_date` NOT NULL **or** forbid `tba` terms. (§6)
10. Messaging: `threads.staff_last_read_at` (or per-participant `thread_reads`) — staff read state. (§14)
11. Stays: `bookings.kennel` / `bookings.belongings` only if kennel assignment is a real requirement (mock-only today, DRIFT-4); per-night boarding-capacity store only if boarding is meant to be capped. (§5)
12. Dogs: nullable `dogs.gender` / `dogs.weight_grams` only if the portal truly renders them; everything else derivable (VIP = flag, `home_campus` = owner.location, `member_since` = created_at). (§2)
13. Requests: `pending_requests.deny_reason` if deny reasons should persist (accepted-then-dropped today, NOTE-5). (§4)
14. Invoices: an invoice location/campus source (column or booking join) if the portal's campus filter is kept. (§9)
15. Media: widen `media_kind` beyond image|video **only** if pdf attachments are truly required — flagged breaking; prefer dropping pdf. (§1)

**Not a proposal (already landed):** `notifications.dismissed_at` — added by the committed notifications Phase-1 work at `07025b3` (`schema.sql:1485`, §A Amendment 2026-07-24); §A Amendment 2026-07-24 (Phase 1) is committed at `578d13d`; NEW in the uncommitted Phase-2 WIP: additive nullable `deep_link_kind`/`deep_link_id` on notifications (schema.sql:1487-1488) and scheduled_notifications (schema.sql:1519-1520). (§15)

## Roll-up 4 — shapes to PROMOTE into wire.ts <a id="rollup-4"></a>

The systemic finding: **wire.ts v1.0.0 covers ~6 staff surfaces, responses only.** Nearly every domain has real wire shapes living route-inline or in `src/lib/*Wire.ts`, hand-mirrored by mobile with no drift guard — and the portal would become the *third* hand-roll. Promotion = additive minor bump, backend commit first, both clients resync.

**Existing shapes to hoist verbatim (kill current hand-mirrors):**
- `GroupClassWire`, `CohortWire` (`lib/groupClassWire.ts`), `EnrollmentWire` + the `POST /enrollments` body + typed 422 details (`enrollments.ts`) — DRIFT-26.
- `CreditsWire` (`credits.ts:39-58`, mirrored in mobile `creditsRepository.ts:58-81`) — NOTE-12.
- `MembershipWire` + `MembershipCreateWire` (`memberships.ts:66-100`) + `StaffMembershipWire` (`staffMemberships.ts:33-35`) — promote BEFORE the parked pause/resume portal UI (DRIFT-21).
- `AnnouncementWire` (`announcementWire.ts:35`), `EventWire` + `EventRsvpWire` (`eventWire.ts:36,48`) — domain entirely uncovered.
- `DayCapacityWire` (route-local; bypasses contract sync today — NOTE-1), `CancelWindowSettingWire` (`staffCancelWindow.ts`, when a portal editor lands), `CreditExpirySettingWire` (same trigger).
- `ParkedInvoiceWire` → widened `StaffInvoiceWire` + `RefundWire` (charge-keyed). (§9)
- Owner `DogWire` (`lib/dogWire.ts`) — the standing STATUS.md owner-surface expansion follow-up; the staff dog extension builds on it.
- Error envelope + `ApiErrorCode` enum (`lib/errors.ts` ↔ portal `ApiError`) — the standing STATUS.md follow-up; portal's code vocabulary is currently disjoint.

**Net-new wire shapes to define at birth (never portal-local):**
- `StaffMeWire` (staff `/me` is explicitly unratified — NOTE-35), staff `OwnerWire` family (modeled on the `/me` inline builder, `me.ts:35-62`), extended `StaffDogWire` fields.
- `CohortSessionWire`, `{ recipient_count }` reminder response. (§6)
- `StaffMemberWire` (extends the `ThreadParticipantWire` pattern + `StaffRole`), `ShiftWire`, `PayPeriodWire`, `TimeOffRequestWire` (timestamptz punches, structured dates — the mock's `ClockTime`/`range` strings die at the edge). (§13)
- `CreditLotDetailWire` (⚠️ name collision: backend already exports a *different* inline `CreditLotWire` — needs a distinct name or coordinated rename) + `CreditLedgerEntryWire`. (§8)
- Check-in board/event wires and `DashboardSnapshotWire` (gated on the check-in decision). (§3)
- Booking-rules / dog-flags / groups wires (gated). (§12)
- Retail item wire (gated). (§11)
- Typed `ReportWire.*_content` blobs (replace the four `unknown`s — standing STATUS.md follow-up; deletes mobile's local narrowing). (§7)

**Portal shapes to retire by alignment (never mint parallels):** portal `Booking`→`BookingWire`, `BookingRequest`→`PendingRequestWire` (a feature rewrite, not a field remap — DRIFT-10), `Stay`→`BookingWire`+derived status, `Report`→`ReportWire`+`ReportProgram`, `MessageThread`→`ThreadWire`+`MessageWire[]`, `Package`→`CreditPackageWire`, `Announcement`/`SchoolEvent`→`AnnouncementWire`/`EventWire`, `Owner`/`Dog`→the new staff wires, `MessageAttachment`→`MediaWire` family, portal `ApiError` codes→backend snake_case codes, `Campus`/`CampusAbbr`/'fay'/'ben'→`LocationKey` slugs, portal `AttendanceMark`→wire `AttendanceStatus`, portal class/report/credit id vocabularies→backend enums (DRIFT-22/33/13).

Also contract work, not promotion: **request/POST bodies are uncontracted across the board** (wire.ts is response-only) — every reuse/modify above should land its body shape in wire.ts (or a companion) as part of the portal wiring.

## Roll-up 5 — Open questions for Allison <a id="rollup-5"></a>

Decision-level items only; each domain section carries its full list. Ordered by blast radius.

1. **Portal contract-wiring (to-build; canonicity RESOLVED 2026-07-25).** `fetchd_staff_portal_desktop @ scaffold/phase-1` is the only portal — the wire-synced `~/Desktop/fetchd-staff-portal` was deleted (history unrecoverable; its `2a3d9c0` sync is void). Building the portal's `sync:contracts` + generated `contracts.ts` is now the gate for "both clients move together." Staff-identity model (`staff_role` vs `permissions`, DRIFT-43/56) and the divert-approval UI backlog land on this portal; the vocabulary migration stays a live decision, now portal-independent. STATUS.md's alignment table/mirror have been annotated.
2. **Check-in/lodging model** (gates 4 check-in ops + dashboard + 2 schema items): the portal's event-sourced fold (expected→in→out, stepwise undo, departure stamps) vs the backend's single terminal `booking_dogs.attendance` write. BUSINESS-LOGIC records no decision (bookings-scheduling.md OQ #7).
3. **Booking-rules engine** (gates all 4 `rules.*` ops): configurable block/approve/warn rules vs the four **locked, unbypassable** gate triggers + two hardcoded divert reasons. A toggle that softens the vaccine gate would relitigate a locked 2026-05-19 health ruling — which conditions (if any) are staff-authorable?
4. **Staff auth + provisioning:** the portal has zero auth today (hardcoded `ME_STAFF_ID`); backend `authenticate`/`requireStaff` machinery exists, but the staff-invite tooling (Supabase user + `app_role` → `/auth/webhook`) is unverifiable from any repo. Staff `/me` wire is explicitly unratified (NOTE-35) — freeze it before the portal consumes it. Also: does the portal need staff self-edit (`PATCH /me` hard-403s staff)?
5. **Eval-verdict writer (trust inversion):** no staff verb sets `evaluation_status`; it's owner-writable today (BUG-11) and `POST /dogs/:id/evaluation` is documented-but-unimplemented (BUG-10). Who records pass/fail in production, and should the owner-writable path be closed?
6. **Reports lifecycle:** accept the recommended client-held-drafts model (no schema change; send = existing POST; delete = soft-expire) or mandate server-side drafts (breaking-ish: status column + notification suppression)? Also: keep the backend's duplicate-(dog, program, date) tolerance, and does DELETE clear `bookings.session_report_id` back-links?
7. **Requests — undo/bulk:** approve/deny are terminal one-way states; undo-of-deny needs an explicitly-permitted new transition, undo-of-approve is effectively unimplementable, and zero-arg bulk-approve can't supply per-category scheduling. Drop the affordances or redesign them? Should deny reasons persist (+ notify the owner — the portal's "Owner notified" toast is false for deny, DRIFT-9)?
8. **Stays:** is boarding capacity real (BOARD_CAP=15 is mock-only) or uncapped by design? Kennel/belongings columns or fiction? B&T fixed-14-nights a rule or an artifact? May staff cancel a completed stay? What's the money model for mid-stay day-school days (comp vs credit vs PAYG)?
9. **Group-classes direction:** does the portal adopt the backend cohort model (dropping tba/topic/held-week/class-defaults concepts) or does the backend grow them (instructor + schedule columns, nullable start_date)? Also: who writes `dog_completed_classes` (BUG-8 — no writer exists, which starves eligibility)?
10. **Invoice model:** the portal's multi-line numbered invoices + Overdue/Refunded statuses + card|credit|cash invoice-keyed refunds are incompatible with the shipped single-amount, charge-keyed, card-only model (DRIFT-17). Confirm the portal aligns to shipped; which refund methods does v1 actually support (where do credit-back/cash land)? Is invoice campus in scope (no column)?
11. **Retail/shop scope:** entirely absent from the backend (no table/route/wire) — in product scope at all? If yes: sales-tax, inventory semantics, POS/invoice-creation pipeline, role gating all undecided.
12. **Offering config ownership** (the 3 OPEN ops): does non-price reservation-type config (schedule, per-campus offered/capacity, portal visibility, pre-visit email, check-in questions) become backend-owned, or stay client-hardcoded with only price in `service_rates`? Group-class pricing double-homes in `group_classes` — which catalog would an editor write?
13. **Package concept:** portal "packages" span all categories with visibility + a subscription model incompatible with §J.1 (DRIFT-12/20); `credit_packages` covers day-programs only. What are non-day-program packages, and which subscription model wins?
14. **Staff-ops product design** (whole domain greenfield): pay-period boundary + rate source, time-off approval workflow, punch corrections, one-shift-per-day, PTO accrual, and pay-data visibility gating (mock exposes everyone's pay to all staff — DRIFT-57).
15. **Messaging model:** staff read-state schema (single `staff_last_read_at` vs per-participant), thread creation (POST /threads deferred; one-thread-per-(owner, topic) is portal-spec only — DRIFT-41), `owner_id` on `ThreadWire` vs staff projection, channels/opt-outs (no backend model), staff attachments (purpose owner-only today) + pdf kind.
16. **Announcements/events authoring:** backend POST vs ops-seeded content (DRIFT-47); lossy audience model (group/"All clients" don't map to `target_location`); recipient-count persistence; staff RSVP roster read (deliberate soft-empty today, R37); recurring-series authoring convention.
17. **Error contract:** which error-code vocabulary is canonical (backend snake_case vs portal's disjoint set), and do `details` payloads get per-code typing when the envelope moves into wire.ts?
18. **Smaller but real:** owner-directory derived fields (`balance_cents`, contactPref mapping); `day_capacity` overrides have no writer route (SQL-only today); staff agreement-compliance view (owner `GET /agreements` returns `[]` for staff); media-by-report read missing (R26 — portal-uploaded report photos can't reach the owner app); credits staff-branch-vs-dedicated-endpoint choice + manual credit-adjustment verb; cancelled-stays visibility (`findLiveActive` excludes them).

## Method & verification <a id="method"></a>

Three-phase workflow, 45 read-only agents (15 domains × audit → adversarial verify → write), ~12.4k tool-call-seconds, 992 tool calls. Phase 1 diffed demand (`client.ts` + `mock/logic/`) against supply (`routes/` + `wire.ts` + `DATA-CONTRACT.md` + `schema.sql`) per domain. Phase 2 — a separate adversarial verifier per domain — re-opened every citation, re-checked every bucket (A required full coverage of params/fields/auth; C required a broad grep across **all** route files to prove absence), independently re-enumerated every owned route file, cross-checked `BUSINESS-LOGIC/DISCREPANCIES.md` so already-adjudicated bugs/drifts are cited (BUG-n/DRIFT-n/NOTE-n), and flipped anything unconfirmable to INFERRED or an open question. Phase 3 rendered verified entries verbatim.

Bounds on trust: line numbers cite the working tree on 2026-07-24 (backend `07025b3` + uncommitted notifications-WIP docs; portal `6be4c66` + dirty tree) — re-anchor after significant landings. The single INFERRED matrix row (`classes.sendReminder`) has its reasoning in §6. Mock behavior is a demand signal only; where the mock contradicts adjudicated backend rulings, the backend wins and the conflict is cited, not silently resolved.
